8.	kaṭṭaḷikoḷḷā āciriyar eṉṟatu. kaṭṭaḷi yaṭikoḷḷāta āciriyar eṉṟapaṭi. kaṭṭaḷi yaṭiyeṉṟatu - eḻutteṇṇi vakukku maṭiyi. oḻinta iyaṟcīr eṉṟatu - nērnēr allāta iyaṟcīrkaḷi. avi nirinēr mutaliya ēḻu. 
9.	ivi eṉṟatu - neṭṭeḻuttukkaḷi. tammi eṉṟatu. eḻuttākiya tammi eṉṟapaṭi. tammi - tammiyalpi. iṭiniṟṟal - taṉṉi yuṇartti eḻuttātaṟkum poruḷiyuṇarttic collātaṟkum iṭiyāka niṟṟal. eṉavē, eḻuttukaḷ collāyaviṭattun tammi yuṇartti eḻuttātalumuṭiya veṉpatām.
10.	muṟṟummi eṉṟatu - aintum eṉṟatiluḷḷa ummiyi. atu eccappaṭṭu niṉṟateṉṟatu. cila moḻiyām eṉṉum poruḷ payantu niṉṟamiyi. ellārum vantilar eṉpuḻic cilar vantār eṉap poruḷpaṭutalpōla. (col - iṭi - cū - 37).
11.	iṅkē īreḻuttorumoḻi toṭarmoḻi eṉṟu vakuttatu cila pala eṉṉum tamiḻ vaḻakku nōkki yeṉṟu kūṟuvatilum, vaṭamoḻi vaḻakku nōkkiyeṉṟu kūṟutalē poruttamākum. naṉṉūl viruttikārar aṅṅaṉamē kūṟuvar. iṅkē nacciṉārkkiṉiyar ceyyuḷiyalōṭu māṟu paṭā vaṇṇam ōreḻuttu moḻi īreḻuttu moḻi toṭar moḻikaḷi oṟṟeḻuttut taḷḷikkoḷḷa vēṇṭumeṉṟal poruntātu. ēṉeṉil māttiri paṟṟi aci vakuttalāṟ ceyyuḷiyalil oṟṟe ḻuttu mutaliyavaṟṟi āciriyar taḷḷukiṉṟārātalāṉum, īṇṭu eḻuttuppaṟṟi āciriyar ōreḻuttumoḻi mutaliyavaṟṟi vakuttuk kūṟukiṟārātalāṉu meṉpatu. aṉṟiyum puṇarcci kūṟum iyalkaḷiṉ kaṇṇum āciriyar kuṟṟiyalukarap puṇariyalil neṭiṟṟoṭark kuṟṟiyalukaratti īreḻuttorumoḻi (cū - 16) eṉṟum, āytan toṭarntaṉavaṟṟi āytat toṭarmoḻiyeṉṟum, maṟṟum īroṟṟut toṭarmoḻi yeṉṟum, valloṟṟut toṭarmoḻiyeṉṟum kūṟuvati nōkkumpōtu oṟṟiyum kuṟṟiyalukarattiyum kūṭṭi moḻi vakuttalē avar karuttātal naṉku pulappaṭum. aṉṟiyum nacciṉārkkiṉiyarkkum eḻuttu nōkki moḻivakuttuk kōṭalē karuttātal 145ām cūttira uriyil mey mutaliyavaṟṟi īreḻuttu moḻiyēṉṟē kūṟalāṉaṟiyappaṭum. eḻuttāṟ collātalē kūṟaliṉ kol eṉpuḻi lakaramuñ cērntu iraṇṭeḻuttālāya moḻiyeṉṟu kūṟuvatēyaṉṟi, lakarattit taḷḷik kakara okarattāṟ ṟaṉiyēyāṉa teṉṟu kūṟamuṭiyātākalāṉum atu poruntāmi yaṟiyappaṭum. uriyāciriyarkku mituvē karuttāta lavaruriyā ṉuṇarka. aṉṟiyum `akaramutaṉakara viṟuvāy' eṉpuḻi āciriyar oṟṟiṉiyum eḻutteṉṟu karutiṉāreṉiṉ āṇṭu eḻuttiṉ taṉmi kūṟiṟṟeṉṟa nacciṉārk kiṉiyarkku īṇṭu (eḻuttāṉ moḻiyātaṟ kaṇṇum) eḻuttiṉ taṉmi kūṟal uṭaṉpāṭēyātal kāṇka.   
12.	nacciṉārkkiṉiyar iccūttirattiṟku itu uyir meykkaṇ ēṟi uyirmeyyāy niṉṟaviṭattu ammeyyāṟ peyar peṟumāṟu kūṟukiṟateṉṟu karutturittup, piṉ paṉṉīruyirum meyyiṉ ṟaṉmiyilē tammuṭiya taṉmi mayaṅkiṟṟākap peyar kūṟiṟ kuṟṟamilli eṉṟu patavuriyum kūṟi, meyyiṉ taṉmiyāvatu, meyyōṭu kūṭiya uyirum vaṉmi meṉmi iṭimi eṉṟu peyar peṟutaleṉṟu virivuriyuṅ kūṟi, mūviṉattāṟ peyar peṟu māṟṟiṟku utāraṇamuṅ kāṭṭiṉar. āyiṉ iṅṅaṉam valintu māṟṟip poruḷ kōṭalāṟ pōnta payaṉiṉmiyiṉ, uriyāciriyar kūṟiya vāṟu iṭinili mayakkap puṟaṉaṭiyākak koṇṭu, tam vaṭiviṉi yalpic collumiṭattu ellā meyyeḻuttum meymmayakka niliyil mayaṅkal kuṟṟamilliyeṉṟu kōṭalē poruttamām. naṉṉūlārum ivvāṟē `tampeyar.............. iyalumeṉpa' eṉak kūṟutal kāṇka. aṉṟiyum valleḻut tiyiyi ṉavveḻuttu mikumē eṉṟatu. meyyeḻuttu muṉṉum uyir piṉṉumāka olittu niṉṟa muṟipaṟṟi yaṉṟi, uyiriyuṅ kūṭṭiyaṉṟu ātalāṉum nacciṉārkkiṉiyar karuttup poruntāteṉka. 
13.	āṉam eṉṟu pirippatu nalam. āṉam - kuṟṟam. hānam eṉṉum vaṭacoṟṟiripu. 
14.	moḻikku mutalā meḻuttic colvaṉavaṟṟiṟkēyeṉṟatu. moḻikku mutalil niṟkum eḻuttuk kuṟilāyiṉ ataṉ kīḻ varuvaṉa vaṟṟiṟkē īṇṭu ārāycciyeṉṟapaṭi. eṉavē pukar pukaḻ eṉpaṉa vaṟṟil iraṇṭeḻuttukkup piṉ varutaliṉ āṇṭārāycci illi yeṉpatu karuttu. 
15.	itaṟku nacciṉārkkiṉiyar eḻuttukkaḷatu kuṟiyataṉmiyum neṭiyataṉmiyum, māttiri eṉṉum uṟuppiṉic cevikaruviyāka aḷakkiṉṟa aḷavu toḻilālē ceyyuṭkuk koḷḷappaṭutaliṉ. ammāttiri tammuṭṭoṭarntu niṟkiṉṟa coṟkaḷellām, neṭṭeḻutti māttiri mikutaṟkut toṭarnta collām, eṉṟu poruḷ kūṟi neṭṭeḻuttukaḷi māttiri mikutaṟkuk kuṟṟeḻuttukkaḷ avaṟṟōṭu kūṭiniṟkumeṉṟu karuttuk koḷkiṉṟaṉar. itaṟku muṉṉuḷḷa cūttiraṅkaḷ moḻikkaṇ eḻuttukaḷ mayaṅkumāṟu kūṟi atikārap paṭṭu niṟṟalāṉum piṉṉuḷḷa cūttiramum mayakkamē kūṟalāṉum, nacciṉārkkiṉiyar kūṟiyavāṟu ceyyuḷkaḷ tattam icipeṟum poruṭṭuk kuṟṟeḻuttukkaḷ neṭṭeḻuttukkaḷōṭu kūṭi, avaṟṟi ṉōciyi mikuttuniṟkum eṉṟu poruḷpaṭa vantatākak kōṭaliṉum mayakkam paṟṟi vantatōr aiyamaṟukka vantatākak kōṭalē poruttamātaliṉ iccūttirattiṟku uriyāciriyar uriyē poruttamākum. uyireḻuttukkuk kuṟumiyum neṭumiyum aḷaviṟ koḷḷappaṭutaliṉ, toṭar moḻikkīḻ (aḵtāvatu pukar pukaḻ eṉpaṉavaṟṟiṉ kīḻ) niṉṟa rakara ḻakaraṅkaḷellām neṭiṟkīḻ niṉṟa rakara ḻakaraṅkaḷiṉ iyalpi yuṭiyaṉaveṉṟu koḷḷappaṭumeṉpatu uriyāciriyar uri. iṅkē neṭṭeḻuttiṉ iyalpuṭiya eṉṟatu - pukar pukaḻ eṉpaṉa kuṟiliṇik kīḻ niṟpiṉum, kār kāḻ eṉṉum iraṇṭu māttiriyiyuṭiya neṭṭeḻuttukkuk kīḻ niṉṟaṉapōla īṇṭuk koḷḷappaṭu miyalpi. ikkarutti ōrātu pukar pukaḻ eṉpavaṟṟi neṭṭeḻuttu moḻiyāka uriyāciriyar koṇṭāreṉṟu nacciṉārk kiṉiyar maṟuttal poruntāteṉka.
16.	pōlum moḻivayiṉ eṉṟum pāṭam.
17.	ceyyuḷiṟutikkaṇ, pōlum eṉṉuñ colliṉkaṇ eṉṟiruppatu nalam.
18.	peyareccam peyarkoṇṭaṉṟi nillātātaliṉ iṟutic collāy nillātu; muṟṟē iṟutic collāy niṟkum eṉpatu karuttu.
19.	itaṟku nacciṉārkkiṉiyar terintu eṉpati moḻippaṭut ticip piṉum eṉpatōṭum kūṭṭi, oṟṟum kuṟṟiyalukaramum poruṭaru           niliyi yārāyntu moḻippaṭuttuc coṉṉālum, ceyyuḷiyaliṉ māttiri kuṟintu niṟkum nili nōkki eḻutteṉappaṭāveṉṟu vēṟākak kūṟiṉum. avviraṇṭiṭattum eḻuttāntaṉmi tiriyā veṉṟu poruḷ kūṟuvar. īṇṭu meyyeḻutteṉṟum kuṟṟiyalukara meṉṟum āciriyar vitantu kūṟāmiyāṉum, terintu eṉpataṟku iṉṉatit terintu eṉṟu terittuk kūṟāmiyāṉum atu poruḷaṉṟākaliṉ uriyāciriyar karuttē itaṟkup poruttamām. naṉṉūlārum itit taḻuviyē `moḻiyāyt toṭariṉu muṉṉaṉit teḻuttē' eṉṟār. 
20.	kuṟṟukaratti nērpum niripumākak kōṭalāṟ pōlum vēṟicittal kuṟṟukarattiṟkiṉmiyiṉ eṉṟār. ivvākkiyam muṉṉum piṉṉum kūṟiya poruḷkaḷukku muraṇākak kāṇappaṭaliṉ iṭiyil eḻutap paṭṭatu pōlum. 
21.	kuṟṟeḻuttintum neṭṭeḻuttintumeṉavum pāṭam.  
22.	koḷḷaṟka eṉṟatu poruntātu. itu akkālattuk koḷḷappaṭṭu vaḻaṅki vantamiyiṉ. itaṉi naṉṉūl viruttiyurikārar cantiyakkaram meṉṟal poruntātu. itaṉi yām `centami'ḻil veḷippaṭuttiya `pōli eḻuttu' eṉṉum kaṭṭuriyi nōkkit teḷika.
23.	ikara yakaram iṟuti viravi naṭanta moḻivaḻakku akkālat tuṇ ṭeṉpatu itaṉā laṟiyappaṭum.
24.	uyirmey eṉṟatu - uyirōṭu kūṭiya meyyi atu varuñ cūttirattu `uyiroṭuñ cellum' eṉṟataṉālu maṟiyappaṭum.
25.	avi auveṉṉum oṉṟalaṅ kaṭiyē. (pāṭa vēṟupāṭu)
26.	aḷapeṭip piṉvanta kuṟṟeḻuttuṅ koḷvareṉṟatu. `aḷapeṭi mikūu mikara viṟupeyar' eṉṟa cūttirattuḷ aḷapeṭi yeḻuttāka varum ikaratti, ikara viṟupeyareṉṟu āciriyar koṇṭamiyi.
27.	tantu puṇarnturittalāṉ eṉṟatu - uḷporuḷallātataṉi uḷ poruḷpōlat tantu kūṭṭi urittali. iṅkē uyir ṅakarattōṭu kūṭi īṟāvatupōlak kūṟiyatu tantu puṇarttal. iḵtu orutti.
28.	viṉippeyareṉṟatu - toḻiṟpeyari.
29.	aññi eṉṟatu tāyi. akanāṉūṟṟil "eṉ aññi ceṉṟavāṟē" eṉa varutalā ṉuṇarka. īṇṭu makaḷit tāy eṉṟatu uvappup paṟṟi.
30.	viyaṅkōḷ - ēval.
31.	iccūttirattāl ōcipaṟṟiya poruḷ vēṟupaṭumeṉpatu peṟappaṭum. `toṉi' eṉpatu mik karuttu. 
32.	itu moḻiyiṟutippōli kūṟiṟṟu. ṉakarattōṭu makaram ottu naṭavātu. makarattōṭu ṉakaram ottu naṭakkumeṉpatu karuttu. 

piṟappiyal
akara mutal ṉakaraviṟutiyākavuḷḷa mutaleḻuttukkaḷ muppatiyum nūṉmarapilum cārpeḻuttu mūṉṟiyum moḻi marapilum vittuṇarttiya āciriyar. ammuppattu mūṉṟeḻuttukkaḷiṉ piṟappu muṟiyiṉi ivviyalāl uṇarttukiṉṟār. ataṉāl ivviyal piṟappiyaleṉṉum peyarttāyiṟṟu.
untiyilirunteḻukiṉṟa kāṟṟāṉatu tali, miṭaṟu, neñcu eṉṉum mūṉṟaṭaṅkaḷilum nilipeṟṟu avaṟṟuṭaṉ pal, utaṭu, nā, mūkku, aṇṇam (mēlvāy) ākiya uṟuppukkaḷ tammiṟ porunti amitipeṟa vēṟuvēṟuruvākiya eḻuttukkaḷāyp piṟantu pulappaṭa vaḻaṅkutalē eḻuttukkaḷuk kuriya potuvākiya piṟappu muṟiyākum itaṉi ivviyaliṉ mutaṟ cūttirattu āciriyar viritturikkiṉṟār. uyireḻuttukkaḷ paṉṉiraṇṭum tattam nilitiriyātu miṭaṟṟu vaḷiyāṟ piṟakkum. eṉavē uyireḻuttukkaḷiṉ piṟappiṭam miṭaṟu eṉpatu pulaṉām. ōriṭattē oru muyaṟciyāṟ palaveḻuttukkaḷ piṟakkumeṉap potuvakiyāṟ kūṟiṉum avveḻuttukkaḷiṭiyē nuṇṇiya vēṟupāṭukaḷ uḷḷaṉaveṉa āciriyar teḷivupaṭuttukiṉṟār.
ivviyaliṉkaṇ 7-mutal 11-variyuḷḷa cūttiraṅkaḷil kaṅa, caña, ṭaṇa, tana, ākiya meyyeḻuttukkaḷukku neṭuṅ kaṇakku muṟi paṟṟip piṟappuk kūṟukiṉṟār. 12 mutal nāvatikāram paṟṟip piṟappukkūṟat toṭaṅki ṟaṉa, raḻa, laḷa, pama, vaya, eṉṉum meyyeḻuttukkaḷukkup piṟappuk kūṟiyuḷḷār. iṅkup piṟappukkūṟiya meyyeḻuttukkaḷuḷ melleḻuttāṟum tattam piṟappiṉatu ākkañ colliya iṭattē nili peṟṟaṉa vāyiṉum avi mūkkiṉkaṇ uḷatākiya kāṟṟōciyāl iyipu peṟattōṉṟiyolippaṉavām. ivvāṟu melleḻuttāṟukkum mūkkuvaḷiyiṉ toṭarpuṭimi yiṉic collavē iṭiyeḻuttāṟum miṭaṟṟuvaḷiyiyum valleḻuttāṟum talivaḷiyiyum peṟṟu olippaṉaveṉak koṇṭār iḷampūraṇar.
mutaleḻuttukkaḷic cārntutōṟṟiṉallatu taṉit tolikkum iyalpillātaṉa eṉa ārāyntu veḷippaṭuttap paṭṭa cārpeḻuttukkaḷ mūṉṟum tattamakkuc cārpākiya muta leḻuttukkaḷiṉ piṟappiṭattiyē tamakkuriya piṟappiṭamākap porunti icippaṉavām. ivvāṟu eḻuttukkaḷiṉ piṟap puṇarttiya tolkāppiyaṉār avveḻuttukkaḷ peṟum māttiriyiṉik kuṟittukkūṟum viḷakkam ivaṇ karutattakuva tākum. ellāveḻuttukkaḷiyum veḷippaṭac collap paṭṭa iṭattiṉkaṇ eḻuṅkāṟṟiṉālē olikkumiṭattu avveḻuttukkaḷ yāvum koppūḻaṭiyilirunteḻuṅ kāṟṟāṉatu taliyaḷavuñ ceṉṟu mīṇṭum neñciṉkaṇ nilipeṟutalākiya tiritaruṅ kūṟu pāṭṭiṉiyuṭiyaṉa. ivveḻuttukkaḷukku iṅṅaṉam uṟaḻcci vārattiṉāl uḷatām akatteḻuvaḷiyiciyiṉi nuṭpamāka ārāyntu māttiri variyaṟiyāl aḷantu koḷḷutal antaṇaratu maṟinūṟ kaṇṇatākiya muṟiyākum. ammuṟiyiṉi innūliṟ collātu ellārkkum pulaṉākap puṟattē veḷippaṭṭicikkum meyterivaḷiyiciyākiya eḻuttuk kaḷukkē yāṉ iṅku māttiri kūṟiṉēṉ eṉpatu āciriyar kūṟiya viḷakkamākum. untiyil eḻuntakāṟṟu muṉṉart talikkaṭ ceṉṟu piṉṉar miṭaṟṟilēvantu ataṉpiṉṉar neñcilē niṟṟal uṟaḻccivāram eṉappaṭum. mūlātārattilirun teḻuṅ kāṟṟōci akatteḻuvaḷiyici yeṉappaṭṭatu. ellārkkum eḻutturuvam naṉku pulaṉāka vāyiliruntu puṟattē veḷippaṭ ṭicikkum kāṟṟiṉālākiya eḻuttōciyē meyterivaḷi yici yeṉappaṭum. antaṇar maṟiyiṟ kūṟumāṟu akat teḻuvaḷiyiciyākiya eḻuttukkaḷukku aḷapukūṟiṉ aḵtu ellārkkum naṉṟāka viḷaṅkāteṉak karutiya tolkāppiyaṉār puṟatteḻunticikkum meyterivaḷi yiciyākiya eḻuttukkaḷukkē māttiri kūṟuvārāyiṉar.
untuyileḻunta kāṟṟiṉik kūṟupaṭutti māttiri kūṭṭikkoḷḷutalum, mūlātāram mutalākak kāṟṟeḻumāṟu kūṟutalum, antaṇar maṟikku uḷateṉṟu kūṟiya ivvāciriyar, avar matam paṟṟip peṟuvatōr payaṉiṉṟeṉa ic cūttirattāl uyttuṇara vittaliṉ, iccūttiram piṟaṉ kōṭkūṟal eṉṉum uttikkiṉam eṉṟār nacciṉārkkiṉiyar.
- veḷḷivāraṇaṉār 
nūlvarici 10, pak. 168-170

mūṉṟāvatu
piṟappiyal
83.			unti mutalā muntuvaḷi tōṉṟit
					taliyiṉu miṭaṟṟiṉu neñciṉu niliip
					pallu mitaḻu nāvu mūkku
					maṇṇamu muḷappaṭa veṇmuṟi niliyā
					ṉuṟuppuṟ ṟamiya neṟippaṭa nāṭi
					yellā veḻuttuñ colluṅ kālip
					piṟappi ṉākkam vēṟuvē ṟiyala
					tiṟappaṭat teriyuṅ kāṭci yāṉa
eṉpatu cūttiram. ivvōttu - eḻuttukkaḷiṉatu piṟappu uṇarttutaliṟ piṟappiyaleṉṉum peyarttāyiṟṟu. cārpiṟ ṟōṟṟattu eḻuttun taṉimeyyum moḻiyiṉaṉṟi uṇarttalā kāmiyiṉ, avi piṟakkum moḻiyi moḻimarapiṭi uṇarttip piṟappu uṇartta vēṇṭutaliṉ, nūṉmarapiṉ piṉṉar viyātu itaṉi moḻimarapiṉ piṉṉar vittār.
ic cūttiram - eḻuttukkaḷiṉatu potuppiṟavi ittuṇi nilikkaḷattu niṉṟu pulappaṭumeṉkiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: ellā eḻuttum piṟappiṉ ākkañ colluṅ kāli - tamiḻeḻuttu ellāvaṟṟiṟkum āciriyaṉ kūṟiya piṟappiṉatu tōṟṟaravi yāṅ kūṟumiṭattu, unti mutalāt tōṉṟi muntuvaḷi - koppūḻaṭiyākat tōṉṟi muntukiṉṟa utāṉa ṉeṉṉuṅ kāṟṟu, taliyiṉum miṭaṟṟiṉum neñciṉum nilii - taliyiṉkaṇṇum miṭaṟṟiṉkaṇṇum neñciṉ kaṇṇum nili peṟṟu, pallum itaḻum nāvum mūkkum aṇṇamum uḷappaṭa eṇmuṟi niliyāṉ uṟuppuṟṟu - pallum itaḻum nāvum mūkkum aṇṇamumeṉṟa aintuṭaṉē akkāṟṟu, niṉṟa taliyum miṭaṟum neñcuṅ kūṭa eṭṭākiya muṟimiyiyuṭiya taṉmiyoṭu kūṭiya uṟuppukkaḷōṭu oṉṟuṟṟu, amiya - iṅṅaṉam amita lāṉē, vēṟu vēṟu iyala - avveḻuttukkaḷatu tōṟṟaravu vēṟuvēṟu pulappaṭa vaḻaṅkutali yuṭiya, kāṭciyāṉa nāṭi neṟippaṭa - ataṉi aṟivāṉ ārāyntu avaṟṟiṉ vaḻiyilē maṉampaṭa, tiṟappaṭat teriyum - appiṟappu vēṟupāṭukaḷel lāṅ kūṟupaṭa viḷaṅkum eṉṟavāṟu.
1colluṅkāli vaḷi nilipeṟṟu uṟuppukkaḷuṟṟu iṅṅaṉam amitalāṉē avi vaḻaṅkutaliyuṭiya; avaṟṟiṉ vaḻakkam avaṟṟiṉ vaḻiyilē maṉan tiṟappaṭat teriyumeṉak kūṭṭi urittuk koḷka. iṅṅaṉam kūṟavē, 2muyaṟciyum muyaluṅ karuttāvum uṇmi peṟṟām.	(1)
84.		avvaḻip
					paṉṉī ruyirun tannili tiriyā
					miṭaṟṟup piṟanta vaḷiyi ṉicikkum.
iḵtu - uyireḻuttiṟkup potup piṟavi kūṟukiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: paṉṉīruyirun tannili tiriyā - paṉṉiraṇṭu uyirun tattam māttiri tiriyāvāy, avvaḻip piṟanta - avvunti yiṭattup piṟanta, miṭaṟṟu vaḷiyiṉ icikkum - miṭaṟṟiṉkaṇ nilipeṟṟa kāṟṟāṉ olikkum eṉṟavāṟu.
eṉavē, kuṟṟiyalikaramuṅ kuṟṟiyalukaramun tannili tiriyu meṉṟārāyiṟṟu. avveḻuttukkaḷik kūṟi uṇarka.	(2)
85.		avaṟṟuḷ
					aā āyiraṇ ṭaṅkān tiyalum.
iḵtu - avvuyirkaḷuṭ cilavaṟṟiṟkuc ciṟappup piṟavi kūṟukiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: avaṟṟuḷ - muṟkūṟiya paṉṉiraṇṭu uyir kaḷuḷ, a ā āyiraṇṭu - akara ākāraṅkaḷākiya avviraṇṭum, aṅkāntu iyalum - aṅkāntu kūṟum muyaṟciyāṟ piṟakkum  eṉṟavāṟu.
muyaṟci uyirkkiḻavaṉ kaṇṇatu. a ā eṉa ivaṟṟiṉ vēṟupāṭu uṇarka.	(3)
86.			i ī e ē aiyeṉa vicikku
					mappā lintu mavaṟṟō raṉṉa
					vavitā
					maṇpaṉ mutaṉā viḷimpuṟa luṭiya.
ituvum atu.
itaṉ poruḷ: i ī e ē ai eṉa icikkum appālintum - i ī e ē ai eṉṟu kūṟappaṭum akkūṟṟu aintum, avaṟṟōraṉṉa - akara ākāraṅkaḷ pōla aṅkāntu kūṟum muyaṟciyāṟ piṟakkum; avitām aṇpal mutal nā viḷimpu uṟal uṭiya - avitām aṅṅaṉam piṟakkumāyiṉum aṇpallum aṭinā viḷimpum uṟap piṟakkum vēṟupāṭuṭiya eṉṟavāṟu.
aṇpal: viṉittoki. eṉavē, nāviḷimpu aṇuku taṟkuk kāraṇamāṉa palleṉṟu ataṟkōr peyarāyiṟṟu.                 i ī e ē ai eṉa ivaṟṟiṉ vēṟupāṭu uṇarka.	(4)
87.			u ū o ō auveṉa vicikkum	
					mappā lintu mitaḻkuvin tiyalum.
ituvum atu.
itaṉ poruḷ: u ū o ō au eṉa icikkum appā lintum - u ū o ō au eṉṟu collappaṭum akkūṟṟu aintum, itaḻ kuvintu iyalum - itaḻ kuvittuk kūṟap piṟakkum eṉṟavāṟu.
u, ū, o, ō, au eṉa ivaṟṟiṉ vēṟupāṭu uṇarka.	(5)
88.		tattan tiripē ciṟiya veṉpa.
itu, muṟkūṟiya uyirkkum mēṟkūṟum meykkum potu viti kūṟic ciṅkanōkkākak kiṭantatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: tattan tiripē ciṟiya eṉpa - uyirkaḷum meykaḷum ovvoru tāṉaṅkaḷuṭ piṟappaṉavaṟṟik kūṭṭik kūṟiṉēmāyiṉum nuṇṇuṇarvāṉ ārāyumiṭattut tammuṭiya vēṟupāṭukaḷ ciṟiyavāka uṭiya eṉṟu kūṟuvar pulavar eṉṟavāṟu.
avi eṭuttal paṭuttal nalital vilaṅkal eṉṟavāṟṟā ṉun talivaḷi neñcuvaḷi miṭaṟṟuvaḷi mūkkuvaḷi eṉṟavāṟṟā ṉum piṟavāṟṟāṉum vēṟupaṭumāṟu nuṇṇuṇar vuṭiyōr kūṟi uṇarka. ai vilaṅkaluṭiyatu. valliṉan talivaḷiyuṭi yatu. melliṉam - mūkkuvaḷi yuṭiyatu. iṭiyiṉam - miṭaṟṟuvaḷi yuṭiyatu. ēṉiyavuṅ kūṟik kaṇṭu uṇarka.	(6)

89.		kakāra ṅakāra mutaṉā vaṇṇam.
itu, meykaḷuṭ cilavaṟṟiṟkup piṟappuk kūṟukiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: kakāra ṅakāram mutal nā aṇṇam - kakāramum ṅakāramum mutalnāvum mutalaṇṇamum uṟap piṟakkum eṉṟavāṟu.
uyirmeyyākac cūttirattuk kūṟiṉun taṉimeyyākak kūṟik kāṇka. mutali iraṇṭaṟkuṅ kūṭṭuka. ka ṅa eṉa ivaṟṟiṉ vēṟupāṭu uṇarka. 	(7)
90.		cakāra ñakāra miṭinā vaṇṇam.
ituvum atu.
itaṉ poruḷ: cakāra ñakāram iṭinā aṇṇam - cakāramum ñakāramum iṭināvum iṭiyaṇṇamum uṟap piṟakkum eṉṟavāṟu.
iṭiyi iraṇṭaṟkuṅ kūṭṭuka. ca ña eṉa ivaṟṟiṉ vēṟupāṭu uṇarka.	(8)
91.		ṭakāra ṇakāra nuṉinā vaṇṇam.
ituvum atu.
itaṉ poruḷ: ṭakāra ṇakāramum nuṉi nā aṇṇam - ṭakāramum ṇakāramum nuṉināvum nuṉiyaṇṇamum uṟap piṟakkum eṉṟavāṟu.
nuṉiyi iraṇṭaṟkum kūṭṭuka. ṭa ṇa eṉa ivaṟṟiṉ vēṟupāṭu uṇarka.	(9)
92.	avvā ṟeḻuttu mūvakip piṟappiṉa.
itu, mēlaṉavaṟṟiṟkōr aiyam akaṟṟiyatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: avvāṟeḻuttum mūvakip piṟappiṉa - akkūṟappaṭṭa āṟeḻuttum mūvakiyākiya piṟappiṉi uṭiya eṉṟavāṟu.
eṉavē, avi kakāram mutal nāviṉum, ṅakāram mutal aṇṇattiṉum piṟakkumeṉṟu ivvāṟē 3niraṉiṟivakiyāṉ aṟuvakip piṟappiṉa alla eṉṟār.	(10)
93.		aṇṇa naṇṇiya paṉmutaṉ maruṅki
					ṉānuṉi parantu meyyuṟa voṟṟat
					tāmiṉitu piṟakkun takāra nakāram.
itu, meykaḷuṭ cilavaṟṟiṟkup piṟavi kūṟukiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: aṇṇam naṇṇiya palmutal maruṅkil - aṇṇattic cērnta palliṉataṭiyākiya iṭattē, nānuṉi parantu 4meyyuṟa oṟṟa - nāviṉatu nuṉi parantu ceṉṟu taṉ vaṭivu mikavum uṟumpaṭi cēra, takāra nakāran tām iṉitu piṟakkum - takāra nakāram eṉṟavitām iṉitākap piṟakkum eṉṟavāṟu.
ta na eṉa ivaṟṟiṉ vēṟupāṭu uṇarka. muṉṉar uṟuppuṟṟu amiya eṉṟu kūṟi, īṇṭu meyyuṟa oṟṟa eṉṟār, 5ciṟitu oṟṟavum varuṭavum piṟappaṉa uḷavākaliṉ.	(11)
94.		aṇari nuṉinā vaṇṇa moṟṟa
					ṟaḵkā ṉaḵkā ṉāyiraṇṭum piṟakkum.
ituvum atu.
itaṉ poruḷ: nuṉi nā 6aṇari aṇṇam oṟṟa  - nāviṉatu nuṉi mē ṉōkkic ceṉṟu aṇṇatti tīṇṭa, ṟaḵkāṉ ṉaḵkāṉ āyiraṇṭum piṟakkum - ṟakāra ṉakāramākiya avviraṇṭum piṟakkum eṉṟavāṟu.
itu mutalāka neṭuṅkaṇakku muṟiyaṉṟi nāvatikāram paṟṟik kūṟukiṉṟār.
ṟa ṉa eṉa ivaṟṟiṉ vēṟupāṭu uṇarka.	(12)
95.			nuṉinā vaṇari yaṇṇam varuṭa
					rakāra ḻakāra māyiraṇṭum piṟakkum.
ituvum atu.
itaṉ poruḷ: nuṉi nā aṇari aṇṇam varuṭa nāviṉatu nuṉi mēṉōkkic ceṉṟu aṇṇattit taṭava, rakāra ḻakāram āyiraṇṭum piṟakkum - rakāra ḻakāramākiya avviraṇṭum piṟakkum eṉṟavāṟu.
ra ḻa eṉa ivaṟṟiṉ vēṟupāṭu uṇarka.	(13)
96.		nāviḷimpu vīṅki yaṇpaṉ mutaluṟa
				vāvayi ṉaṇṇa moṟṟavum varuṭavum
				lakāra ḷakāra7mā yiraṇṭum piṟakkum.
ituvum atu.
itaṉ poruḷ: nā vīṅki viḷimpu aṇpal mutaluṟa - nā mē ṉōkkic ceṉṟu taṉ viḷimpu aṇpalliṉaṭiyilē uṟā niṟka, āvayiṉ aṇṇam oṟṟa lakāramāy - avviṭattu av vaṇṇatti annāt tīṇṭa lakāramāyum, āvayiṉ aṇṇam varuṭa ḷakāramāy - avviṭattu avvaṇṇatti an nāt taṭava ḷakāramāyum, iraṇṭum piṟakkum - ivviraṇṭe ḻuttum piṟakkum eṉṟavāṟu.
la ḷa eṉa ivaṟṟiṉ vēṟupāṭu uṇarka.
8ittuṇiyum nāvatikāram kūṟiṟṟu.	(14)
97.	itaḻiyintu piṟakkum pakāra makāram.
ituvum atu.
itaṉ poruḷ: itaḻ iyintu piṟakkum pakāra makāram - mēlitaḻuṅ kīḻitaḻuṅ tammiṟ kūṭap pakāramum makāramum piṟakkum eṉṟavāṟu.
pa ma eṉa ivaṟṟiṉ vēṟupāṭu uṇarka.	(15)
98.	pallita ḻiyiya vakāram piṟakkum.
itu, vakāram piṟakkumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: pal itaḻ iyiya vakāram piṟakkum - mēṟpalluṅ kīḻitaḻuṅ kūṭa vakāramāṉatu piṟakkum eṉṟavāṟu.
va eṉa varum. itaṟkum itaḻ iyitaliṉ makarattiṉ piṉṉar vittār.	(16)
99.		aṇṇañ cērnta miṭaṟṟeḻu vaḷiyici
					kaṇṇuṟ ṟaṭiya yakāram piṟakkum.
itu, yakāram piṟakkumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: eḻuvaḷi miṭaṟṟuc cērnta ici - untiyileḻunta kāṟṟu miṭaṟṟiṭattuc cērnta ataṉāṟ piṟanta ōci, aṇṇaṅ kaṇṇuṟṟu aṭiya - aṇṇatti aṇintu uralāṇi iṭṭāṟpōlac ceṟiya, yakāram piṟakkum - yakāra voṟṟup piṟakkum eṉṟavāṟu.
9āṇi - maram. ya  eṉa varum.	(17)
100.	melleḻut tāṟum piṟappi ṉākkañ
					colliya paḷḷi niliyiṉa vāyiṉu
					mūkkiṉ vaḷiyici yāppuṟat tōṉṟum.
itu, melleḻuttiṟkuc ciṟappuviti kūṟukiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: melleḻuttu āṟum piṟappiṉ ākkañ colliya 10paḷḷi niliyiṉa āyiṉum - melleḻuttukkaḷ āṟun tattam piṟappiṉatu ākkañ colliya iṭattē nilipeṟṟaṉa vāyiṉum, mūkkiṉ vaḷiyici yāppuṟat tōṉṟum - ōci kūṟuṅkāl mūkkiṉkaṇ uḷatākiya vaḷiyiṉ iciyāṉ yāppuṟat tōṉṟum eṉṟavāṟu.
avi aṅṅaṉamātal kūṟikkāṇka.
yāppuṟa eṉṟataṉāṉ iṭiyiṉattiṟku miṭaṟṟuvaḷiyum valliṉattiṟkut talivaḷiyuṅ koḷka.	(18)
101.		cārntuvari ṉallatu tamakkiyal pilaveṉat
					tērntu veḷip paṭutta vēṉi mūṉṟun
					tattañ cārpiṟ piṟappoṭu civaṇi
					yotta kāṭciyiṟ ṟammiyal piyalum.
itu, cārpiṟōṟṟaṅkaḷ piṟakkumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: cārntuvariṉ allatu - cila eḻuttukkaḷic cārntu tōṉṟiṉallatu, tamakku iyalpu ila eṉa - tamakkeṉat tōṉṟutaṟku ōriyalpilaveṉṟu, tērntu veḷippaṭutta tam miyalpu mūṉṟum - ārāyntu veḷippaṭuttap paṭṭa eḻuttukkaḷ tammuṭiya piṟappiyalpu mūṉṟaṉiyuṅ kūṟuṅkāl, tattam cārpiṟ piṟappoṭu civaṇi iyalum - tattamakku uriya cārpākiya meykaḷatu ciṟappup piṟappiṭattē piṟattalōṭu porunti naṭakkum; ēṉi otta kāṭciyiṉ iyalum - oḻinta āytan taṉakkup poruntiṉa neñcuvaḷiyāṟ piṟakkum eṉṟavāṟu.
kāṭciyeṉṟatu neñciṉi. kēṇmiyā  nāku  nunti eṉavum, eḵku eṉavum varum.
āytattiṟkuc cārpiṭaṅ kuṟiyataṉ muṉṉar (eḻu. 38) eṉpataṉāṟ kūṟiṉār. iṉi āytan talivaḷiyāṉum miṭaṟṟu vaḷiyāṉum piṟakkumeṉpārum uḷar. moḻinta poruḷō ṭoṉṟa avvayiṉ moḻiyātataṉi muṭṭiṉṟu muṭittaleṉpataṉāṉ, aḷapeṭiyum uyirmeyyun tammi ākkiya eḻuttukkaḷatu piṟappiṭamē iṭamāka varumeṉṟu uṇarka.		(19)
102.		ellā veḻuttum veḷippaṭak kiḷantu
					colliya paḷḷi yeḻutaru vaḷiyiṟ
					piṟappoṭu viṭuvaḻi yuṟaḻcci vārat
					takatteḻu vaḷiyici yariṟapa nāṭi
					yaḷapiṟ kōṭa lantaṇar maṟittē
					yaḵtiva ṇuvalā teḻuntupuṟat ticikku
					meyteri vaḷiyici yaḷavunuvaṉ ṟiciṉē.
iḵtu - eḻuttukkaṭam piṟappiṟkup puṟaṉaṭi kūṟukiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: ellāveḻuttuṅ kiḷantu veḷippaṭa - āciriyaṉ ellāveḻuttukkaḷum piṟakkumāṟu muntunūṟ kaṇṇē kūṟi veḷippaṭukkiyi ṉālē, colliya paḷḷi piṟappoṭu viṭuvaḻi - yāṉum avvāṟē kūṟiya eṇvaki nilattum piṟakkiṉṟa piṟappōṭē avveḻuttukkaḷik kūṟumiṭattu, eḻutaru vaḷiyiṉ uṟaḻccivārattiṉ aḷapu kōṭal - yāṉ kūṟiyavāṟu aṉṟi untiyil tōṉṟuṅ kāṟṟiṉatu tiritaruṅkūṟṟiṉkaṇṇē māttiri kūṟikkōṭalum, akattu eḻu vaḷiyici ariltapa nāṭik kōṭal - mūlātārat til eḻukiṉṟa kāṟṟiṉōciyik kuṟṟamaṟa nāṭikkōṭalum, antaṇar maṟittē - pārppāratu vētattu uḷavē; annilimi āṇṭu uṇarka; aḵtu ivaṇ nuvalātu - aṅṅaṉam kōṭali īṇṭuk kūṟalākāmiyiṉ in nūṟkaṭ kūṟātē, eḻuntu puṟattu icikkum - untiyiṟ ṟōṉṟip puṟattē 11pulappaṭṭu olikkum, meyteri vaḷiyici aḷapu nuvaṉṟiciṉē - poruṭeriyuṅ kāṟṟiṉatu tuṇiviṟkē yāṉ māttiri kūṟiṉēṉ; avaṟṟiṉatu māttiriyi uṇarka eṉṟavāṟu.
itaṉi iraṇṭu cūttiramākkiyum urippa.
itu piṟaṉkōṭ kūṟaleṉṉum uttikku iṉam, eṉṉi? untiyil eḻunta kāṟṟiṉik kūṟupaṭutti māttiri kūṭṭik kōṭalum, mūlatāram mutalākak kāṟṟeḻumāṟu kūṟalum, vētattiṟku uḷateṉṟu ivvāciriyar kūṟi ammatam paṟṟi avar koḷvatōr payaṉ iṉṟeṉṟaliṉ. untiyil eḻunta kāṟṟu muṉṉart talikkaṭ ceṉṟu, piṉṉar miṭaṟṟilē vantu, piṉṉar neñcilē niṟṟali uṟaḻccivārattu eṉṟār. akat teḻuvaḷi yeṉavē mūlātāra meṉpatu peṟṟām.
iṉcāriyiyi attuccāriyiyōṭu kūṭṭuka. ēkāran tēṟṟam.
meyterivaḷiyeṉavē, 12poru ṭeriyā muṟkum vīḷiyum muyaṟciyāṉāmeṉiṉum poru ṭeriyāmiyiṉ avi kaṭiyap paṭṭaṉa. eṉavē, collap piṟantu coṟku uṟuppām ōciyi ivar eḻutteṉṟu vēṇṭuvareṉa uṇarka.
13niliyum vaḷiyum muyaṟciyu mūṉṟu
	miyala naṭappa teḻutteṉap paṭumē
eṉṟārākaliṉ.	(20)
piṟappiyal muṟṟiṟṟu.
kaṇēcayyar aṭikkuṟippukaḷ:
1.	colluṅkāli eṉpati eḻuttukkaḷik kūṟupavaṉuṭiya viṉi yākki uriyāciriyar kūṟuvar. atuvē ciṟanta uriyātal kāṇka.
2.	vaḷitōṉṟi nilipeṟṟu uṟuppuṟṟu amiya eṉṟataṉāṉē             aṅṅaṉam amitaṟku uyirkkiḻavaṉatu muyaṟciyum avaṉum vēṇṭu meṉpatu peṟappaṭum. uyirkkiḻavaṉ - karuttā.
3.	niraṉiṟi vakiyā ṉaṟuvakip piṟappiṉa alla eṉṟatu. `cakara ñakāram iṭinā vaṇṇam' eṉpuḻic cakaramiṭināvilum ñakaramiṭi yaṇṇattilum eṉṟu niraṉiṟiyākak koḷḷa aṟuvakip piṟappiṉa vām. aṅṅaṉam alla eṉṟapaṭi.
4.	meyyuṟa oṟṟa eṉṟatu - aḻunta oṟṟa eṉṟapaṭi.
5.	ciṟitu oṟṟap piṟappaṉa - ṟakaram ṉakaram lakaram eṉpaṉa. ciṟitu varuṭap piṟappaṉa - ḷakāra rakāra ḻakāraṅkaḷ.
6.	aṇarutal - mēṉōkkic cēṟal. aṇari - mēṉōkkic ceṉṟu.
7.	avviraṇṭum eṉa muṉpōṟ kūṟalē poruttam.
8.	ittuṇiyum nāvatikāram kūṟiṟṟeṉṟatu. 8-m cūttirattil nāviṟ piṟakkum eḻutti atikārappaṭa vittu atu mutalāka nāviṟ piṟakkum eḻuttukkaḷiyē kūṟivantamiyi. atikāram - talimi. muṟimi eṉpar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar.
9.	āṇi eṉṟatu - uraliṉ aṭittuvāratti maṟikkumpaṭi iṭum maratti.
10.	paḷḷi - iṭam.
11.	pulappaṭal - cevikkup pulappaṭal.
12.	poruḷ teriyā eṉpataṟku eḻuttākiya poruḷ teriyāta eṉpatu poruḷ. iṉṟēl, muṟkum vīḷiyum poruḷuṇarttumēṉum eṉṟu ivar muṟkūṟiyatōṭu māṟupaṭum.
13.	nili eṉṟatu - iṭatti. vaḷi eṉṟatu - kāṟṟi. muyaṟci eṉṟatu - muyaṟcip piṟappi. 

puṇariyal
moḻikaḷ tammiṟ puṇartaṟkuriya karuviyiṉ iyalpiṉik kūṟutaliṉ itu puṇariyal eṉṉum peyarttāyiṟṟu. ivviyaliṟ kūṟappaṭum vitikaḷ piṉvarum iyalkaḷiṟ kūṟappaṭum ceykipaṟṟiya vitikaḷukkup payaṉpaṭutaliṉ karuvikaḷeṉap paṭṭaṉa.
tamiḻ neṭuṅkaṇakkiluḷḷa muppattu mūṉṟeḻuttukkaḷuḷ irupattiraṇṭeḻuttukkaḷ moḻikku mutalāmeṉavum, irupattu nāṉkeḻuttukkaḷ moḻikku īṟāmeṉavum, ellā moḻikaḷukkum iṟutiyum mutalumāvaṉa mey, uyir eṉṉum ivviruvaki yeḻuttukkaḷeṉavum, moḻiyiṟutiyil niṉṟa meyyellām puḷḷipeṟṟu niṟkumeṉavum, moḻiyiṟutiyi luḷḷa kuṟṟiyalukaramum meyyiṉ taṉmiyiyuṭiyatā meṉavum, moḻiyīṟāy niṉṟa uyirmey uyirīṟṟiṉ taṉmiyiyuṭiyatā meṉavum ivviyalil 1-mutal 4-variyuḷḷa cūttiraṅkaḷ kūṟukiṉṟaṉa.
nilimoḻiyi niṟuttacol eṉṟum varumoḻiyik kuṟittuvarukiḷaviyeṉṟum tolkāppiyaṉār vaḻaṅkuvar. niṟutta colliṉ īṟṟeḻuttōṭu kuṟittuvarukiḷaviyiṉ mutalaleḻuttup porunta avvirumoḻikaḷum iyintu varutalē puṇarcciyeṉappaṭum. appuṇarcci uyirīṟṟuc col muṉ uyir varumiṭam, uyirīṟṟuc colmuṉ meyvarumiṭam, meyyīṟṟuc colmuṉ uyir varumiṭam, meyyīṟṟuc colmuṉ mey varumiṭam eṉa eḻuttuvakiyāl nāṉku vakippaṭum; peyaroṭu peyar, peyaroṭu toḻil, toḻiloṭu peyar, toḻiloṭu toḻil eṉac col vakiyāl nāṉkākum. moḻikaḷ puṇaruṅkāl iṭiniṉṟa eḻuttukkaḷ oṉṟu maṟṟeṉṟākat tiritali meypiṟitātal eṉṟum, avviṭattup putiyaveḻuttut toṉṟutali mikutal eṉṟum, aṅku muṉṉirunta eḻuttuk keṭutalik kuṉṟal eṉṟum, ivvēṟu pāṭetuvumiṉṟi avvirumoḻikaḷum muṉṉuḷḷavāṟu puṇarntu niṟṟali iyalpeṉṟum, innāṉkiṉiyum moḻipuṇariyalpu eṉṟum āciriyar kuṟiyiṭṭu vaḻaṅkiyuḷḷār. niṟuttacollum kuṟittu varukiḷaviyum taṉittaṉiyē aṭimoḻi peṟṟuvariṉum nilimoḻi varumoḻiyākap puṇartaṟku uriyaṉavām. muṉ piṉṉāka māṟi niṉṟa marūu moḻikaḷum nilimoḻi varumoḻiyākap puṇarkkappaṭutaluṇṭu.
moḻippuṇarcci vēṟṟumip puṇarcciyeṉavum alvaḻip puṇarcciyeṉavum poruḷ vakiyāl irutiṟappaṭum ivaṟṟuḷ vēṟṟumip puṇarcciyi vēṟṟumi kuṟitta puṇarmoḻi niliyeṉṟum alvaḻip puṇarcciyi vēṟṟumiyalvaḻip puṇarmoḻi niliyeṉṟu āciriyar vaḻaṅkiyuḷḷār. ivviruvakip puṇarmoḻikaḷum eḻuttu mikutalum cāriyi mikutalum ākiya irutiṟattālum naṭappaṉa. vēṟṟumi yurupāvaṉa ai, oṭu, ku, iṉ, atu, kaṇ eṉṉum āṟām eṉat tolkāppiyaṉarkku muṟpaṭṭa tamiḻiya ṉūlār vakippaṭuttiyuḷḷārkaḷ. vēṟṟumiyurupukaḷ puṇarum nilimikkaṇ peyariṉ piṉṉiṭattē niṟṟaṟkuriyaṉa. urupēṟkum peyarkaḷ uyartiṇip peyar, aḵṟiṇip peyar eṉa iruvakippaṭum. avi vēṟṟumiyurupōṭu poruntuṅkāl peyarkkum vēṟṟumiyurupiṟkumiṭiyē cāriyi mikum. cāriyikaḷāvaṉa iṉ, vaṟṟu, attu, am, oṉ, āṉ, akku, ikku, aṉ eṉavarum ivvoṉpatum ivipōlvaṉa piṟavum ām. iṉ cāriyi ikaraṅkeṭṭu ṉakaramey maṭṭum niṟṟalum, ṉakaraṅ keṭṭu ikaravuyir maṭṭum niṟṟalum, ṉakaram ṟakaramākat tirintu niṟṟalum ākiya mūṉṟu tiripuṭiyateṉṟum, vaṟṟuc cāriyi mutaṟkaṇuḷḷa vakaramey keṭa aṟṟu eṉattiriyumeṉṟum, iṉ, oṉ, āṉ, aṉ eṉṉum iccāriyikaḷiṉ ṉakaram ṟakaramāyt tiritaluṇṭeṉṟum, akaravīṟṟuc colmuṉ varum attuccāriyi akaraṅkeṭa ttu eṉa niṟkumeṉṟum, ikara aikāra vīṟṟuc colmuṉvarum ikkuc cāriyi ikaraṅkeṭa kku eṉa niṟkumeṉṟum, valleḻuttu mutaṉmoḻi varuṅkāl akkuc cāriyi iṟutik kuṟṟiyalukaramum ataṉulūrappaṭṭa kakarameyyum akkakara meyyiṉmēl niṉṟa kakarameyyum orucērakkeṭa a eṉat tirintu niṟkumeṉṟum, kacata varu moḻiyāy varuṅkālattu am cāriyiyiṉ makaram muṟiyē ṅa ña na eṉat tiritalum, melleḻuttum iṭiyeḻuttum varumoḻiyāy varuṅ kālattuk keṭutalumākiya nilimit teṉṟum, coṟkaḷ peyarun toḻilumāyp pirintum peyarum peyarumāyk kūṭiyum icippa vēṟṟumiyurupu virintu niṟku miṭattum maṟintu niṟkumiṭattum moḻikaḷip pirittuk kāṇuṅkāl peyarkkum vēṟṟumiyurupiṟku miṭiyē vantu niṟṟal cāriyiyiṉ iyalpeṉṟum cāriyiyiṉ ilakkaṇat tiṉi āciriyar teḷivāka variyaṟuttuk kāṭṭiyuḷḷar.
coṟkaḷi nilimoḻi varumoḻiyākap pirittuk kāṇum muṟiyiṉit coṟcitar maruṅku eṉavum peyar cāriyi urupu mutaliya colluṟuppukkaḷ oṉṟaṉpiṉṉoṉṟāka oṭṭi naṭattaṟkuriya moḻivaḻakkiṉi oṭṭutaṟkoḻukiya vaḻakku eṉavum āciriyar kuṟiyiṭṭu vaḻaṅkiyuḷḷār. niṟutta colluṅ kuṟittu varukiḷaviyumākap pirittaṟkēṟṟavāṟu oṭṭi nillātu, nilākkatir nilāmuṟṟam eṉṟāṟ pōla uṭaṅkiyintu niṟkum puṇarmoḻikaḷ oṭṭutaṟkoḻukiya vaḻakkiṉa allaveṉavuṅ ākavē avi cāriyi peṟā veṉavum iḷampūraṇar kūṟiya viḷakkam ivaṇ cintittuṇarat takuvatām.
kāram, karam, kāṉ eṉpaṉa eḻuttiṉ cāriyikaḷām. avaṟṟuḷ karam, kāṉ eṉṉum iru cāriyikaḷiyum neṭṭeḻuttup peṟutalilli. kuṟṟeḻuttu mēṟkūṟiya mūṉṟu cāriyikaḷiyum peṟum. neṭṭeḻuttukkaḷil ai, au eṉṉumiraṇṭum kāṉ cāriyiyum peṟum. eṉa ivviyalil iyipuṭimi karuti eḻuttuc cāriyikaḷum uṭaṉ kūṟappaṭṭaṉa.
meyyīṟṟiṉmuṉ uyirmutaṉ moḻi varuṅkāl varumoḻi mutaliluḷḷa uyir taṉittu nillātu; meykaḷukkuriya puḷḷi peṟutalākiya avviyalpiṉik keṭuttu nilimoḻi yīṟṟiluḷḷa ammeyyuṭaṉ kūṭi niṟkum. iṅṅaṉam kūṭiya uyir pirintu nīṅkiyavaḻi nilimoḻiyīṟṟiluḷḷa mey mīṇṭum taṉ paḻiya vaṭivākiya puḷḷiyip peṟum. ivviyalpiṉi ivviyaliṉ 36, 37-ām cūttiraṅkaḷāl  āciriyar kuṟippiṭuvar. kuṟṟiyalukaramum meyyīṟupōlun taṉmiyattu eṉa ivviyaliṉ mūṉṟām cūttirattāl āciriyar māṭṭēṟṟik kūṟiyuḷḷār. immāṭṭēṟu puḷḷi peṟutalum uyirēṟa iṭaṅkoṭuttalumākiya meyyiṉ taṉmikaḷuḷ puḷḷi peṟutali vilakki uyirēṟa iṭaṅkoṭuttalākiya avvaḷavukkuc cellutaliṉ immāṭṭēṟu orupuṭiccēṟa leṉavuṇarka eṉa uriyāciriyar viḷakkaṅ kūṟuvar. kuṟṟiyalukaramum aṟṟeṉa moḻipa eṉa muṉṉar māṭṭēṟu kūṟiṉamiyāl meyyīṟṟiṉmuṉ uyir taṉittu nillātavāṟu pōṉṟu kuṟṟiyalukaravīṟṟiṉ muṉṉum uyir taṉittu nillātu akkuṟṟiyalukarattōṭu porunti niṟkum eṉat tol kāppiyaṉār uyttuṇara vittami peṟappaṭum.
uyirīṟṟiṉmuṉ uyirmutaṉmoḻi varumiṭattu uyirōṭu uyirkkuk kalantu niṟkum iyalpiṉmiyāl iru moḻikaḷum oṭṭi nillātu viṭṭicippaṉavām. nilimoḻiyīṟṟilum varu moḻi mutalilum niṟkum avviraṇṭuyirkaḷum uṭampaṭutaṟ poruṭṭu avaṟṟiṭiyē yakara vakaraṅkaḷuḷ oṉṟu uṭampaṭu meyyāy varum. uṭampaṭāta iraṇṭuyirkaḷum uṭampaṭutaṟ poruṭṭu iṭiyē tōṉṟum mey uṭampaṭumey eṉa vaḻaṅ kappaṭum. uṭaṉpāṭu, uṭampāṭu eṉa maruvi vaḻaṅkiyatu varumuyirkku uṭampāka aṭukkum mey uṭampaṭumey eṉapporuḷ kōṭalumuṇṭu. ellā moḻikkum iṟutiyum mutalum uyir varumvaḻi avaṟṟiṭiyē uṭampaṭumey peṟutali vilakkār eṉat tolkāppiyaṉār kūṟutalāl avar kālattu uyirīṟṟiṉ muṉ uyirmutaṉmoḻi vantu puṇaruṅkāl uṭampaṭumey peṟṟē varavēṇṭumeṉṉum variyaṟiyilli yeṉpatu naṉku pulaṉākum. uṭampaṭumeyyāka varutaṟkuriya eḻuttukkaḷ iviyeṉat tolkāppiyaṉār kūṟātu pōyiṉum avariyaṟṟiya iyaṟṟamiḻ nūlākiya it tolkāppiyattilum itaṟkuppiṉ tōṉṟiya tamiḻ nūlka ḷellā vaṟṟilum yakara vakaraṅkaḷē uṭampaṭumeyyāka āḷappeṟṟuḷḷami kāṇalām. uṭampaṭu meyyē yakāra vakāram, uyir mutaṉ moḻi varūuṅkāliyāṉa eṉa nacciṉārkkiṉiyar kāṭṭiya paḻañcūttiram yakara vakaraṅkaḷē uṭampaṭumeyyāy varutaṟkuriyaṉa eṉpatit teḷivāka viḷakkutal kāṇka. uyirkaḷuḷ ikara īkāra aikāra vīṟukaḷ yakara vuṭampaṭumey koḷḷum eṉavum, ēkāravīṟu yakara vakaraṅkaḷuḷ oṉṟi uṭampaṭumeyyākap peṟṟuvarum eṉavum, ēṉiya vuyirīṟukaḷ yāvum vakara vuṭampaṭu meyyē peṟum eṉavum iḷampūraṇarum nacciṉārkkiṉiyarum vakipeṟa viḷakkiyuḷḷārkaḷ.
eḻuttāl oṉṟupōlat tōṉṟic collāl vēṟu paṭṭup poruḷ viḷaṅki niṟkum puṇarmoḻikaḷ ōci vēṟu pāṭṭaṟ pirintu puṇarcci vēṟupaṭutal colnaṭiyiṉ nili peṟṟa paṇpākum. ivvāṟu varum puṇarmoḻikaḷ kuṟippiṉāluṇarum poruḷiyuṭiyaṉa. eḻuttu vakiyāl ittaṉmiya eṉat teḷivāka vuṇarum muṟimiyi yuṭiyaṉa alla. eṭuttukkāṭṭākac cempoṉ patiṉṟoṭi eṉṉum puṇar moḻi poṉṉippaṟṟip pēcciṉkaṇ eṭuttāḷap paṭṭāl cempoṉ + patiṉṟoṭi eṉap pirintum, cempik kuṟittu nikaḻum pēccil eṭuttāḷappaṭṭāl cempu + oṉpatiṉṟoṭi eṉap pirintum ōci vēṟupaṭṭu vēṟuvēṟu poruḷuṇartti niṟṟal kāṇalām. ivaṟṟiṉ iyalpiṉi ivviyaliṉ īṟṟiluḷḷa iraṇṭu cūttiraṅkaḷālum āciriyar aṟivuṟuttukiṉṟār. eṉavē avar kālattu iṅṅaṉam nuṇṇitākap poruḷuṇarttum puṇarmoḻikaḷ peruka vaḻaṅkiṉami naṉku pulaṉākum.
- ka. veḷḷivāraṇaṉār 
nūlvarici 10, pak. 170-174

nāṉkāvatu
puṇariyal
103.		mūṉṟutali yiṭṭa muppatiṟ ṟeḻutti
					ṉiraṇṭutali yiṭṭa mutalā kirupaḵ
					taṟunāṉ kīṟoṭu neṟiniṉ ṟiyalu
					mellā moḻikku miṟutiyu mutalu
					meyyē yuyireṉ ṟāyī riyala
eṉpatu cūttiram. moḻimarapiṟ kūṟiya moḻikaḷip potu vakiyāṟ puṇarkkum muṟimi uṇarttiṉamiyiṟ puṇariyal eṉpatu ivvōttiṟkup peyarāyiṟṟu. īṇṭu muṟimi yeṉṟatu mēṟ 1ceyki yōttukkaḷuṭ puṇartaṟku uriyavāka īṇṭuk kūṟiya karuvikaḷi.
iccūttiram eṉṉutaliṟṟō veṉiṉ, moḻimarapiṟ kūṟiya moḻikku mutalā meḻuttum moḻikku īṟāmeḻuttum ittuṇi yeṉṟalum, ellā moḻikkum īṟum mutalum, meyyum uyirumallatu illiyeṉṟu variyaṟuttalum, 2īṟum mutalu māka eḻuttu nāṟpattāṟu uḷavō eṉṟu aiyuṟṟārkku, eḻuttu muppattu mūṉṟumē aṅṅaṉam īṟum mutalumāy niṟpateṉṟu aiyamaṟuttalum nutaliṟṟu.
itaṉ poruḷ:  mutal iraṇṭu taliyiṭṭa irupaḵtu īṟu aṟu nāṉkākum mūṉṟu taliyiṭṭa muppatiṟṟeḻutti ṉoṭu - moḻikku mutalā meḻuttu, iraṇṭi muṭiyilē yiṭṭa irupaḵtum, moḻikku īṟāmeḻuttu, irupattu nāṉkumākiṉṟa mūṉṟi muṭiyilēyiṭṭa muppatākiya eḻuttukkaḷōṭē, neṟiniṉṟu iyalum ellā moḻikkum - vaḻakkuneṟikkaṇiṉṟu naṭakkum 3mūvaki moḻikkum, meyyē uyireṉṟu āyīriyala iṟutiyum mutalum - meyyum uyirumeṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭa avviraṇṭu iyalpiṉiyuṭiya eḻuttukkaḷē īṟum mutalum āvaṉa eṉṟavāṟu.
irupattiraṇṭu mutalāvaṉa paṉṉīruyirum oṉpatu uyir meyyum 4moḻimutaṟ kuṟṟiyalukaramumām. irupattunāṉku īṟāvaṉa paṉṉīruyirum patiṉoru puḷḷiyum īṟṟuk kuṟṟiyalukara mumām. meyyi muṟkūṟiṉār nālvakip puṇarcciyum meykkaṇ nikaḻumāṟu uyirkkaṇ nikaḻā veṉṟaṟku.
utāraṇam:	maram eṉa meymutalum meyyīṟum, ili eṉa uyirmutalum uyirīṟum, āl eṉa uyirmutalum mey yīṟum, viḷa eṉa meymutalum uyirīṟumām. moḻi yākkam, iyalpum vikāramumeṉa iraṇṭām. uyir tāmē niṉṟu mutalum īṟumātal iyalpu. avi meyyōṭu kūṭi niṉṟu aṅṅaṉamātal vikāram.	(1)
104.		avaṟṟuḷ
					5meyyī ṟellām puḷḷiyoṭu niliyal.
itu, muṟkūṟiyavāṟṟāl taṉimey mutalāvāṉ ceṉṟa taṉi vilakkaliṉ eytiyatu vilakkiṟṟu.
itaṉ poruḷ: avaṟṟuḷ - muṟkūṟiya meyyum uyirumeṉṟa iraṇ ṭaṉuḷ, meyyīṟu ellām puḷḷiyoṭu niliyal - mey moḻikku īṟāyavi yellām puḷḷi peṟṟu niṟkum eṉṟavāṟu.
eṉavē, moḻikku mutalāyiṉavi yellām puḷḷi yiḻantu uyirēṟi niṟkumeṉṟārāyiṟṟu. iṉṉum īṟṟumey puḷḷi peṟṟu niṟku meṉṟataṉāṉē, uyirmutaṉ moḻi tammēl vantāl avi uyirēṟa iṭaṅkoṭuttu niṟku meṉpatūuṅ kūṟiṉārāyiṟṟu. ivviti muṟkūṟiyataṉṟō veṉiṉ, āṇṭut taṉi mey patiṉeṭṭum puḷḷi peṟṟu niṟkumeṉṟum, avitām uyirē ṟuṅkāṟ puḷḷiyiḻantu niṟkumeṉṟuṅ kūṟiṉār; īṇṭu mey mutal meyyīṟeṉap poruḷurikka vēṇṭiṉamiyiṉ moḻimutaṉ meykaḷum puḷḷi peṟumō veṉṟu aiyuṟṟa aiyam akaṟṟak kūṟiṉāreṉṟu uṇarka. maram eṉa puḷḷi peṟṟu niṉṟatu, ariteṉa vantuḻi, maramariteṉṟu ēṟi muṭintavāṟu kāṇka.	(2)
105.		6kuṟṟiya lukaramu maṟṟeṉa moḻipa.
itu, muṉṉarp puḷḷi yīṟṟumuṉ uyirtaṉit tiyalā teṉṟu meykku eytuvikkiṉṟa karuviyi etiratu pōṟṟi, uyirkkum eytuvikkiṉṟa karuvic cūttiram.
itaṉ poruḷ: kuṟṟiyalukaramum aṟṟeṉa moḻipa - īṟṟuk kuṟṟiyalukaramum puḷḷiyīṟupōla uyirēṟa iṭaṅ koṭukku meṉṟu kūṟuvar pulavar eṉṟavāṟu.
immāṭṭēṟu orupuṭic cēṟal, puḷḷi peṟāmiyiṉ. aṅṅaṉam uyirēṟuṅkāṟ kuṟṟukaram keṭṭuppōka niṉṟa oṟṟiṉmēl uyirēṟiṟṟeṉṟu koḷḷaṟka. nākariteṉpuḻi muṉṉark kuṟṟukara vōciyum piṉṉar uyirōciyum peṟṟu avviraṇṭuṅ kūṭi niṉṟallatu apporu ḷuṇarttalākā miyiṉ, iḵtu uyirōṭuṅ kūṭi niṟkumeṉṟār.(3)
106.		uyirmey yīṟu 7muyirīṟ ṟiyaṟṟē.
itu, meyyē yuyireṉ ṟāyī riyala (eḻut. 103) eṉṟa uyirk kaṇ nikaḻvatōr aiyam akaṟṟiyatu; uyirmey yeṉpatōr īṟu uṇṭēṉum atu puṇarkkappaṭātu, atuvum uyirāyē aṭaṅku meṉṟaliṉ.
itaṉ poruḷ: uyir meyyīṟum - uyirmey moḻi yiṉatu īṟṟiṉ kaṇ niṉṟatum, uyirīṟṟiyaṟṟē - uyirīṟṟiṉ iyalpi yuṭittu eṉṟavāṟu.
ummiyāṉ iṭiniṉṟa uyirmeyyum uyir iyalpi yuṭittu eṉṟārāyiṟṟu. ummi eccavummi. īṟṟiṉum iṭi yiṉum niṉṟaṉa uyiruḷ aṭaṅkumeṉavē mutal niṉṟaṉa meyyuḷ aṭaṅku meṉṟār. itaṉāṉē mēl viḷa eṉṟāṟ pōlum uyir meykaḷellām akaravīṟeṉṟu puṇarkkumāṟu uṇarka. varaku: itaṉi mēl uyirttoṭar moḻi yeṉpa. muṉṉar meyyiṉ vaḻiyatu (eḻut. 18) eṉṟatu ōreḻuttiṟ keṉṟu uṇarka. ittuṇiyum moḻimarapiṉ oḻipu kūṟiṟṟu.(4)
107.   uyiriṟu coṉmu ṉuyirvaru vaḻiyu
					muyiriṟu coṉmuṉ meyvaru vaḻiyu
					meyyiṟu coṉmu ṉuyirvaru vaḻiyu
					meyyiṟu coṉmuṉ meyvaru vaḻiyumeṉ
					ṟivveṉa vaṟiyak kiḷakkuṅ kāli
					niṟutta collē kuṟittuvaru kiḷaviyeṉ
					ṟāyī riyala puṇarnilic cuṭṭē.
itu, mēṟkūṟum puṇarccikaḷellām irumoḻip puṇarcci yallatu illi eṉpatūum, aḵtu eḻuttu vakiyāṉ nāṉkāmeṉpa tūum uṇarttukiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: uyiriṟu coṉmuṉ uyir varuvaḻiyum - uyir taṉakku īṟāka iṟuñ colliṉ muṉ uyir mutalākiya moḻivarum iṭamum, uyiriṟu coṉmuṉ meyvaru vaḻiyum - uyir taṉakku īṟāka iṟuñ colliṉ muṉṉar mey mutalākiya moḻi varum iṭamum, meyyiṟu coṉmuṉ uyirvaru vaḻiyum - mey taṉakku īṟāka iṟuñ colliṉ muṉṉar uyir mutalākiya moḻi varum iṭamum, meyyiṟu coṉmuṉ meyvaru vaḻiyum - mey taṉakku īṟāka iṟuñ colliṉ muṉṉar meymutalākiya moḻi varum iṭamum, eṉṟu puṇarnilic cuṭṭu - eṉṟu collappaṭṭa oṉṟiṉō ṭoṉṟu kūṭum nilimiyākiya karuttiṉkaṇ, ivveṉa aṟiyak kiḷakkuṅ kāli - avaṟṟi ittuṇiyeṉa variyaṟiyi ellārum aṟiya yāṅ kūṟuṅ kālattu, niṟutta collē kuṟittuvarukiḷavi yeṉṟu āyīriyala - muṉṉar niṟuttap paṭṭa collum ataṉi muṭittalik kuṟittu varuñ collum eṉṟu collappaṭṭa avviraṇṭu iyalpiṉi yuṭiya eṉṟavāṟu.
eṉavē, nāṉku vakiyāṉuṅ kūṭuṅkāl irumoḻi yallatu puṇarcciyiṉṟu eṉṟārāyiṟṟu.
utāraṇam:	āvuṇṭu, āvalitu, ālili, ālvīḻntatu eṉa muṟiyē kāṇka. viḷaviṉik kuṟittāṉ eṉṟavaḻic cāriyiyum urupum nili moḻiyāyē niṟkumeṉpatu nōkki ataṉi niṟutta colleṉṟum, muṭikkuñ collik kuṟittuvarukiḷavi yeṉṟum kūṟiṉār. itaṉāṉē nilimoḻiyum varumoḻiyuṅ kūṟiṉār. 8muṉṉar meyyē yuyir (eḻut. 103) eṉṟatu oru moḻikku; iḵtu irumoḻikkeṉṟu uṇarka.	(5)
108.	avaṟṟuḷ
					niṟutta colli ṉīṟā keḻuttoṭu
					kuṟittuvaru kiḷavi mutaleḻut tiyiyap
					peyaroṭu peyarip puṇarkkuṅ kālum
					peyaroṭu toḻilip puṇarkkuṅ kālun
					toḻiloṭu peyarip puṇarkkuṅ kālun
					toḻiloṭu toḻilip puṇarkkuṅ kālu
					mūṉṟē tiripiṭa ṉoṉṟē yiyalpeṉa
					vāṅkan nāṉkē moḻipuṇa riyalpē.
itu muṉṉar eḻuttuvakiyāṉ nāṉku puṇarcci eytiya ïruvakicbcālluñbcālvakiyāṉumeṉkākumeṉpaöum,mṅṅaṉamòṇarvatubcālluñbcāllummṉṟuvḻuttumvḻuttubmaṉpatūumcṇarttukiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: avaṟṟuḷ - nilimoḻi varumoḻi yeṉṟavaṟṟuḷ, niṟutta colliṉ īṟāku eḻuttoṭu kuṟittuvaru kiḷavi mutaleḻuttu iyiya - muṉṉar niṟuttap paṭṭa colliṉatu īṟākiṉṟa eḻuttōṭu ataṉi muṭikkak karuti varukiṉṟa colliṉatu mutaleḻuttup porunta, peya roṭu peyarip puṇarkkuṅ kālum - peyarc collōṭu peyarc collik kūṭṭum iṭattum, peyaroṭu toḻilip puṇarkkuṅ kālum - peyarc collōṭu toḻiṟ collik kūṭṭum iṭattum, toḻiloṭu peyarip puṇarkkuṅkālum - toḻiṟ collōṭu peyarccollik kūṭṭum iṭattum, toḻiloṭu toḻilip puṇarkkuṅ kālum - toḻiṟ colloṭu toḻiṟ collik kūṭṭum iṭattum, mūṉṟē tiripu iṭaṉ oṉṟē iyalpeṉa āṅku annāṉkē - tiriyum iṭam mūṉṟu iyalpu oṉṟu eṉṟu muntunūliṟ kūṟiya an nāṉku ilakkaṇamumē, moḻi puṇar iyalpu - īṇṭu moḻikaḷ tammuṭ kūṭum ilakkaṇam eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam: cāttaṉki, cāttaṉuṇṭāṉ, vantāṉ cāttaṉ, vantāṉ pōyiṉāṉ cāttaṉ eṉa muṟiyē kāṇka. ivi nāṉku iṉattōṭuṅ kūṭap patiṉāṟām. iṭiyum uriyun tāmāka nillāmiyiṟ peyarviṉiyē kūṟiṉār, iṭic collum uriccollum puṇarkkuñ ceykip paṭṭuḻip puṇarppuc ciṟupāṉmi. 9peyarp peyarum oṭṭup peyarumeṉa iraṇṭu vakippaṭum peyar. teriniliviṉiyuṅ kuṟippu viṉiyumeṉa iraṇṭu vakippaṭun toḻil. nilimoḻiyatu īṟṟeḻuttu muṉṉarp piṟantu keṭṭuppōka varumoḻiyiṉ mutaleḻuttup piṉpiṟantu keṭṭamiyāṉ muṟiyi piṟantu keṭuvaṉa oruṅku niṉṟu puṇaru māṟṟiṉmiyiṉ puṇarckiyeṉpatu oṉṟiṉṟām piṟaveṉiṉ, accoṟkaḷik kūṟukiṉṟōruṅ kēṭkiṉṟōrum .caya iṭiyaṟavu paṭāmal uḷḷattiṉ kaṇṇa uṇarvarātaliṉ avvōci kēṭiṉṟi uḷḷattiṉ kaṇnilipeṟṟup puṇarntaṉavēyām. ākavē, piṉṉark 10kaṇ kūṭākap puṇarkiṉṟa puṇarcciyum muṭintaṉavēyā meṉṟu uṇarka. iṉi, muyaṟkōṭu uṇṭeṉṟāl atu, kuṟittu varukiḷaviyaṉmiyiṟ . 11itu tāṉ iṉṟeṉṟāṟ puṇarkkappaṭumeṉṟu uṇarka. (6)
109.	avitām
					meypiṟi tātaṉ mikutal kuṉṟaleṉ
					nivveṉa moḻipa tiriyu māṟē.
itu, muṟkūṟiya mūṉṟu tiripum āmāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: avitān tiriyumāṟu - muṉṉart tiripeṉṟu kūṟiya avitān tirintu puṇarum neṟiyi, mey piṟitātal mikutal kuṉṟa leṉṟu ivveṉa moḻipa - mey vēṟupaṭutal mikutal kuṉṟaleṉṟu kūṟappaṭum immūṉṟu kūṟṟiyuṭiya veṉṟu kūṟuvar āciriyar eṉṟavāṟu.
immūṉṟum allātatu iyalpāmeṉṟu uṇarka. ivi 12vikaṟpikkap patiṉāṟu utāraṇamām. maṭkuṭam, malittali, maravēr ivi peyaroṭu peyar puṇarnta mūṉṟu tiripu. maṇmali eṉpatu iyalpu. coṟkēṭṭāṉ, palākkuṟittāṉ, maranaṭṭāṉ ivi peyaroṭu toḻil puṇarnta mūṉṟu tiripu. koṟṟaṉ vantāṉ: iḵtu iyalpu. vantāṉāṟ cāttaṉ, koṭāp poruḷ, ōṭunākam ivi toḻiloṭu peyar puṇarnta mūṉṟu tiripu. vantāṉ cāttāṉ: iḵtu iyalpu. vantāṟkoḷḷum, pāṭappōyiṉāṉ, cāñāṉṟāṉ ivi toḻiloṭu toḻil puṇarnta mūṉṟu tiripu. vantāṉ koṇṭāṉ, iḵtu iyalpu.  mūṉṟu tiripeṉṉātu iṭaṉeṉṟataṉāṉ oru puṇarccikkaṇ mūṉṟum oruṅkēyum varappeṟu meṉṟu uṇarka. makattāṟ koṇṭāṉ: iḵtu aṅṅaṉam vantavāṟu makara īṟṟu nāṭpeyarkkiḷavi (eḻut. 331) eṉṉuñ cūttirattāṉ uṇarka. iraṇṭu varuvaṉavuṅ kāṇka, (7) 
110.		niṟutta colluṅ kuṟittuvaru kiḷaviyu
					maṭiyoṭu tōṉṟiṉum puṇarnilik kuriya.
itu, nilimoḻi aṭiyaṭuttum varumoḻi aṭi yaṭuttum avvirumoḻiyum aṭiyaṭuttum puṇarumeṉa eytiyataṉmēṟ ciṟappu viti kūṟukiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: niṟuttacolluṅ kuṟittuvarukiḷaviyum - nili moḻiyāka niṟuttiṉa collum ataṉik kuṟittu varuñ collum, aṭiyoṭu tōṉṟiṉum puṇarnilikku uriya - tāmē puṇarātu orōvoru col aṭiyaṭuttu variṉum iraṇṭum aṭiyaṭuttu variṉum puṇar nilimikku uriya eṉṟavāṟu.
13aṭiyāvaṉa - ummittokiyum irupeyaroṭṭup paṇput tokiyumām.
utāraṇam:	 patiṉāyirattoṉṟu, āyirattorupaḵtu, patiṉāyirat tirupaḵtu eṉa varum. ivvaṭikaḷ oru collēyām. 14vēṟṟumit tokiyum uvamat tokiyum muṭiyap paṇput tokiyum viṉit tokiyum piḷantu muṭiyāmiyiṉ oru collēyām. aṉ moḻittokiyun taṉakku vēṟōr muṭipiṉmi yiṉ oru collēyām. ittokic coṟkaḷellām aṭiyāy varuṅkālattu orucollāy varumeṉṟu uṇarka. uṇṭacāttaṉ vantāṉ, uṇṭuvantāṉ cāttaṉ eṉpaṉavum oru collē yām.	(8)
111.	15maruviṉ ṟokuti mayaṅkiyaṉ moḻiyu
					muriyavi yuḷavē puṇarnilic cuṭṭē.
itu, marūuc coṟkaḷum puṇarccipeṟu meṉpatūum, niṟutta colluṅ kuṟittuvaru kiḷaviyumāyp poruḷiyi pillaṉavum puṇarcci peṟṟāṟpōla niṟkumeṉpatūum uṇarttukiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: maru moḻiyum - iruvakiyāki maruviya coṟkaḷum, iṉṟokuti mayaṅkiyaṉ moḻiyum - cevikkiṉi tākac coṟṟira ḷiṭattu niṟutta colluṅ kuṟittuvaru kiḷaviyumāy oṭṭiṉāṟ pōla niṉṟu poruḷuṇarttātu pirintu piṉṉarc ceṉṟu oṭṭip poruḷuṇartta mayaṅkutal iyaṉṟa coṟkaḷum, puṇar nilic cuṭṭu uriyavi uḷa - puṇarum nilimik karuttiṉkaṇ uriyaṉa uḷa eṉṟavāṟu.
moḻiyumeṉpataṉi maru eṉpataṉōṭuṅ kūṭṭuka. iṉ ṟokuti eṉṟār, pāveṉṉum uṟuppu nikaḻap poruḷoṭṭā maṟ cāṉṟōr coṟkaḷic cērttaliṉ.
utāraṇam:	muṉṟil, mīkaṇ ivi ilakkaṇattoṭu poruntiya maru. ilakkaṇam allā maru vaḻaṅkiyaṉ maruṅkiṉ maruvoṭu tirinavum (eḻut. 483) eṉpuḻik kāṭṭutum. iṉi,

irumpu tirittaṉṉa māyiru maruppiṟ
paralava laṭiya virali teṟippa	
eṉpuḻi maruppiṉirali yeṉṟu oṭṭi iraṇṭāvataṉ toki yāyp poruṭantu puḷḷi yīṟṟumuṉ ṉuyirtaṉit tiyalā (eḻut. 138) eṉṟu uyirēṟi muṭintu mayaṅkiniṉṟatu. āyiṉ maruppiṟpara leṉṟu mey piṟitāy oṭṭi niṉṟavā ṟeṉṉi yeṉiṉ, maruppiṉi yuṭiya paraleṉa vēṟṟumittokip poruḷ uṇarttāmiyiṉ, aḵtu acceyyuṭku iṉṉōci nikaḻtaṟkup pakarattiṉ muṉṉar niṉṟa ṉakaram nakaramāyt tirintu niṉṟa tuṇiyēyāyp puṇarccip payaṉiṉṟi niṉṟatu. iṅṅaṉam puṇarcci yeytiṉāṟpōla, māṭṭilak kaṇattiṉkaṇṇum moḻi māṟṟiṉkaṇṇum niṟṟal coṟku iyalpeṉṟaṟku aṉṟē āciriyar iṉṟokuti yeṉṟa teṉṟu uṇarka. karuṅkā lōmik kāṇpiṉ peruñciṉi (akam. 3) eṉpuḻi ōmicciṉi yeṉṟu oṭṭi ōmiyiṉatu ciṉiyeṉap poruḷ tarukiṉṟatu, iṉṉōci tarutaṟkuk kakaravoṟṟu mikkuk kāṇpi ṉeṉpataṉōṭum oṭṭiṉāṟ pōla niṉṟatu. teyva mālvarit tirumuṉi yaruḷāl (cilap. 3 : 1) eṉpuḻit teyva variyeṉṟu oṭṭit teyvat taṉmiyuṭiya vari yeṉap poruḷ tarukiṉṟatu, iṉṉōci tarutaṟku māleṉpataṉōṭum oṭṭiṉāṟpōlak kuṟintu niṉṟatu. mūṉṟu tiripum vantavāṟu kāṇka.
iṉi eccattiṉkaṇṇum,
poṉṉōṭip pukaraṇinutaṟ
ṟuṉṉaruntiṟaṟ kamaḻkaṭā attu
eyiṟupaṭi yāka veyiṟkata viṭāak
kayiṟupiṇik koṇṭa kaviḻmaṇi maruṅkiṟ
peruṅki yāṉi yirumpiṭart taliyiruntu
maruntil kūṟṟat taruntoḻil cāyā			(puṟam. 3)
eṉa māṭṭāy oṭṭiniṉṟatu, kayiṟu piṇikkoṇṭa eṉpata ṉōṭum oṭṭiṉāṟpōla niṉṟu oṟṟaṭuttatu, iṉṉōci peṟu taṟku. piṟa cāṉṟōr ceyyuṭkaṇ ivvāṟum piṟavāṟum puṇarcciyilvaḻip puṇarcci peṟṟāṟpōla niṟpaṉa ellā vaṟṟiṟkum ituvē ōttākak koḷka.	(9)
112.	vēṟṟumi kuṟitta puṇarmoḻi niliyum
					vēṟṟumi yalvaḻip puṇarmoḻi niliyu
					meḻuttē cāriyi yāyiru paṇpi
					ṉoḻukkal valiya puṇaruṅ kāli.
itu mūvakit tiripiṉuḷ mikkuppuṇarum puṇarcci iru vakiya eṉkiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: puṇaruṅkāli - nālvakip puṇarcciyuḷ mikka puṇarcci puṇaruṅ kālattu, vēṟṟumi kuṟitta puṇarmoḻi niliyum - vēṟṟumip poruṇmiyiṉik kuṟitta puṇar moḻiyiṉatu taṉmiyum, vēṟṟumi alvaḻip puṇarmoḻi niliyum - vēṟṟumiyallāta alvaḻiyiṭattup puṇarum moḻiyiṉatu taṉmiyum, eḻuttē cāriyi āyiru paṇpiṉ oḻukkal valiya - eḻuttu mikutaluñ cāriyi mikutalumākiya avviraṇṭu kuṇattiṉāṉuñ cellutalit tamakku valiyāka vuṭiya eṉṟavāṟu.
eṉavē, ēṉip puṇarccikaḷukku ittuṇi vēṟupāṭu iṉṟeṉa uṇarka.
utāraṇam: viḷaṅkōṭu: iḵtu eḻuttup peṟṟatu. makaviṉki: itu cāriyi peṟṟatu. iṉi alvaḻikkaṇ viḷak kuṟitu: iḵtu eḻuttup peṟṟatu. paṉiyiṉkuṟi: itu cāriyi peṟṟatu. itaṟkup paṉi kuṟintateṉpatu poruḷām. iḵtu aḷavuppeyar. oḻukkal valiya veṉṟataṉāṉ, ikkūṟiya iraṇṭum eḻuttuñ cāriyi yum uṭaṉpeṟutaluṅ koḷka. avaṟṟuk kōṭeṉpatu vēṟṟumikkaṇ iraṇṭum peṟṟatu. kalattukkuṟi yeṉpatu alvaḻikkaṇ iraṇṭum peṟṟatu. itaṟkuk kalaṅkuṟintatuteṉpatu poruḷām. iyalpu kaṇattuk kaṇ ivviraṇṭum uṭaṉpeṟutaliṉṟu. alvaḻi muṟkūṟātatu vēṟṟumi yallātatu alvaḻiyeṉa vēṇṭutaliṉ. eḻuttuppēṟu 16yāppuṭimiyāṉum eḻuttiṉāṟ cāriyi yātalāṉum eḻuttu muṟ kūṟiṉār. vēṟṟumi mēlic cūttirattē kūṟukiṉṟār.
alvaḻiyāvaṉa - avvurupukaḷ tokkum virintum nillātu puṇarvaṉa. avi eḻuvāyvēṟṟumi 17āṟu payaṉiliyōṭum puṇarnta puṇarcciyum, viḷivēṟṟumi taṉ poruḷōṭu puṇarnta puṇarcciyum, muṟṟu peyarōṭum viṉiyōṭum puṇarnta puṇarcciyum, peyareccamum viṉiyeccamum peyarōṭum viṉiyōṭum puṇarnta puṇarcciyum, uvamat tokiyum, ummittokiyum, irupeyaroṭṭup paṇputtoki yum, iṭiccollum uriccollum peyarōṭum viṉiyōṭum puṇarnta puṇarcciyum, aṉmoḻittoki poruḷōṭu puṇarnta puṇarcciyum, paṇputtokiyum viṉit tokiyum virintu niṉṟavaḻip puṇarnta puṇarcciyumeṉa uṇarka.	(10)
113.			aioṭu kuiṉ atukaṇ ṇeṉṉu
					mavvā ṟeṉpa vēṟṟumi yurupē.
itu, mēl vēṟṟumi yeṉappaṭṭa avaṟṟiṉ peyarum muṟiyun tokiyum uṇarttukiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ:  vēṟṟumiyurupu - muṟkūṟiya vēṟṟumic coṟ kaḷi, ai oṭu ku iṉ atu kaṇ eṉṉum avvāṟeṉpa - ai oṭu ku iṉ atu kaṇ eṉṟu collappaṭum avvāṟu urupumeṉṟu colvar āciriyar eṉṟavāṟu.
18mēṟ collatikārattu eḻuvāyiyum viḷiyiyuṅ kūṭṭi vēṟṟumi eṭṭeṉpārāleṉiṉ, ai mutaliya vēṟṟumi yāṟun tokkum virintum perumpāṉmiyum pulappaṭṭu niṉṟu peyarp poruḷic ceyappaṭuporuḷ mutaliyaṉavāka vēṟupāṭu ceytu puṇarcci yeytuvikku meṉṟaṟku īṇṭu āṟeṉṟār. āṇṭu 19eḻuvāyum viḷiyuñ ceyappaṭuporuḷ mutaliyavaṟṟiṉiṉṟu tammi vēṟupaṭuttup poruḷ māttiram uṇarttiniṉṟum viḷiyāy etirmuka mākki niṉṟum iṅṅaṉañ ciṟupāṉmiyāyp pulappaṭa nillā vēṟupāṭu uṭiyavēṉum, aviyum oruvāṟ ṟāṉ vēṟṟumiyāyiṉa veṉṟaṟku āṇṭu eṭṭu eṉṟāreṉa uṇarka.		(11)
114.		valleḻuttu mutaliya vēṟṟumi yurupiṟ
					kolvaḻi yoṟṟiṭi mikutal vēṇṭum.
itu, nāṉkāvataṟkum ēḻāvataṟkum urupiyali nōkkiya toru karuvi kūṟukiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ:valleḻuttu mutaliya vēṟṟumi yurupiṟku valleḻuttu aṭiyāy niṉṟa nāṉkāvataṟkum ēḻāvataṟkum, olvaḻi oṟṟu iṭi mikutal vēṇṭum - poruntiyavaḻi valloṟṟāyiṉum melloṟṟāyiṉum iṭikkaṇ mikkup puṇartali virumpum āciriyaṉ eṉṟavāṟu.
variyātu oṟṟeṉavē, valloṟṟum melloṟṟum peṟṟām.
utāraṇam:	maṇikku maṇikkaṇ, tīkku tīkkaṇ, maṉikku            maṉik kaṇ eṉavum, vēykku vēykkaṇ, ūrkku, ūrkkaṇ, pūḻkku pūḻkkaṇ eṉavum, uyirīṟu mūṉṟiṉum puḷḷiyīṟu mūṉṟiṉum perumpāṉmi valloṟṟu mikku varum. taṅkaṇ naṅkaṇ nuṅkaṇ eṅkaṇ eṉa mel loṟṟu mikkatu. ivaṟṟiṟku nilimoḻi makarakkēṭu 20urupiyaliṟ kūṟupa. 21āṅkaṇ īṅkaṇ ūṅkaṇ eṉpaṉa cuṭṭeḻuttu nīṇṭu niṉṟaṉa. ivaṟṟiṟku oṟṟukkēṭu kūṟutaṟku oṟṟiṉṟu.
iṉi, nāṉkaṉurupiṟku melloṟṟu mikāteṉṟu uṇarka.
iṉi, olvaḻi yeṉpataṉāṉ ēḻāmurupiṉkaṇ nampikaṇ eṉa ikara īṟṟiṉkaṇṇum, naṅkikaṇ eṉa aikāra īṟṟiṉ kaṇṇun tāykaṇ eṉa yakara īṟṟiṉkaṇṇum, aracarkaṇ eṉa rakara īṟṟiṉkaṇṇum, oṟṟu mikāmi koḷka.
iṉi, meypiṟitātali muṉṉē kūṟātu mikutali muṟ kūṟiya ataṉāṉē, poṟku poṟkaṇ, vēṟku vēṟkaṇ, vāṭku vāṭkaṇ eṉat tirintu muṭivaṉavuṅ koḷka. itaṉāṉē, avaṉ kaṇ avaḷkaṇ eṉa uyartiṇip peyarkkaṇ ēḻaṉurupu iyal pāy varutaluṅ koḷka. ivaṟṟiṟkuk kuṉṟiya puṇarcci varumēṉuṅ koḷka.
koṟṟikku koṟṟikaṇ, kōtikku kōtikaṇ eṉa viravuppeyarkkum itaṉāṉē koḷka.	(12)
115.		āṟa ṉurupi ṉakarak kiḷavi
					yīṟā kakaramuṉik keṭutal vēṇṭum.
iḵtu āṟāvataṟkut tokimarapi nōkkiyatoru karuvi kūṟukiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ:āṟaṉurupiṉ akarakkiḷavi - atuveṉ ṉum āṟaṉuru piṉkaṇiṉṟa akaramākiya eḻuttu, īṟāku akaramuṉik keṭutal vēṇṭum - neṭumutal kuṟukum moḻikaṭku īṟāku puḷḷi yakaramoṭu niliyum (eḻut. 161) eṉa vitittataṉāl uḷatākiya akarattiṉ muṉṉart tāṉ keṭutali virumpum āciriyaṉ eṉṟavāṟu.
tamatu, namatu, ematu, numatu, taṉatu, eṉatu, niṉatu eṉavarum. itu nilimoḻikku ōr akaram peṟumeṉa vitiyātu urupu akaram ēṟi muṭiyumeṉa vitittāl varuṅ kuṟṟam uṇṭō veṉiṉ, 22niṉava kūṟuva leṉava kēṇmati (puṟam. 35) eṉṟāṟ pōla āṟāvataṟku uriya akara urupiṉ muṉṉarum ōr akara eḻuttuppēṟu nili moḻikkaṇ varutaluḷatākak karutiṉā rātaliṉ, āṟaṉurupiṟkum nāṉkaṉurupiṟkum potuvāka nili moḻikkaṇ akarappēṟu vitittu, atuveṉṉum, orumiyurupu vantāl āṇṭup peṟṟu niṉṟa akarattiṉ muṉṉar atu veṉpataṉkaṇ akaraṅ keṭuka veṉṟu īṇṭuk kūṟiṉā rātaliṉ ataṟkuk kuṟṟam uṇṭeṉṟu uṇarka.	(13)
116.	vēṟṟumi vaḻiya peyarpuṇar niliyē.
itu, vēṟṟumi peyarkkaṇ niṟkumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ:vēṟṟumi peyarvaḻiya - vēṟṟumikaḷ peyariṉ piṉṉiṭattaṉavām, puṇarnili - avaṟṟoṭu puṇarum nilimik kaṇ eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam: cāttaṉi, cāttaṉoṭu, cāttaṟku, cāttaṉiṉ, cāttaṉatu, cāttaṉkaṇ eṉa varum. maṟṟu itu kūṟiyamuṟiyiṉ (col. 69) eṉṉum vēṟṟumi yōttiṟ cūttirattāṟ peṟutu meṉiṉ, peyaroṭu peyarip puṇarttal mutaliya nālvakip puṇarcciyiṉi yum vēṟṟumi alvaḻi eṉa iraṇṭāka aṭakkutaliṉ, toḻiṟpiṉṉum urupu varumeṉa eytiyataṉi vilakku taṟku īṇṭuk kūṟiṉāreṉka. āyiṉ ivvilakkutal viṉiyiyal mutaṟ cūttirattāṟ peṟutu meṉiṉ, atu 23mutaṉiliyik kūṟiṟṟeṉpatu āṇṭu uṇarka.	(14)
117.		uyartiṇip peyarē aḵṟiṇip peyareṉ
					ṟāyiraṇ ṭeṉpa peyarnilic cuṭṭē.
itu,  muṟkūṟiya peyarkaṭkup peyarum muṟiyun tokiyuṅ kūṟukiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: cuṭṭu nilippeyar - poruḷi oruvar karututaṟkuk kāraṇamāṉa nilimiyiyuṭiya peyarkaḷi, uyartiṇip peyarē aḵṟiṇip peyareṉṟu āyiraṇ ṭeṉpa - uyar tiṇip poruḷi oruvaṉ karututaṟkuk kāraṇamāṉa peyarum, aḵṟiṇip poruḷi oruvaṉ karututaṟkuk kāraṇamāṉa peyarum eṉṉum avviraṇṭeṉṟu kūṟuvar āciriyar eṉṟavāṟu.
peyariyaluḷ, avaṉ ivaṉ uvaṉ eṉpaṉa mutalāka uyar tiṇip peyarum, atu itu utu eṉpaṉa mutalāka aḵṟiṇip peyarum āmāṟu avaṟṟiṟku ilakkaṇaṅ kūṟukiṉṟār, īṇṭuk kuṟiyiṭṭāḷutal māttiriyē kūṟiṉāreṉṟu uṇarka. iṉik koṟṟaṉ koṟṟi eṉṟāṟpōlum viravup peyaruṅ, koṟṟaṉkuṟiyaṉ koṟṟikuṟiyaḷ koṟṟaṉ kuḷampu koṟṟi kuṟitu eṉap piṉvaruvaṉavaṟṟāṟ ṟiṇiteritaliṉ, irutiṇip peyariṉkaṇ aṭaṅkum. 24koṟṟaṉcevi koṟṟicevi eṉpaṉavum piṉṉar varukiṉṟa viṉikaḷāṟ tiṇi viḷaṅki aṭaṅkumāṟu uṇarka. iṉi, aḵṟiṇi viravuppeya riyalpumā ruḷavē (eḻu.155) eṉṟāṟ pōlap piṟāṇṭum ōtutalpaṟṟi niliyeṉṟataṉāṉ, viravup peyar kōṭalum oṉṟu.	(15)
118.	avaṟṟuvaḻi maruṅkiṟ cāriyi varumē.
itu, cāriyi varumiṭaṅ kūṟukiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: avaṟṟuvaḻi maruṅkiṉ - accollap paṭṭa iru vakip peyarkaḷiṉ piṉṉākiya iṭattē, cāriyi varum - cāriyic coṟkaḷ varum eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam: āṭūuviṉki, makaṭūuviṉki, palavaṟṟuk kōṭu eṉap 25puṇariyaṉiliyiṭip poruṇilikku utavi vantaṉa. cāriyi yeṉṟataṉ poruḷ, vēṟāki niṉṟa irumoḻiyun tammiṟ cārtaṟ poruṭṭu iyintu niṉṟatu eṉṟavāṟu.	(16)
119.	avitām
					iṉṉē vaṟṟē yattē yammē
					yoṉṉē yāṉē yakkē yikkē
					yaṉṉeṉ kiḷavi yuḷappaṭap piṟavu
					maṉṉa veṉpa cāriyi moḻiyē.
iḵtu - accāriyikaṭkup peyarum muṟiyum uṇarttu kiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: avitām - muṉṉarc cāriyi yeṉappaṭṭa avi tām, iṉṉē vaṟṟē attē ammē oṉṉē āṉē akkē ikkē aṉṉeṉ kiḷavi uḷappaṭa aṉṉa eṉpa - iṉṉum vaṟṟum attum ammum oṉṉum āṉum akkum ikkum aṉṉeṉṉuñ collōṭu kūṭa oṉpatākiya attaṉmiyuṭiyaṉavum, piṟavuñ cāriyi moḻi eṉpa - avi yoḻintaṉavuñ cāriyic collāmeṉpar āciriyar eṉṟavāṟu.
piṟavāvaṉa: tam, nam, num, um, ñāṉṟu, keḻu, ē, ai eṉpaṉa vām. ivaṟṟuḷ ñāṉṟu oḻintaṉa eṭuttōtuvar āciriyar. eṭutta naṟaviṉ 26kuliyalaṅ kāntaḷ (kali. 40) itu viṉit toki; cāriyi yaṉṟu. iṉ cāriyi vaḻakkuppayiṟciyum palakāl eṭuttōtap paṭutalum 27potuvakiyāṉ ōtiyavaḻit tāṉē cēṟalumākiya ciṟappu nōkki muṉ vittār. vaṟṟum attum iṉ pōla mutal tiriyumākalāṉuñ ceyki yoppumiyāṉum ataṉ piṉ vittār. am īṟu tiriyu mātaliṉ tiripu paṟṟi ataṉpiṉ vittār. oṉ īṟu tiriyu mēṉum vaḻakkuppayiṟci yiṉṟi nāṉkā murupiṉkaṇ tiritaliṉ ataṉpiṉ vittār. āṉ poruṭ puṇarccikkum urupu puṇarccikkum varu meṉṟu ataṉ piṉ vittār. akku īṟu tiriyumēṉum urupu puṇarccik kaṇ vārā miyiṉ ataṉpiṉ vittār. ikku mutal tiriyumēṉuñ ciṟupāṉmipaṟṟi ataṉpiṉ vittār. aṉ iṉ pōlac ciṟattaliṟ piṉ vittār.
āṉurupiṟkum āṉcāriyikkum, iṉṉurupiṟkum iṉcāri yikkum vēṟṟumi yāteṉiṉ, avi cāriyiyāṉa iṭattu yātāṉum ōr urupēṟṟu muṭiyum; urupāyiṉa iṭattu vēṟōr urupiṉi ēlāveṉṟu uṇarka. iṉi makattukki eṉpuḻit takaravoṟṟun takara vukaramum varumeṉṟu kōṭu meṉiṉ, iruḷattuk koṇṭāṉeṉṟāl attu eṉavē vēṇṭutaliṉ āṇṭum attuniṉṟē keṭṭa teṉṟu kōṭum. akku ikku eṉpaṉavum pirittuk kūṭṭak kiṭakkum. tāḻakkō leṉa akkup peṟṟu niṟṟalāṉum, 28āṭikku eṉpuḻik kukaram nāṉkaṉurupākāmi yāṉum ivi cāriyi yāmāṟu uṇarka. 	(17)
120.	avaṟṟuḷ
					iṉṉi ṉikara māvi ṉiṟuti
					muṉṉark keṭuta lurittu mākum.
itu, muṟkūṟiyavaṟṟuḷ iṉcāriyi mutal tiriyumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: avaṟṟuḷ - muṟkūṟiya cāriyikaḷuḷ, iṉṉiṉ ikaram-iṉcāriyiyatu ikaram, āviṉ iṟuti muṉṉar - ā eṉṉum ōreḻut torumoḻi muṉṉar, keṭutal urittumākum-keṭṭumuṭiyavumpeṟumeṉṟavāṟu.
‘urittumākum’ eṉṟataṉāṟ keṭātu muṭiyavum peṟum eṉṟavāṟu.  iḵtu oppakkūṟal eṉṉum utti.
utāraṇam: āṉi āviṉi, āṉoṭu āviṉoṭu, āṟku āviṟku, āṉiṉ āviṉiṉ, āṉatu āviṉatu, āṉ kaṇ āviṉkaṇ eṉa varum.
iṉi, muṉṉar eṉṟataṉāṉē, 29māviṟkum ivvāṟē koḷka. māṉi māviṉi, māṉoṭu māviṉoṭu, māṟku mā viṟku eṉa oṭṭuka.  ākāra īṟeṉṉaṟātu āviṉiṟutiyeṉṟu ōtiṉamiyiṉ mā ileciṉāṟ koḷḷappaṭṭatu.
iṉi, āṉkōṭu āviṉkōṭu, māṉkōṭu māviṉkōṭu eṉa urupiṟkuc ceṉṟacāriyi poruṭkaṭ ceṉṟuḻiyuṅkoḷf.		(18)
121.		aḷavāku moḻimuta ṉiliiya vuyirmici
					ṉaḵkāṉ ṟaḵkā ṉākiya nilittē.
iḵtu - avviṉ cāriyi īṟutiriyumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: aḷavākum moḻi - aḷavup peyarāyp piṉṉiṟkum moḻikku, mutal niliiya uyirmici ṉaḵkāṉ - muṉṉar niṉṟa eṇṇup peyarkaḷiṉ īṟṟu niṉṟa kuṟṟukarattiṉ mēl vanta iṉcāriyiyatu ṉakaram, ṟaḵkāṉākiya nilittu - ṟakaramāyt tiriyum nilimiyi yuṭittu eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam: patiṟṟakal, patiṟṟuḻakku eṉa varum. ivaṟṟip patteṉa niṟutti niṟiyumaḷavum (eḻu. 436) eṉṉuñ cūttirattāl iṉcāriyi koṭuttuk, kuṟṟiya lukaram meyyōṭuṅ keṭumē (eḻu. 433) eṉṟataṉāṟ kuṟṟukaram mey yoṭuṅ keṭuttu, 30vēṇṭuñ ceyka ceytu muṟṟaviṉvarūum’ (eḻu. 433) eṉpataṉāṉ oṟṟiraṭṭittu muṭikka.
niliiya eṉṟataṉāṟ, 31piṟavaḻiyum iṉṉiṉ ṉakaram ṟakara mātal koḷka. patiṟṟeḻuttu, patiṟṟaṭukku, oṉpatiṟ ṟeḻuttu, patiṟṟoṉṟu, patiṟṟiraṇṭu, patiṟṟoṉpatu eṉa ellāvaṟṟōṭum oṭṭikkoḷka. accūttirattiṟ kuṟiyātākum (eḻu. 436) eṉṟataṉāṟ poruṭ peyarkkum eṇṇup peyarkkum iṉ koṭukka.	(19)


122.		vaḵkāṉ meykeṭac cuṭṭumuta limmu
			ṉaḵkā ṉiṟṟa lākiya paṇpē.
itu, vaṟṟu mutal tiriyumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: cuṭṭumutal aimmuṉ - cuṭṭeḻuttiṉi mutalākavuṭiya aikāra īṟṟuc coṉmuṉ vaṟṟu varuṅkāli, vaḵkāṉ mey keṭa aḵkāṉniṟṟalākiapaṇò –avvaṟṟuc cāriyiyiṉatu vakaramākiya oṟṟukkeṭ āṇṭu ēṟiya akaram niṟṟal ataṟku uḷatākiya kuṇam eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam: 	aviyaṟṟi, iviyaṟṟi, uviyaṟṟi eṉa varum. iṉṉum ivaṟṟi, avi ivi uvi eṉa niṟuttic cuṭṭu mutalākiya viye ṉiṟuti (eḻut. 177) eṉṟataṉāṉ vaṟṟum urupuṅ koṭuttu vēṇṭuñ ceyki ceyka. ivvāṟē ellā urupiṟkum oṭṭuka. aviyaṟṟuk kōṭu eṉa urupiṟkuc ceṉṟa cāriyi poruṭkaṭ ceṉṟa vaḻiyuṅ koḷka.
ākiya paṇpu eṉṟataṉāṉē, 32evaṉeṉpatu paṭutta lōciyāṟ peyarāyavaḻi evaṉ eṉa niṟutti, vaṟṟum urupuṅ koṭuttu, vaṟṟumici yoṟṟeṉṟu ṉakaraṅ keṭuttu, akaravuyir muṉṉar vaṟṟiṉ vakaraṅ keṭumeṉak keṭuttu, evaṟṟi evaṟṟoṭu eṉa muṭikka.	(20)
123.	ṉaḵkāṉ ṟaḵkā ṉāṉka ṉurupiṟku.
iḵtu iṉ oṉ āṉ aṉṉeṉṉum ṉakara īṟu nāṉkun - tiriyumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: ṉaḵkāṉ nāṉkaṉurupiṟku ṟaḵkāṉ - ṉakāra īṟṟu nāṉku cāriyiyiṉ ṉakaramum nāṉkāmurupiṟku ṟakāramāyt tiriyum eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam: viḷaviṟku, kōoṟku, orupāṟku, ataṟku eṉa varum.
itaṉi aḷavāku moḻimutal (eḻu. 121) eṉpataṉ piṉ viyātu īṇṭu vittatu, ṉakara īṟukaḷellām uṭaṉ tiriyu meṉṟaṟku. āṇṭu vippiṉ iṉ cāriyiyē tiriyumeṉpatu paṭum. oṉṟu mutalākap pattūrntu varūu, mellā veṇṇum (eḻu. 199) eṉpataṉāṉ, orupāṟku eṉpataṉi muṭikka.(21)

124.		āṉi ṉakaramu mataṉō raṟṟē
					nāṇmuṉ varūum vaṉmutaṟ ṟoḻiṟkē.
iḵtu - āṉiṉ īṟu poruṭpuṇarccikkaṇ tiriyumeṉ kiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: nāḷmuṉ varūum vaṉmutaṟ toḻiṟku - nāṭpeyar muṉṉar varum valleḻutti mutalāka uṭiya toḻiṟcoṟku iṭiyē varum, āṉiṉ ṉakaramum ataṉō raṟṟu - āṉ cāriyi yiṉ ṉakaramum nāṉka ṉurupiṉkaṇ varum āṉcāriyi pōla ṟakaramāyt tiriyum eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam: paraṇiyāṟ koṇṭāṉ, ceṉṟāṉ, tantāṉ, pōyiṉāṉ eṉa varum. nāṇmuṟ ṟōṉṟun toḻiṉilik kiḷavikku (eḻu. 247) eṉṟataṉāṉ āṉ cāriyi koṭuttuc ceyki ceyka. iṉi, ummiyi iṟantatu taḻīiyatākki, nāḷallavaṟṟumuṉ varum vaṉmutaṟṟoḻiṟkaṇ iṉṉiṉ ṉakaramum ataṉōṭu okkumeṉap poruḷurittup, paṉiyiṟkoṇṭāṉ, vaḷiyiṟ koṇṭāṉ eṉa iṉṉiṉ ṉakaramum ṟakaramātal koḷka.
iṉi, 33ñāpakattāl toḻiṟkaṇ iṉṉiṉ ṉakaran tiriyu meṉavē, peyarkkaṇ iṉṉiṉ ṉakaran tiritalun tiriyāmiyuṅ koḷka. kuṟumpiṟkoṟṟaṉ, paṟampiṟpāri eṉat tirintu vantaṉa. kurukiṉkāl, eruttiṉpuṟam eṉat tiriyātu vantaṉa.	(22)
125.	atti ṉakara makaramuṉi yilli.
iḵtu - attu mutal tiriyumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ:attiṉ akaram - attuc cāriyiyiṉ akaram, akara muṉi illi - akara īṟṟuc coṉ muṉṉar illiyām eṉṟavāṟu.
attavaṇ variṉum varinili yiṉṟē (eḻu. 219) eṉpataṉāṉ, makappeyar attuppeṟṟu niṉṟatu, makattukki yeṉa akaraṅkeṭṭu niṉṟatu. viḷavattukkaṇeṉpuḻik keṭātu niṟṟal attēvaṟṟē (eḻut. 133) eṉpataṉuḷ teṟṟeṉ ṟaṟṟē eṉpataṉāṟ kūṟuka.	(23)
126.	ikki ṉikara mikaramuṉi yaṟṟē.
iḵtu - ikku mutal tiriyumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: ikkiṉ ikaram - ikkuc cāriyiyiṉatu ikaram. ikaramuṉi aṟṟu - ikara īṟṟuc coṉ muṉṉar muṟkūṟiya attup pōlak keṭum eṉṟavāṟu.
tiṅkaḷ muṉvariṉ (eḻu. 248) eṉpataṉāṟ peṟṟa ikku, āṭikkuk koṇṭāṉ, ceṉṟāṉ, tantāṉ, pōyiṉāṉ eṉa ikaraṅkeṭṭu niṉṟatu. iḵtu iṭapporuṭṭu.	(24)
127.	aiyiṉ muṉṉaru mavviya ṉiliyum.
ituvum atu.
itaṉ poruḷ: aiyiṉ muṉṉarum avviyal niliyum - ikkiṉ ikaram ikara īṟṟuccoṉ muṉṉaraṉṟi aikāra īṟṟuc coṉ muṉṉarum mēṟkūṟiya keṭutaliyalpilē niṟkum eṉṟavāṟu.
tiṅkaḷu nāḷu muntukiḷan taṉṉa (eḻut. 286) eṉpataṉāṟ, cittirikkuk koṇṭāṉ eṉpuḻip peṟṟa ikku aikārattiṉ muṉṉark keṭṭavāṟu kāṇka.	(25)
128.			eppeyar muṉṉarum valleḻuttu varuvaḻi
					34yakki ṉiṟutimeym miciyoṭuṅ keṭumē
						kuṟṟiya lukara muṟṟat tōṉṟātu.
iḵtu akku mutal oḻiya ēṉiya keṭumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: eppeyar muṉṉarum - evvakippaṭṭa peyarccoṉ muṉṉarum, valleḻuttu varuvaḻi - valleḻuttu varu moḻiyāy varumiṭattu, akkiṉ iṟutik kuṟṟiyalukaram muṟṟat tōṉṟātu - iṭi niṉṟa akkuc cāriyiyiṉ iṟuti niṉṟa kuṟṟiyalukaram muṭiyat tōṉṟātu, meymmiciyoṭuṅ keṭum - akkuṟṟukaram ēṟi niṉṟa valloṟṟut taṉakkumēl niṉṟa valloṟṟōṭuṅ keṭum eṉṟavāṟu.
oṟṟunili tiriyā takkoṭu varūum (eḻu. 418) eṉṟata ṉāṉ, akkuppeṟṟa 35kuṉṟakkūki maṉṟappeṇṇi eṉpaṉa vum, vēṟṟumi yāyi ṉēṉi yiraṇṭum (eḻu. 329) eṉpata ṉāṉ, akkuppeṟṟa īmakkuṭam kammakkuṭam eṉpaṉavum, tamiḻeṉ kiḷaviyum (eḻu. 385) eṉpataṉāṉ, akkup peṟṟa tamiḻak kūttu eṉpatuvum, akku īṟum īṟṟayalum keṭṭavāṟu kāṇka. iṅ ṅaṉam varutaliṉ eppeyar eṉṟār. muṟṟa veṉpataṉāṉ, vaṉkaṇamaṉṟi ēṉiyavaṟṟiṟkum ivviti koḷka. tamiḻnūl, tamiḻayāḻ, tamiḻavariyar eṉa varum. iṉṉum itaṉāṉē, tamakkēṟṟa iyipu valleḻuttuk koṭuttu muṭittuk koḷka. aṉṟik kēṭōtiya kakaravoṟṟu niṟkumeṉiṉ, cakaran takaram pakaram vantavaṟṟiṟkuk kakara voṟṟā kāmi uṇarka.	(26)
129.		ammi ṉiṟuti kacatak kālit
					taṉmey tirintu ṅañana vākum.
iḵtu am īṟu tiriyumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: ammiṉ iṟuti - ammuc cāriyiyiṉ iṟutiyākiya makaravoṟṟu, kacatak kāli - kacatakkaḷ varumoḻiyāy varuṅ kālattu, taṉmey tirintu ṅañana ākum - taṉ vaṭivu tirintu  ṅañanakkaḷām eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam: puḷiyaṅkōṭu, cetiḷ, tōl eṉavarum.
itu puḷimarak kiḷavikku (eḻu. 244) eṉpataṉāṉ ammup peṟṟatu. kacatakkālit tiriyumeṉavē pakarattiṉkaṇ tiripiṉṟā yiṟṟu. meytirinteṉṉātu taṉmey eṉṟataṉāṉ, ammiṉ makaramēyaṉṟit tam nam num um eṉṉuñ cāriyi makaramun tirital koḷka. ellār taṅkiyum, ellā naṅkiyum, ellīr nuṅkiyum, vāṉavari villun tiṅkaḷum eṉa varum. (tuṟikēḻūraṉ, vaḷaṅkēḻūraṉ eṉakkeḻuveṉṉuñ cāriyiyatu ukarakkēṭum,ekaranīṭciyuñceyyuṇmuṭipu eṉṟu koḷka.)		(27)
130.	meṉmiyu miṭimiyum varūum kāli
					yiṉmi vēṇṭu meṉmaṉār pulavar.
iḵtu - ammīṟu iyalpukaṇattu muṉṉark keṭu meṉkiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: meṉmiyum iṭimiyum varūuṅkāli - meṉ kaṇamum iṭikkaṇamum varumoḻiyāy varuṅkālattu, 36iṉmi vēṇṭum eṉmaṉār pulavar - ammuc cāriyi iṟuti makaramiṉṟi muṭitali vēṇṭumeṉṟu kūṟuvar pulavar eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam:	puḷiyañeri nuṉi muri yāḻ vaṭṭu eṉa varum. uriyiṟkōṭaleṉpataṉāṟ, puḷiyaviliyeṉa uyir varuvaḻi īṟu keṭutalum, puḷiyiliyeṉa ammu muḻuvatuṅ keṭutaluṅ koḷka. puḷiyavili yeṉṟatu 37oṭṭutaṟ koḻukiya vaḻakku (eḻut. 132) aṉṟu. meṉ kaṇamum iṭik kaṇamum uyirk kaṇamun tammuḷokku mēṉum ammu muḻuvatuṅ keṭṭu varutalum uṭimiyiṉ uyiri eṭuttōtārāyiṉar. puḷiṅkāy eṉpatu marūu muṭipu.	(28)
131.		iṉṉeṉa varūum vēṟṟumi yurupiṟ
					kiṉṉeṉ cāriyi yiṉmi vēṇṭum.
iḵtu - iṉcāriyi aintāmurupiṉkaṇ muḻuvatuṅ keṭu meṉkiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: iṉṉeṉa varūum vēṟṟumi yurupiṟku - iṉ ṉeṉṟu colla varukiṉṟa vēṟṟumi yurupiṟku, iṉṉeṉ cāriyi iṉmi vēṇṭum - iṉ eṉṉuñ cāriyi tāṉ iṉṟi muṭitali virumpum āciriyaṉ eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam: viḷaviṉ palāviṉ kaṭuviṉ taḻuviṉ cēviṉ vuviṉ eṉa varum. ivaṟṟiṟku vīḻpaḻa meṉavum, nīṅkiṉā ṉeṉavuṅ koṭuttu muṭivu uṇarka. ūriṉīṅki ṉāṉ eṉa ēṉiyavaṟṟōṭum oṭṭuka. iṉi, avaṟṟuḷ iṉṉi ṉikaram (eḻu. 120) eṉṟataṉ piṉ itaṉi viyāta muṟiyaṉṟik kūṟṟiṉāṉ, iṉcāriyi keṭātu vaḻakkiṉkaṇṇuñ ceyyuṭkaṇṇum niṟṟal koḷka. pāmpiṉiṟ kaṭitu tēḷ, kaṟpiṉiṉ vaḻāa naṟpala vutavi, akaṭucērpu porunti yaḷaviṉi ṟiriyātu eṉa varum. iṉi, iṉmiyum vēṇṭu meṉṉum ummi tokku niṉṟatākki ataṉāṉ ivi kōṭalum oṉṟu.	(29)
132.		38peyarun toḻilum pirintoruṅ kicippa
						vēṟṟumi yurupu nilipeṟu vaḻiyun
						tōṟṟam vēṇṭāt tokutik kaṇṇu
						moṭṭutaṟ koḻukiya vaḻakkoṭu civaṇic
						coṟcitar maruṅkiṉ vaḻivantu viḷaṅkā
						tiṭiniṉ ṟiyaluñ cāriyi yiyaṟki
						yuṭimiyu miṉmiyu moṭuvayi ṉokkum.
itu, muṟkūṟiya cāriyikaḷellām puṇarmoḻiyuḷḷē varumeṉpatūum, ammoḻitām ivi yeṉpatūum, avi vārāta moḻikaḷum uḷaveṉpatūum kūṟukiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: peyarun toḻilum - peyarc collun toḻiṟ collum, pirintu icippa oruṅku icippa - peyarun toḻilu māyp pirinticippa, peyarum peyarumāyk kūṭiyicippa, vēṟṟumi yurupu nilipeṟu vaḻiyum - vēṟṟumi ceyyum urupukaḷ tokātu nilipeṟṟa iṭattum, tōṟṟam vēṇṭāt tokutik kaṇṇum - avvēṟṟumi yurupukaḷ tōṟṟutal vēṇṭātu tokka iṭattum, oṭṭutaṟku oḻukiya vaḻakkoṭu civaṇi - tām poruntutaṟkēṟpa naṭanta vaḻakkoṭu porunti, coṟcitar maruṅkiṉ - cāriyi peṟum puṇarmoḻi kaḷip pirittuk kāṇumiṭattu, cāriyi iyaṟki vaḻivantu viḷaṅkātu iṭi niṉṟu iyalum - accāriyiyiṉatu taṉmi accoṟkaḷiṉ piṉṉē vantu viḷaṅkātu naṭuvē niṉṟu naṭakkum, uṭimi yum iṉmiyum oṭu vayiṉ okkum - accāriyi uṇṭātalum illiyātalum oṭuvurupi ṉiṭattu ottuvarum eṉṟavāṟu.
oṭuviṟ kokkum eṉavē, ēṉiyavaṟṟiṟku ovvāvāyiṉa.
utāraṇam: viḷaviṉik kuṟittāṉ, kūḻiṟkuk kuṟṟēval ceyyum. ivi pirinticittu urupu nili peṟṟaṉa. aṉṉeṉ cāriyi (eḻu. 194) eṉpataṉek kuṟṟiya lukarat tiṟuti (eḻu. 195) eṉpataṉic cēra vittataṉāl, iṉ cāriyi varutal koḷka. ivviraṇṭurupuñ cāriyi niṟpap perumpāṉmiyun tokāveṉṟuṇarka. viḷaviṉik kuṟittavaṉ, kaṭicūttirattiṟkup poṉ - ivi oruṅkicippa urupu nili peṟṟaṉa. vāṉattiṉ vaḻukki, vāṉattu vaḻukki eṉac cāriyi peṟṟup pirinticittu aintāmurupu nilipeṟṟum, nili peṟātum vantaṉa. vāṉattiṉ vaḻukkal, vāṉattu vaḻukkal eṉac cāriyi peṟṟu oruṅku icittu aintām urupunili peṟṟum nilipeṟātum vantaṉa. vāṉattiṉ vaḻukkal, vāṉattu vaḻukkal ivi melleḻut tuṟaḻum (eḻu. 312) eṉṉuñ cūttirattu vaḻakkattāṉa eṉpataṉāṉ attuk koṭuttu makaraṅ keṭukka oruṅkicittaṉa. viḷaviṉatu kōṭu, viḷaviṉ kōṭu eṉa oruṅkicittuc cāriyi peṟṟavaḻi āṟaṉurupu tokātuntokkum niṉṟatu. itaṟkup pirinticitta liṉṟu. marattukkaṭ kaṭṭiṉāṉ, marattuk kaṭṭiṉāṉ eṉap pirinticittavaḻiyum, marattukkaṭ kuraṅku, marattuk kuraṅku eṉa oruṅkicittavaḻiyuñ cāriyi niṉṟavaḻi ēḻaṉurupu tokātun tokkum niṉṟatu. kiḷip peyarellām (eḻu. 307) eṉṟataṉuṭ koḷa eṉṟataṉāṉ ṇakāram ṭakāramāyiṟṟu. nilāveṉ kiḷavi yattoṭu civaṇum (eḻu. 228) eṉa vititta attu, nilākkatir nilāmuṟṟam eṉṟavaḻi peṟā tāyiṟṟu, aḵtu oṭṭutaṟ koḻukiya vaḻakku aṉmiyiṉ. nilāttuk koṇ ṭāṉ, nilāttuk koṇṭavaṉ eṉpaṉa urupu tokkuḻi iruvaḻiyum peṟṟaṉa. 39ellār tammiyum eṉac cāriyi īṟṟiṉ kaṇṇum varutaliṉ iṭiniṟṟal perumpāṉmi yeṉṟaṟku iyalum eṉṟār. pūviṉoṭu virinta kūntal, pūvoṭu virinta kūntal eṉa uṭimiyum iṉmiyum oṭuvayiṉ ottaṉa. iṉi iyaṟkiyeṉṟataṉāṉ, oṭu urupiṉkaṭ peṟṟum peṟāmiyum varutalaṉṟip, peṟṟē varutaluṅ koḷka. palavaṟṟoṭu eṉa varum.			(30)
133.		attē vaṟṟē yāyiru moḻimē
					loṟṟumey keṭuta ṟeṟṟeṉ ṟaṟṟē
					yavaṟṟumuṉ varūum valleḻuttu mikumē.
iḵtu - attu vaṟṟu eṉpaṉavaṟṟiṟku nilimoḻiyatu oṟṟuk kēṭum, varumoḻi vaṉkaṇattukkaṇ oṟṟuppēṟu mākiya ceyki kūṟukiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ:  attēvaṟṟē āyiru moḻimēl oṟṟu - attum vaṟṟumākiya avviraṇṭu cāriyimēl niṉṟa oṟṟu, mey keṭutal teṟṟeṉṟaṟṟu - taṉ vaṭivu keṭutal teḷiyap paṭṭatu; avaṟṟumuṉ varūum valleḻuttu mikumē - avviru cāriyi muṉṉum varum valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam: kalattukkuṟi, avaṟṟukkōṭu eṉa varum. attiṭi varūuṅ kalameṉ aḷavē (eḻu. 168) cuṭṭu mutal vakara miyu meyyum (eḻu. 183) eṉpaṉavaṟṟāṉ attum vaṟṟum peṟṟuvarum makara vakara īṟukaṭku 40īṟṟu valle ḻuttuviti iṉmiyiṉ, avaṟṟumuṉ varūum valleḻuttu mikumeṉṟu cāriyi valleḻuttu vitittār. valleḻuttu iṉṟit tirintu muṭivaṉa ṇakāramum, ṉakāramum, lakāramum, ḷakāramumām. makara īṟṟiṟku attum, vakara īṟṟiṟku vaṟṟum varumeṉpatu avvac cūttiraṅkaḷāṟ peṟṟām. vaṟṟē attē eṉṉāta muṟiyaṉṟik kūṟṟiṉāṉ, puḷḷi yīṟṟiṉ muṉṉar attiṉmici yoṟṟuk keṭātu niṟṟaluṅ koḷka.
utāraṇam: viṇṇattuk koṭkum, veyilattuc ceṉṟāṉ, iruḷattuk koṇṭāṉ eṉa varum. meyyeṉṟataṉāṉ, attiṉ akaram akara muṉṉarē yaṉṟip piṟa uyir muṉṉaruṅ keṭum orōviṭat teṉṟu koḷka. 41aṇṇāttēri, tiṭṭāttukkuḷam eṉa ākārattiṉ muṉṉarum varum attiṉ akaraṅ keṭṭatu. ivaṟṟi akara īṟākkiyum muṭippa.
iṉit teṟṟeṉṟaṟṟē eṉṟataṉāṉ, attiṉ akaran teṟṟeṉak keṭātu niṟkum iṭamuṅ koḷka. atavattukkaṇ, viḷavattukkaṇ eṉa varum.	(31)
134.		kāramuṅ karamuṅ kāṉoṭu civaṇi
					nērat tōṉṟu meḻuttiṉ cāriyi.
itu, moḻiccāriyi viṭṭu, eḻuttukkaṭku varuñ cāriyikaḷiṉ peyarum muṟiyun tokiyum uṇarttukiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: kāramuṅ karamuṅ kāṉoṭu civaṇi - kāramuṅ kara muṅ kāṉoṭu porunti, eḻuttiṉ cāriyi nērat tōṉṟum - eḻuttiṉkaṇ varuñ cāriyiyātaṟku ellā āciriyarāṉum uṭampaṭat tōṉṟum eṉṟavāṟu.
kāramuṅ karamum 42aṭuttuc colliyavaḻi iṉiticitta lāṉum, vaḻakkuppayiṟci yuṭimiyāṉum, vaṭaveḻuttiṟkum uriyavā talāṉuñ cērak kūṟiṉār. kāṉ attaṉmi yiṉmiyiṉāṟ piṉ vittār. nērattōṉṟumeṉavē nērattōṉṟātaṉavum uḷa vāyiṉa. avi, āṉam ēṉam ōṉam eṉka. ivi citinta vaḻakkēṉuṅ kaṭiya lākāvāyiṉa.	(32)
135.		avaṟṟuḷ
					karamuṅ kāṉu neṭṭeḻut tilavē.
iḵtu, avaṟṟuṭ cila cāriyi cilaveḻuttōṭu vārā veṉa eytiyatu vilakkiṟṟu.
itaṉ poruḷ: avaṟṟuḷ - muṟkūṟiyavaṟṟuḷ, karamuṅ kāṉum neṭ ṭeḻut tila - karamuṅ kāṉum neṭṭeḻuttiṟku varutalila eṉṟavāṟu.
eṉavē, neṭṭeḻuttiṟkuk kāram varumāyiṟṟu. ākāram īkāram eṉa oṭṭuka. aikāram aukārameṉac cūttiraṅkaḷuḷ varumāṟu kāṇka.	(33)
136.	varaṉmuṟi mūṉṟuṅ kuṟṟeḻut tuṭiya.
iḵtu aiyam akaṟṟiyatu; eṉṉi? neṭṭeḻuttiṟkuc cila cāriyi vilakkiṉāṟ pōlak kuṟṟeḻuttiṟkum vilakkaṟpāṭu uṇṭō veṉa aiyam nikaḻtaliṉ.
itaṉ poruḷ: varaṉmuṟi mūṉṟum - 43varalāṟṟu muṟimiyi yuṭiya mūṉṟu cāriyiyum, kuṟṟeḻuttuṭiya - kuṟṟeḻuttup peṟṟu varutaliyuṭiya e-ṟu.
akāram akaram maḵkāṉ eṉa oṭṭuka. vakāra miciyum akara ikaram vaḵkāṉ meykeṭa eṉavum piṟavuñ cūttiraṅ kaḷuṭ kāṇka. iḵkāṉ oḵkāṉ eṉpaṉa peruvaḻakkaṉṟu. varaṉmuṟi yeṉṟataṉāṉ, aḵkāṉ eṉpuḻi āytam peṟutal koḷka. itu kuṟiyataṉ muṉṉarāytap puḷḷi (eḻut. 38) eṉpataṉāṟ peṟātāyiṟṟu, moḻiyāy nillāmiyiṉ.	(34)
137.	aikāra aukāraṅ kāṉoṭun tōṉṟum.
iḵtu - avaṟṟuṭ karamuṅ kāṉum eṉpataṟkōr puṟaṉaṭi kūṟukiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: aikāra aukāraṅ kāṉoṭun tōṉṟum - neṭṭeḻuttukkaḷuḷ aikāra aukāraṅkaḷ muṉ vilakkappaṭṭa kāṉoṭun tōṉṟum eṉṟavāṟu.
aikāṉ, aukāṉ eṉa varum. ummi iṟantatu taḻīiyiṟṟu, kārattik karutaliṉ.	(35)
138.	44puḷḷi yīṟṟumuṉ ṉuyirtaṉit tiyalātu
					meyyoṭuñ civaṇu mavviyal keṭuttē.
itu, puḷḷi yīṟṟumuṉ uyirmutaṉmoḻi vanta kālattup puṇarum muṟimi kūṟukiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: puḷḷi yīṟṟumuṉ uyir taṉittu iyalātu - puḷḷi yīṟṟuc coṉmuṉṉar vanta uyirmutaṉ moḻiyiṉ uyir taṉittu naṭavātu, meyyoṭuñ civaṇum - appuḷḷiyoṭum kūṭum, av viyal keṭuttu - tāṉ taṉittu niṉṟa avviyalpiṉik keṭuttu eṉṟavāṟu.
eṉavē, nīrōṭu kūṭiya pālpōla niṉṟateṉṟu oṟṟumi kūṟiṉār, īṇṭu. itaṉāṉē uyirmeyyeṉap peyar peṟṟatu.
utāraṇam:	pālaritu, pālāḻi, ālili, poruḷīṭṭam, vāṉu laku, vāṉūṭu, vēleṟintāṉ, vēlēṟṟāṉ, poru ḷiyam, poruḷoṉṟu, nāṇōṭiṟṟu, col luviyam eṉa varum.
oṉṟeṉa muṭittaleṉpataṉāṉ iyalpallāta puḷḷi muṉṉar uyirvantālum ivviti koḷka.  ataṉi ataṉoṭu nāṭuri eṉa varum. ivaṟṟic cuṭṭumuta lukara maṉṉoṭu (eḻut. 176), urivaru kāli nāḻik kiḷavi (eḻu. 240) eṉpaṉavaṟṟāṉ muṭikka. puḷḷiyīṟṟu muṉṉumeṉa ummiyi māṟṟi eccavummi yākkik kuṟṟiyalukarattiṉ muṉṉarum eṉa avviti koḷka. eṉavē, kuṟṟiyalukaramu maṟṟu (eḻu. 105) eṉṟataṉōṭum poruntiṟṟām. nākaritu, varakaritu eṉavarum.	(36)
139.	45meyyuyir nīṅkiṟ ṟaṉṉuru vākum.
iḵtu - uyirmey, puṇarccikkaṇ uyir nīṅkiyavaḻip paṭuva toru viti kūṟukiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: mey uyir nīṅkiṉ - mey taṉṉōṭu kūṭi niṉṟa uyir puṇarcciyiṭattup pirintu vēṟu niṉṟatāyiṉ, taṉ uruvākum - tāṉ muṉṉarp peṟṟu niṉṟa puḷḷivaṭivu peṟum eṉṟavāṟu.
āl ili, ataṉ ai eṉa varum.
uyir eṉṉa vaṭiviṟṟeṉṟu āciriyar kūṟāmiyiṉ, uyirk kaṇ ārāycci yiṉṟu.
iṉi, ekara okaraṅkaḷip puḷḷiyāṉ vēṟṟumi ceytaliṉ, toṉṟu toṭṭu vaḻaṅkiṉa vaṭivuṭiya veṉṟu kōṭalumām. puṇarcciyuḷ uyirmeyyiṉip pirippārāta liṉ, itu kūṟākkāṟ kuṉṟak kūṟalāmeṉṟu uṇarka.	(37)
140.		ellā moḻikku muyirvaru vaḻiyē
					yuṭampaṭu meyyi ṉuruvukoḷal variyār.
iḵtu - uyirīṟum uyirmutaṉmoḻiyum puṇarumvaḻi nikaḻva toru karuvi kūṟukiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: ellā moḻikkum - nilimoḻiyum varumoḻiyu māyp puṇarum evvaki moḻikkum, uyir varuvaḻi - uyir mutaṉ moḻi varumiṭattu, uṭampaṭu meyyiṉ uruvu koḷal variyār - uṭampaṭu meyyiṉatu vaṭivi uyirīṟu kōṭali nīkkār, koḷvar āciriyar eṉṟavāṟu.
avi yakaramum vakaramum eṉpatu mutaṉūl paṟṟik kōṭum;
uṭampaṭu meyyē yakāra vakāra
muyirmutaṉ moḻivarūuṅ kāli yāṉa
eṉavum,
iṟutiyu mutalu muyirnili variṉē
yuṟumeṉa moḻipa vuṭampaṭu meyyē
eṉavuṅ kūṟiṉārākaliṉ. uyirkaḷuḷ ikara īkāra aikāra īṟu, yakara uṭampaṭumey koḷḷum. ēkāram, yakāramum vakāramuṅ koḷḷum. allaṉavellām vakara uṭampaṭumeyyē koḷḷu meṉṟu uṇarka.
utāraṇam: 	kiḷiyaḻakitu, kurīiyōppuvāḷ variyara makaḷir eṉavum; viḷavaḻakitu, palāvaḻakitu, pūvaḻakitu, kōvaḻakitu, kuvaṭintatu eṉavum oṭṭuka. ēē yivaḷorutti pēṭiyō veṉṟār (cīvaka. 612) ēvāṭal kāṇka eṉa ēkārat tiṟku iraṇṭum vantaṉa.
oṉṟeṉa muṭittaleṉpataṉāṉ, 46vikārappaṭṭa moḻikkaṇṇum uṭampaṭumey koḷka. maravaṭi, āyirutiṇi eṉavarum.  variyāreṉṟataṉāṉ uṭampaṭumey kōṭal orutali yaṉṟu. kiḷi aritu, mūṅkā illi eṉavum varum. oṉṟeṉa muṭittaleṉpataṉāṉ, viṇvattuk koṭ kum eṉac ciṟupāṉmi puḷḷiyīṟṟiṉum varum. celvuḻi uṇpuḻi eṉpaṉa viṉit toki yeṉa maṟukka.	(38)
141.		eḻuttō raṉṉa poruṭeri puṇarcci
					yiciyiṟ ṟirita ṉiliiya paṇpē.
iḵtu - eḻuttukkaḷ oṉṟu palavāmeṉa eytātatu teytuvikkiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ:  eḻuttōraṉṉa poruḷ teri 
puṇarcci - eḻuttu oru taṉmittāṉa poruḷ viḷaṅka niṟkum puṇarmoḻikaḷ, iciyiṟ ṟirital niliiya paṇpu - eṭuttal paṭuttal nalita leṉ kiṉṟa ōci vēṟṟumiyāṟ puṇarcci vēṟupaṭutal nilipeṟṟa kuṇam e-ṟu.
utāraṇam: cempoṉpatiṉṟoṭi, 47cemparutti, kuṟumparampu, nākaṉṟēvaṉpōttu, tāmarikkaṇiyār, kuṉṟē ṟāmā eṉa iciyiṟ ṟirintaṉa.	(39)
142.		avitām
					muṉṉap poruḷa puṇarcci vāyi
					ṉiṉṉa veṉṉu meḻuttukkaṭa ṉilavē.
itu, mēlataṟkōr puṟaṉaṭi kūṟukiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: avitām - palaporuṭkup potu veṉṟa puṇarmoḻikaḷtām, muṉṉapporuḷa - kuṟippāṉ uṇarum poruṇmi yiṉiyuṭiya, puṇarccivāyiṉ iṉṉa veṉṉum eḻuttukkaṭaṉ ila - puṇarcciyiṭattu ittaṉmiyaveṉṉum eḻuttu muṟimiyi uṭiyavalla eṉṟavāṟu.
cempoṉpatiṉṟoṭi eṉpuḻip poṉṉārāycci uḷ vaḻip poṉṉeṉavuñ, cempārāycci uḷvaḻic cempeṉa vuṅ kuṟippāṉ uṇarappaṭṭatu. iciyiṉ tiritaleṉṟatu oli yeḻuttiṟkeṉavum, eḻuttuk kaṭaṉila eṉṟatu varivaṭiviṟ keṉavuṅ koḷka.	(40)
puṇariyal muṟṟiṟṟu.
kaṇēcayyar aṭikkuṟippukaḷ:
1.	ceyki otteṉṟatu piṉvarum tokimarapiṉiyum uyir mayaṅkiyaṉ mutaliya mūṉṟiyalkaḷiyum, nūṉmarapu 1-m cūttira virivuri nōkki yaṟika. īṇṭu - ip puṇariyalil.
2.	īṟu - moḻikku īṟṟil varumeḻuttu. mutal - mutalil varumeḻuttu.
3.	mūvakimoḻi - ōreḻuttorumoḻi, īreḻuttorumoḻi, iraṇṭiṟanticikkum toṭarmoḻi. 
4.	moḻimutaṟ kuṟṟiyalukaram - nunti.
5.	mutaṟ cūttirattilē `iṟutiyu mutalu meyyē yuyireṉ ṟāyīriyala' eṉṟamiyāṉ moḻikku mey mutalāyum varum īṟāyum varum eṉpatu peṟappaṭutaliṉ, avviru moḻikaḷuḷ īṟṟil niṟkum mey eppaṭi niṟkumeṉa iccūttirattāl vitikkiṉṟār. īṟṟil varum meykaḷ puḷḷi peṟṟu niṟkumeṉavē mutalil niṟkum meykaḷ uyirōṭukūṭip puḷḷi peṟātu niṟkumeṉpatu peṟappaṭum. īṟṟil meykaḷ puḷḷipeṟṟu niṟkumeṉṟamiyāṉē. uyir varuṅkāl taṉmēlēṟi muṭiyavum iṭaṅkoṭukkumeṉpatum, ēṉiya meyvaruṅkāl tirintum iyalpāyum muṭiyumeṉpatum peṟappaṭu meṉpatum urikārar karuttu. 
6.	iccūttirattiṟkuk kuṟṟiyalukaramum puḷḷipeṟātu uyirēṟa iṭaṅ koṭukkumeṉṟu iḷampūraṇar poruḷ kūṟiṉar. nacciṉārk kiṉiyarum aṅṅaṉamē kūṟiṉār. aṟṟu eṉpatu mutaṟ cūttirattiṟ kūṟiya puḷḷi peṟutaliyē cuṭṭumaṉṟi atiṟ kūṟāta uyirēṟa iṭaṅkoṭukku meṉpatic cuṭṭātu; ātaliṉ, avvurikaḷ māṭṭēṟṟilakkaṇattōṭu poruntumō eṉpatu ārāyattakkatu. muṉṉum, `meyyi ṉiyaṟki puḷḷiyoṭu niliyal' eṉa meykaḷ puḷḷi peṟutali vititta āciriyar, am meykaḷpōlavē ekara okaramum puḷḷipeṟu meṉap puḷḷipeṟutalōṭu māṭṭi, `ekara okarat tiyaṟkiyu maṟṟē' eṉak kūṟutali ṉāciriyar kūṟum māṭṭiṉilakkaṇam iḵteṉpatu uṇarattakkatu. pērāciriyarkkum civañāṉa muṉivarkkum caṅka yāppeṉṉum nūluṭiyārkkum kuṟṟiya lukaramum puḷḷipeṟumeṉpatu karuttātal nūṉmarapu 2-m cūttirak kuṟippil uṇarttappaṭṭatu. āṅkuk kāṇka. 
7.	ic cūttirattu uyirīṟṟiyaṟṟē eṉa vitittamiyāṉum itaṟku mutaṟ cūttirattuk kuṟṟiyalukaramum meyyīṟupōlap puḷḷipeṟumeṉṟu vitittāreṉṟē tuṇiyappaṭutal kāṇka.
8.	muṉṉar eṉṟatu, nūṉmarapu 18ām cūttiratti.
9.	peyarppeyar - peyarccollāl vanta peyar. oṭṭup peyar - viṉi yālaṇiyum peyar.
10.	kaṇkūṭāka eṉṟatu - varivaṭivinōkki.
11.	itu eṉṟatu - muyaṟkōṭṭi. muyaṟkōṭu iṉṟu eṉṟāṟ puṇarkkap paṭum. eṉṉi? poruḷiyitaliṉ.
12. 	vikaṟpikkap patiṉāṟu varumāṟu: meypiṟitātal, mikutal, kuṉṟal, iyalpātal eṉṉu nāṉkiyum, peyaroṭu peyariyum, peyaroṭu toḻiliyum, toḻiloṭu peyariyum, toḻiloṭu toḻiliyum puṇarppataṉōṭu uṟaḻa naṉṉāṉku patiṉāṟātal kāṇka. 
13.	patiṉāyirattoṉṟu. itil patiṉāyiram eṉpuḻi āyiram patteṉṉum aṭiyoṭu tōṉṟiṟṟu; itupaṇputtoki. paṉṉiraṇṭu ki. itil iraṇṭu patteṉṉum aṭiyoṭu vantatu. paṉṉiraṇṭu - pattum iraṇṭum eṉa ummittoki. itupaṟṟiyē aṭiyāvaṉa irupeya roṭṭup paṇputtokiyum ummittokiyum eṉṟār.
14.	vēṟṟumittokiyum uvamit tokiyum muṭiya eṉṟatu - piḷantu muṭivupeṟa eṉṟapaṭi. eṉṟatu - kuṉṟakkūki, pulippāyttuḷ eṉpuḻi kuṉṟam - kūki eṉavum. puli - pāyttuḷ eṉavum piḷantu puṇarcci peṟa. viṉittokiyum paṇput tokiyum piḷantu muṭivupeṟātu oru collāyē niṟṟaliṉ avi aṭiyeṉappaṭum. aṉmoḻit tokiyum taṉakku vēṟōr muṭipiṉmiyiṉ oru collēyām eṉṟatu. ēṉiya tokikaḷiṉ īṟṟiṟ piṟattaliṉ. avaṟṟiṉ muṭipē taṉakku muṭipaṉṟi vēṟōr muṭipiṉmiyiṉ oru collēyām. eṉavētāṉ piḷantu niṉṟu muṭivu peṟātu. peṟiṉ aṉmoḻi poruḷuṇarttātu eṉṟapaṭi.
15.	maruviṉ tokutiyum mayaṅkiyaṉmoḻiyum eṉap pirittup poruḷ kūṟiṉum, nacciṉārkkiṉiyar karuttiṉpaṭi poruḷ kūṟalām. avar iṉṟokutiyeṉap pirittup poruḷ kōṭal ciṟappiṉṟu. uriyā ciriyar karuttē īṇṭup poruttamām. ēṉeṉiṉ? āciriyar ilak kaṇattōṭu poruntiya marūuc coṟkaḷip pirittup puṇarttaliṉ.
16.	yāppu - vali.
17.	āṟupayaṉili eṉṟatu: `poruṇmi cuṭṭal - viyaṅkoḷavarutal - viṉinili yurittal - viṉāviṟkēṟṟal - paṇpu koḷa varutal peyarkoḷa varutal eṉṟaṉṟi yaṉittum peyarp payaṉiliyē' eṉṉuñ collatikārac cūttirattāṟ kūṟiya āṟu payaṉiliyiyum. avaṟṟiṟku utāraṇam muṟiyē āvuṇṭu. āvāḻka. āvantatu, āyātu, ākaritu, āpala eṉpaṉavām. paṇputtoki virivuḻi aimpālīṟāka viriyum. viṉittoki virivuḻic ceyta ceyyum eṉṉum peyarecca vīṟāka viriyum. ittokikaḷ pirittup puṇarkkap paṭā oru collāy niṟkum. iṅṅaṉam pirittup puṇarkkappaṭāveṉa āciriyar kūṟuvatip palaviṭattuṅ kūṟukiṉṟa nacciṉārkkiṉiyar īṇṭup paṇputtokiyiyum viṉit tokiyiyum virintu niṉṟavaḻip puṇarnta puṇarcciyumeṉak kūṟiyatu poruntumō eṉpatu ārāyattakkatu.  
18.	poruḷ nōkki āṇṭu eṭṭu eṉṟār. varumoḻiyāy niṉṟu puṇarum urupunōkki īṇṭu āṟu eṉṟār eṉka.
19.	eḻuvāy poruḷ māttiramuṇartti niṉṟum, viḷi etirmukamākki niṉṟum eṉa niraṉiṟiyākak koḷka.
20.	urupiyaliṟ kūṟupaveṉṟatu - urupiyal 16-m cūttirattiṟ kūṟupa eṉṟa paṭi. itil meyyeṉpataṉāṟ piṟavayiṉ meyyum keṭukka eṉak kūṟutal kāṇka.
21.	aṅkaṇ iṅkaṇ uṅkaṇ eṉpaṉa āṅkaṇ īṅkaṇ ūṅkaṇ eṉa nīṇṭu niṉṟaṉa eṉṟapaṭi. uyirē nilimoḻiyātaliṉ oṟṟu iṉṟu eṉṟār. 
22.	niṉava eṉava eṉpuḻi niṉ eṉ eṉpaṉa akara urupu varuṅkāl iṭiyil ōrakaram peṟṟu niṉa eṉa eṉṟu niṉṟu piṉ akara vurupōṭu cērntu niṉava eṉava eṉa niṟṟal kāṇka. atupaṟṟiyē `īṟākupuḷḷi akaramoṭu niliyum' eṉṟār eṉpatu karuttu.
23.	mutaṉiliyeṉṟatu - pakutiyi. pakutikkaṉṟu viṉiccoṟkē kūṟiṉāreṉṟalē poruttamām. ēṉeṉiṉ? ilakkaṇam kūṟum vaḻik kūṟātoḻiyiṉ aiyamvarumeṉṟu. viṉiyiyalil `viṉiyeṉap paṭuvatu vēṟṟumi koḷḷātu' eṉṟu kūṟiṉār; uyttuṇarntiṭarp paṭāmal īṇṭuk kūṟiṉāreṉṟu kōṭalē poruttamātaliṉ. 
24.	koṟṟaṉ cevinallaṉ eṉpuḻi uyartiṇiyeṉpatum koṟṟaṉ cevi nīṇṭatu eṉpuḻi aḵṟiṇi eṉpatum viḷaṅkum.
25.	puṇariyaṉiliyiṭip poruṇilik kutavivantaṉa eṉpatu. avaṟṟuṭ puṇariyaṉiliyiṭip poruṇilik kutanavum, eṉṉum (col - 250) cūttirak karutti nōkki niṉṟatu. peyarkūṟi "avaṟṟu vaḻimaruṅkiṟ cāriyi varumē" eṉavē muṉpeyarvaḻi vēṟṟumi varumeṉṟā rēṉum cāriyi varuṅkāl cāriyikkup piṉṉēyē vēṟṟumiyurupu varumeṉpatu iccūttirattu "avaṟṟuvaḻi" eṉpataṉāṟ peṟappaṭṭatu.
26.	kuliyalaṅkāntaḷ eṉpuḻi alaṅkukāntaḷ eṉpatu alaṅkāntaḷ eṉa niṉṟatātaliṉ amcāriyiyeṉṟu koḷḷaṟka eṉpatu karuttu.
27.	potuvakiyāṉōtiyavaḻit tāṉē cēṟal eṉṟatu. iccol ic cāriyi peṟumeṉa vitiyātu potuvākac cāriyip pēṟu kūṟiya viṭattu tāṉē cāriyiyākac cellutal. 
28.	āṭikku eṉpuḻi ikku cāriyi. ivviyal 24-m cūttiram nōkkiyaṟika.
29.	ākāravīṟāyiṉ māvuṅ koḷḷappaṭum. iṅkē āviṉiṟuti eṉṟu āviyē kūṟiṉamiyiṉ mā ilēciṉāṟ koḷḷappaṭṭatu. itaṉi oppak kūṟal eṉṉum uttiyāl āvum māvum eṉpar pērāciriyar. āṉ eṉpatil ṉakaram cāriyi yeṉpar naṉṉūlār.
30.	vēṇṭum ceyki eṉṟatu, meyyil uyirēṟṟi muṭippati. patiṟṟakal eṉpatil iṟṟu cāriyiyeṉpar naṉṉūlār.
31.	piṟavaḻi - aḷavāku moḻi mutalallāta vaḻi. 
32.	evaṉ eṉpatu - eṭuttalōciyāṟ kūṟiṉ viṉāviṉik kuṟippākum. paṭuttalōciyāṟkūṟiṉ viṉāppeyarām eṉṟapaṭi. vaṟṟuccāriyiyi aṟṟuccāriyiyeṉpar naṉṉūlār.
33.	ñāpakam kūṟal eṉṉum uttiyāvatu - cūttirañ ceyyuṅkāṟ cilavaki eḻuttiṉālākiyatākavum poruṇaṉi viḷaṅkavuñ ceyyātu. aritum peritumāka nalintu ceytu ataṉāṉē vēṟu pala poruḷu ṇarttal. ñāpakattāṟ koḷka eṉa oṭṭuka. 
34.	akkiṉiṟuti meymmiciyoṭum eṉpataṟku iṟutimey (ukaram ēṟiniṉṟa mey) taṉmēluḷḷa kakaravoṟṟōṭum keṭum eṉṟu poruḷ koḷḷal ciṟappām. itaṟku mey eḻuvāyāy niṟṟaliṉ meymmiciyoṭuṅ keṭum eṉṟu pāṭamiruttal vēṇṭum. kakaravoṟṟi lēṟi niṉṟa kuṟṟiyalukaramum keṭum eṉṟavāṟu.
35.	kuṉṟakkūki eṉpuḻi. akku meymmiciyoṭuṅ keṭātu, akki luḷḷa kakaravoṟṟu niṟkumeṉṟāl eṉṉiyeṉiṉ? aṅṅaṉam koḷḷiṉ kacatapa mutaṉmoḻi varuṅkāl kakarattukkaṉṟi ēṉiya vaṟṟiṟkup poruntāmiyiṉ valliṉam mikumeṉṟalē porutta māmeṉka. 
36.	iṉmi vēṇṭum eṉṟatu - iṟuti keṭutal vēṇṭum eṉṟapaṭi.
37.	oṭṭutaṟ koḻukiya vaḻakkaṉṟu - cāriyikaḷ varutaṟkuriya moḻi vaḻakkaṉṟu. aṭutta 30-m cūttiram pārkka.
38.	iccūttirattiṟku uriyāciriyarum nacciṉārkkiṉiyarum koṇṭa poruḷi maṟuttuc cēṉāvariyar "peyarum peyarum, peyarum toḻilum pirinticippavum oruṅkicippavum muṟiyē vēṟṟumi urupu nili peṟṟaviṭattum maṟintuniṟkum tokikkaṇṇum" eṉṟu poruḷ kūṟuvar. avar karuttu "peyarum peyarum, peyarum toḻilum vēṟṟumiyuruyu nilipeṟṟavaḻi (virintavaḻi) pirin ticikkumeṉpatum, tokkavaḻi oruṅkicikku meṉpatumākum. ituvē naṉṉūlārkkuṅ karuttātal potuviyaliṟ kūṟiya toki nilic cūttiram nōkki uṇarka."
39.	ellār tammiyum eṉpuḻi - ummi muṟṟummi eṉpar naṉṉūlār.
40.	īṟṟu valleḻuttu vitiyillātatu eṉṟatu kalam. av eṉṉum nili moḻikaḷiṉ īṟṟeḻuttukkaḷ oṟṟātaliṉālē valliṉam mikā; uyirāyiṉ mikum. ātaliṉ vitiyilli eṉṟapaṭi.
41.	aṇṇā, tiṭṭā eṉpaṉa ūrpōlum.
42.	eṭuttuc collal - eḻuppic collal; muyaṟci.
43.	varalāṟṟu muṟimi - vaḻaṅkivanta muṟimi.
44.	iccūttirattu `avviyalkeṭuttu' eṉpataṟkut tāṉ taṉittu niṟkumiyalpiṉik keṭuttu eṉṟu poruḷ koḷḷātu. puḷḷiyīṟāka niṟkumavviyalpiṉikkeṭuttu eṉṟu meykkum. kuṟṟiyalukara vīṟākaniṟkumavviyalpiṉik keṭuttu eṉṟu kuṟṟiyalukara vīṟṟiṟku mēṟpap poruḷkoḷḷiṉ, naṉṉūlār kuṟṟiyalukaraṅ keṭṭup puṇarumeṉak kūṟiyatu tolkāppiyarkkuṅ karuttākum. keṭṭup puṇarumeṉpatē tolkāppiyarkkuṅ karuttātal "yakaram varum vaḻi ikaraṅ kuṟuku - mukarak kiḷavi tuvarat tōṉṟātu" eṉṉuñ cūttiratti uṟṟunōkkiṉ aṟiyappaṭum. iṉi, "puḷḷiyīṟṟiṉmuṉ ṉuyir taṉittiyalātu" eṉpuḻi. īṟṟum eṉa ummiyi virittuk kuṟṟiyalukara vīṟṟiṟku valintu viti koḷvatiṉum "puḷḷiyīṟu" eṉpati irumuṟi ōti iru toṭarākak koṇṭu meyyīṟu eṉṟum, puḷḷi peṟuṅ kuṟṟiyalukara vīṟeṉṟum poruḷ koḷḷalāmeṉpatu ematu karuttu. aṅṅaṉam koḷḷiṉ, "kuṟṟiyalukaramu maṟṟeṉamoḻipa" eṉa āciriyar ōtiya vitikkumōr payaṉuṇṭām. iṭiyiyal "vēṟṟumi poruḷvayi ṉurupā kunavum eṉpatiyum. vēṟṟumi  yiyal aintām vēṟṟumic cūttirattu itaṉiṉiṟṟitu" eṉpatiyum iru toṭarāka vittuc cēṉāvariyarum poruḷ kūṟal kāṇka. aṉṟi oru cūttirattiṟku iru poruḷuṅ koḷvar. avvāṟē kōṭalumām. itupōlvaṉavaṟṟi oppak kūṟal eṉpar pērāciriyar. utti pērāciriyaruri pārkka. ic cūttirattiṟku yāṉ kūṟiya putuk karutti aṅkīkarittut tāmeḻutiya eḻuttatikārak kuṟippuriyil. ṭākṭar P.S. cuppiramaṇiya cātirikaḷum ic cūttirattukkuk kuṟippuri eḻutiyuḷḷār. annūl nōkkiyaṟika. (annūl tiruppaṉantāḷ maṭa veḷiyīṭu.) 
45.	meyyuyir nīṅkiṟ ṟaṉṉuru vākum eṉṟu meyyīṟṟiṟkē kūṟiṉārā yiṉum, kuṟṟiyalukarattiṟkum oṉṟeṉa muṭittalāṟ koḷḷappaṭum.
46.	vikārappaṭṭamoḻi eṉṟatu vitiyīṟṟi. celvuḻi uṇpuḻi eṉpavaṟṟi viṉittokiyeṉa maṟukkaveṉṟu ivar kūṟaliṉ. celluḻi uṇṇuḻi eṉpaṉa pirittup puṇarkkappaṭāta marūu moḻikaḷāy muṟiyē vakaramum pakaramum peṟṟu ivvāṟu niṉṟaṉa veṉpatu karuttup pōlum. 
47.	cempu + arutti. arutti - viruppam. cem + parutti eṉavum piriyum. tāmari + kaṇiyār. kaṇṇi - māli. tām + arikku + aṇiyār. tā + marikku + aṇṇiyār. tā - tāval. ivvutāraṇam makāliṅkiyar patippililli. 

toki marapu
uyiriyum meyyiyum iṟutiyākavuṭiya coṟkaḷip piṉvarum iyalkaḷil īṟukaḷtōṟum taṉittaṉiyē virittōti muṭikkak karutiya āciriyar, pala vīṟukaḷukkum potuvāṉa vitikaḷi ivviyalil ovvōr cūttiraṅkaḷāl tokuttu muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟār. ataṉāl ivviyal tokimarapu eṉṉum peyarttāyiṟṟu. nilimoḻi varumoḻip puṇarccikaṭpaṭum ilakkaṇaṅkaḷāyt toṉṟu toṭṭu varum ilakkaṇa marapukaḷit tokuttuṇarttutaliṉ tokimara peṉap paṭṭatu eṉiṉumamiyum. nilimoḻiyum varumoḻiyum mēl puṇariyaliṟ kūṟiya karuvikaḷāl tokkuppuṇarum ceyki kūṟuvatu ivviyalātaliṉ itu puṇariyalōṭu iyipuṭit tāyiṟṟu.
