ivviyal muppatu cūttiraṅkaḷiyuṭiyatu. itaṉkaṇ alvaḻiyum vēṟṟumiyumākiya iruvaḻiyilum kacatapa eṉṉum valleḻuttu mutaṉmoḻi varuṅkāl mikutaṟkuriya melleḻuttukkaḷ muṟiyē ṅa ña na ma eṉpaṉavām eṉṉum varumoḻik karuviyum, irupattunāṉkīṟṟiṉ muṉṉum vaṉkaṇa moḻinta kaṇaṅkaḷukku iruvaḻiyum varumoḻimuṭipum, ṇakara ṉakara vīṟṟuc coṟkaḷukku iruvaḻiyum nilimoḻi muṭipum, lakara, ṉakara vīṟukaḷiṉ muṉṉum ṇakara ḷakara vīṟukaḷiṉ muṉṉum varum takara nakaraṅkaḷ muṟiyē ṟakara ṉakaraṅkaḷākavum ṭakara ṇakaraṅkaḷākavum tiriyu meṉṉum varumoḻik karuviyum, uyiriyum meyyiyum iṟutiyākavuṭiya muṉṉili viṉic coṟkaḷ valliṉa mutaṉmoḻi varuṅkāl iyalpum uṟaḻcciyu mākiya irunilimiyiyuṭiyaṉa veṉpatum, aukārattiyum ñanamava eṉṉum meykaḷiyum kuṟṟiyalukarattiyum īṟākavuṭiya muṉṉili viṉiccoṟkaḷ muṟkūṟiya muṭipiṟku muṟṟum poruntuvaṉa alla eṉpatum, uyiriyum meyyiyum īṟākavuṭiya uyar tiṇip peyarkaḷ nāṉkukaṇattum iruvaḻiyum iyalpāy muṭiyumeṉpatum, avaṟṟuḷ ikaravīṟṟu uyartiṇip peyar tirintu muṭiyumiṭaṉumuṇ ṭeṉpatum, aḵṟiṇi viravu peyar iyalpāy muṭivaṉavumuḷa veṉpatum, mūṉṟām vēṟṟumip puṇarcciyilum iraṇṭām vēṟṟumip puṇarcciyilum uḷavākun tiripukaḷum, ikara aikāravīṟṟup peyarkaḷukku alvaḻi muṭipum, ikara aikāravīṟṟuḷ ēḻām vēṟṟumiyiṭap poruḷuṇara niṉṟa iṭiccol muṭipum, neṭṭeḻuttiṉ piṉṉiṉṟa meyyīṟu keṭutal kuṟṟeḻuttiṉ piṉṉiṉṟa iṟutimey iraṭṭittal ākiya nilimoḻikkaru viyum, ivaṟṟiṟku urupiyali nōkkiyatōr, varumoḻik karuviyum, ukaramoṭu puṇarum meyyīṟṟuc coṟkaḷ yakaramum uyirum varumoḻiyāy variṉ avvukaram peṟātu iyalpām eṉṉum varumoḻic ceykiyum aḷavuppeyarum niṟippeyarum eṇṇuppeyarum tammiṟ puṇarumāṟum, aḷavuppeyarkkum niṟippeyarkkum mutalātaṟkuriya veḻuttukkaḷ ka ca ta pa na ma va a u eṉṉumiv voṉpatumē yeṉṉam variyaṟiyum, mēlē kūṟappaṭṭaṉa vaṟṟiṟkup puṟaṉaṭiyum, yāvar eṉpatu yār eṉavum yātu eṉpatu yāvatu eṉavum varum marūu muṭipum ivviyalil uṇarttappaṭṭaṉa.
- ka. veḷḷivāraṇaṉār 
nūlvarici 10, pak. 174-176

aintāvatu
tokimarapu
143.		kacatapa mutaliya moḻimēṟ ṟōṉṟu
					melleḻut tiyaṟki colliya muṟiyāṉ
					ṅañanama veṉṉu moṟṟā kummē
					yaṉṉa marapiṉ moḻivayi ṉāṉa
eṉpatu cūttiram.
uyirīṟum puḷḷiyīṟum mēli akattōttiṉuḷ muṭikkum vaḻi īṟukaṭōṟum virittu muṭippaṉavaṟṟi īṇṭu orōvōr cūttiraṅkaḷāṟ ṟokuttu muṭipu kūṟiṉamiyiṉ, ivvōttut tokimarapeṉṉum peyarttāyiṟṟu. mēl mūvaki moḻiyum nālvakiyāṟ puṇarvuḻi, mūṉṟu tiripum ōriyalpum eyti, vēṟṟumi alvaḻi yeṉa irupakutiyavāki, eḻuttuñ cāriyiyum mikkup puṇarumāṟu ituveṉṟu uṇartti, avitām virinta cūttirap poruḷavaṉṟiyun tokkup puṇarumāṟu kūṟutaliṉ, ivvōttup puṇariyalōṭu iyipuṭit tāyiṟṟu. ittaliccūttiram uyirmayaṅkiyali yum, puḷḷimayaṅkiya liyum nōkkiyatōr varumoḻik karuvi kūṟukiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: 1kacatapa mutaliya moḻimēṟṟōṉṟum iyaṟki melleḻuttu - uyirīṟum puḷḷiyīṟum muṉṉar niṟpak  ka - ca - ta- pa -kkaḷi mutalākavuṭiya moḻikaḷ vantāl avaṟṟiṟku mēlē tōṉṟi niṟkum iyalpākiya melleḻuttukkaḷ, yāviyeṉiṉ colliya muṟiyāṉ ṅañanama eṉṉum oṟṟākum - neṭuṅkaṇakkiṟ poruntak kūṟiya muṟiyāṉē kacatapakkaḷukku ṅañanama veṉṉum oṟṟukkaḷ niraṉiṟi vakiyāṉām: aṉṉa marapiṉ moḻivayiṉāṉa - at taṉmittākiya muṟimiyiṉi yuṭiya moḻikaḷiṭattu eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam: viḷaṅkōṭu cetiḷ tōl pū eṉa varum.
itu, marappeyarkkiḷavi (eḻu. 217) eṉpataṉāṉ mel leḻuttup peṟṟatu. maraṅ kuṟitu ciṟitu tītu peritu eṉa alvaḻik kaṭ ṭiriyumāṟu alvaḻiyellām (eḻu. 314) eṉpataṉāṟ peṟu tumēṉum, īṇṭut tōṉṟumeṉṟataṉāl, nilimoḻikkaṭ ṭōṉṟi niṉṟa oṟṟuttirital koḷka. aṉṉa marapiṉ moḻi yaṉmiyiṉ, 2viḷakkuṟumi viḷak kuṟittāṉ eṉpuḻi melleḻuttup peṟāvāyiṉa; ivi ēḻā vatum iraṇṭāvatun tiritaliṉ. iṅṅaṉam eḻuttup peṟuvaṉavun tirivaṉavumellām varumoḻiyēpaṟṟi varumeṉṟu uṇarka.	(1)
144.		ñanama yavaveṉu mutalāku moḻiyu
					muyirmuta lākiya moḻiyu muḷappaṭa
					vaṉṟi yaṉittu mellā vaḻiyu
					niṉṟa coṉmu ṉiyalpā kummē.
itu, muṟkūṟiya nālvakip puṇarcciyuḷ iyalpu puṇaruṅ kāl, ikkūṟiya patiṉēḻeḻuttum varumoḻiyāy vanta iṭattu, irupattu nāṉkīṟṟiṉ muṉṉarum vēṟṟumiyilum alvaḻiyilum varumoḻi iyalpāy muṭikaveṉkiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: ñanamayava eṉum mutalāku moḻiyum - ña - na - ma - ya - va eṉṟu collappaṭum eḻuttukkaḷ mutalāy niṟkuñ coṟkaḷum, uyir mutalākiya moḻiyum uḷappaṭa  -  uyireḻuttu mutalāy niṉṟa coṟkaḷun tammiṟkūṭa, aṉṟiyaṉittum - appatiṉēḻākiya varumoḻikaḷum, ellāvaḻiyum - vēṟṟumiyum alvaḻiyumākiya ellā iṭattum, niṉṟa coṉmuṉ - irupattunāṉku īṟṟavāy niṉṟa peyarccoṉmuṉṉar, iyalpākum - tiripiṉṟi iyalpu puṇarcciyāy niṟkum eṉṟavāṟu.
uyirīṟṟiṉkaṇ ekara okaram oḻintaṉa koḷka.
utāraṇam:	viḷa palā kiḷi kurī kaṭu pū cē ki cō ku eṉa niṟutti, ñāṉṟatu nīṇṭatu māṇṭatu yātu valitu nuntiyatu eṉa meymmutaṉmoḻi varuvittu, poruḷ tarutaṟku ēṟpaṉa aṟintu kūṭṭuka. cō eṉpatu araṇ. ataṟkuc cōñoḷkiṟṟu eṉak koḷka. ku veṉpataṟkuk keḷañekiḻntatu, nīṭiṟṟu eṉka. iṉi ivaṟṟiṉ muṉṉar uyirmutaṉmoḻi varuṅkāl aḻakitu āyiṟṟu illi īṇṭiṟṟu uṇṭu ūṟiṟṟu eḻuntatu ēyntatu aitu oṉṟu ōṅkiṟṟu auviyattatu eṉa varum. ivaṟṟuṭ cōvukku - iṭintatu īṇṭiyatu uḷḷatu ūṟiṟṟu eṉpaṉa vaṟṟōṭu muṟkūṟiyavaṟṟiyu moṭṭuka. iṉi vēṟṟumikkaṇ viḷa mutaliyavaṟṟi niṟutti, ñāṟci nīṭci māṭci yāppu vaṉmi aḻaku ākkam iḷami īṭṭam uyarvu ūṟṟam eḻucci ēṟṟam aiyam oḻivu ōkkam auviyam eṉa oṭṭuka. ēlātaṉavaṟṟiṟku muṟkūṟiyavāṟu pōla ēṟpaṉa koṇarntu oṭṭuka.
iṉip puḷḷiyīṟṟu ṇakāramum ṉakāramum mēṟkūṟupa. ēṉiya īṇṭuk kūṟutum.
utāraṇam: uriñ verin eṉa niṟutti, ñekiḻntatu nīṭiṟṟu aḻakitu āyiṟṟu eṉavum, ñekiḻcci nīṭṭippu aṭivu ākkam eṉavum varuvittu, ellāvaṟṟōṭum oṭṭuka. maram vēy vēr yāḻ eṉa niṟutti, ñāṉṟatu nīṇṭatu māṇṭatu yātu valitu nuntiyatu aḻakitu āyiṟṟu eṉavum, ñāṟci nīṭci māṭci yāppu vaṉmi aṭivu ākkam eṉavum varuvittu, ellāvaṟṟōṭum oṭṭuka. ivaṟṟuḷ, makara īṟu vēṟṟumikkaṭ keṭutal tuvara (eḻu. 310) eṉṟataṉāṟ koḷka. alvaḻikkaṭ keṭutal alvaḻi yellām   (eḻu. 314) eṉṟataṉāṟ koḷka. nilimoḻit tiripu īṇṭuk koḷḷāmi uṇarka. yakara īṟu yakarattiṉ muṉṉar iraṇṭiṭattuṅ keṭutal īṇṭu ellāmeṉṟataṉāṟ koḷka. vēl tev kōḷ eṉa niṟutti, ēṟpaṉa koṇarntu iruvaḻiyum oṭṭuka. ṇakāra lakāra ḷakāra ṉakāraṅkaḷiṉ muṉṉar nakaram varu moḻiyāka vantuḻi annakaran tiritaliṉ attirinta utāraṇaṅkaḷ īṇṭuk koḷḷaṟka. ivaṟṟuḷ tirintu varuvaṉavuḷa; avi 3eṭuttōttāṉum ilēcāṉum ēṉiyōttukkaḷuḷ muṭikiṉṟa vāṟṟāṉ uṇarka. iṉi, ellāmeṉṟataṉāṉ uyirkkaṇamāyiṉ oṟṟiraṭṭi yum uṭampaṭumey peṟṟum uyirēṟiyum muṭiyuṅ karuvittiripukaḷ tiripeṉappaṭā, ivviyalpiṉkaṇṇeṉṟu uṇarka. varaku ñāṉṟatu, varaku ñāṟci eṉak kuṟṟukarattiṉkaṇṇum ivvāṟē koḷka. irupattunāṉku īṟṟiṟkum vēṟṟumikkum alvaḻikkum akattōttiṉuḷ nāṟpatteṭṭuc cūttiraṅkaḷāṉ muṭivaṉa vaṟṟi oru cūttirattāṟ ṟokuttu muṭittār. mēlum ivvāṟē kūṟupa. ivviyalpu varumoḻi nōkkik kūṟiya teṉṟu uṇarka. ivviyalpu puṇarcci meykkaṇ nikaḻumāṟu, uyirkkaṇ nikaḻāmiyiṉ mey muṟkūṟiṉār.	(2)
145.		avaṟṟuḷ
					melleḻut tiyaṟki yuṟaḻiṉum variyār
					colliya toṭarmoḻi yiṟuti yāṉa.
itu, muṟkūṟiya muṭipiṟ cilavaṟṟiṟku ammuṭipu vilakkip piṟitu viti eytuvittatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: avaṟṟuḷ - muṟkūṟiya mūṉṟu kaṇattiṉuḷ, melleḻut tiyaṟki uṟaḻiṉum variyār - melleḻuttu iyalpāta lēyaṉṟi uṟaḻntu muṭiyiṉum nīkkār; colliya toṭar moḻi iṟutiyāṉa - collappaṭṭa toṭarmoḻi yīṟṟukkaṇ eṉṟavāṟu.
ummi - etirmaṟi. eṉavē, uṟaḻāmi valiyuṭit tāyiṟṟu. 	 katirñeri katirññeri nuṉimuri eṉavum, itaḻñeri itaḻññeri nuṉimuri eṉavum varum. varumoḻi muṟkūṟiyavataṉāl, ōreḻuttorumoḻi īreḻuttoru moḻikaḷuḷḷuñ cila uṟaḻccipeṟṟu muṭital koḷka. pūñeri pūññeri nuṉimuri, 4kāyñeri kāyññeri nuṉi muri eṉa varum. colliyaveeṉṟataṉāṉ, ōreḻuttoru moḻikaḷuṭ cila mikku muṭital koḷka.  	kiññerittār, nīṭṭiṉār maṟittār eṉa varum. iṉṉum itaṉāṉē, īreḻuttorumoḻikkaṇ meyñ ñāṉam nūl maṟantōr eṉavarum. ivaṟṟi nalintu kūṟap piṟattaliṉ iyalpeṉ pārum uḷar. pūñāṟṟiṉār eṉṟāṟ pōlvaṉa mikātaṉa.	(3)
146.		ṇaṉaveṉ puḷḷimuṉ yāvu ñāvum
					viṉiyō raṉiya veṉmaṉār pulavar.
itu, yakara ñakara mutaṉmoḻi vanta iṭattu nikaḻvatōr taṉmi kūṟukiṉṟatu. ituvum puṇariyaloḻipāyk karuvip pāṟpaṭum.
itaṉ poruḷ: ṇaṉaveṉ puḷḷimuṉ yāvum ñāvum - ṇakāra ṉakārameṉṟu kūṟappaṭum puḷḷikaḷiṉ muṉṉarvanta yāvum ñāvum mutalākiya viṉiccoṟkaḷ, viṉiyōraṉiya eṉmaṉār pulavar - oru viṉi vanta taṉmiyi okkumeṉṟu colluvar pulavar eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam: 	maṇyātta kōṭṭa maḻakaḷiṟu tōṉṟumē maṇ ñātta kōṭṭa maḻakaḷiṟu tōṉṟumē eṉavum, poṉyātta tārp puravi parikkumē poṉñātta tārp puravi parikkumē eṉavum varum.
viṉikkaṇṇeṉavē, maṇyāmi maṇñāmi eṉap peyarkkaṇ vārāvāyiṉa. ñāmuṟkūṟātu yā muṟkūṟiyavata ṉāṉ ñāc ceṉṟavaḻi yāc cellātu, yāc ceṉṟavaḻi ñāc cellumeṉṟu koḷka. maṇñāṉṟatu eṉṟavaḻi maṇyāṉṟatu eṉṟu vārāmi uṇarka.	(4)
147.		moḻimuta lāku mellā veḻuttum
					varuvaḻi niṉṟa vāyiru puḷḷiyum
					vēṟṟumi yalvaḻit tiripiṭa ṉilavē.
itu, ṇakāra īṟum, ṉakāra īṟum alvaḻikkaṇ iyalpāy muṭiyumeṉkiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: moḻi mutalākum ellā veḻuttum varuvaḻi - moḻikku mutalāmeṉappaṭṭa irupattiraṇ ṭeḻuttum varumoḻiyāy varumiṭattu, niṉṟa āyiru puḷḷiyum - muṉṉark kūṟiniṉṟa ṇakāramum ṉakāramum, vēṟṟumi yalvaḻit tiripiṭaṉ ilavē - vēṟṟumi yallāta iṭattut tiriyumiṭam ila eṉṟavāṟu.
maṇ poṉ eṉa niṟuttik, kaṭitu ciṟitu tītu peritu ñekiḻntatu nīṇṭatu māṇṭatu yātu valitu nunti yatu aṭintatu āyiṟṟu illi īṇṭiṟṟu uṇṭu ūṭṭiṟṟu evviṭattatu ēṟiṟṟu aitu oḻukiṟṟu ōṅkiṟṟu auviyatu eṉa oṭṭuka. varumoḻi muṟkūṟiyavataṉāl, ṇakārattiṟkuc ciṟupāṉmi tiripum uṇṭeṉṟu koḷka. cāṭkōl eṉa varum. itaṟkuc cāṇākiya kōleṉka. ivi niṉṟacoṉ muṉiyalpākum (eḻu. 144) eṉṟavaḻi aṭaṅkāvāyiṉa, atu varumoḻipaṟṟit tiriyāmi kūṟiyatātaliṉ. itu - nili moḻi paṟṟit tiriyāmi kūṟiyatu.	(5)
148.		vēṟṟumik kaṇṇum valleḻut talvaḻi
					mēṟkū ṟiyaṟki yāvayi ṉāṉa.
itu, muṟkūṟiyavāṟṟāṉ vēṟṟumikkaṇ tiripu eyti niṉṟavaṟṟi, īṇṭu vēṟṟumikkaṇṇum valleḻuttu alvaḻit tiriyāveṉa eytiyatu vilakkiṟṟu.
itaṉ poruḷ: āvayiṉāṉa - alvaḻikkaṇ aṅṅaṉan tiriyātu niṉṟa avvoṟṟukkaḷ, vēṟṟumikkaṇṇum - vēṟṟumip poruṭ puṇarcci yiṭattum, valleḻut talvaḻi mēṟ kūṟiyaṟki - valleḻuttallāta iṭattu mēṟkūṟiya iyalpu muṭipām eṉṟavāṟu.
eṉavē, valleḻuttu vantuḻit tiriyu meṉṟārāyiṟṟu.
utāraṇam:	maṇ poṉ eṉa niṟutti, ñekiḻcci nīṭci māṭci yāppu vaṉmi nuntiyatu aḻaku ākkam iṉmi eṉa ēṟpaṉa koṇarntu oṭṭuka. ituvuñ 5ceykic cūttiram. mēl nāṉku cūttirattāṟ kūṟiyaṉa valleḻuttu vantuḻit tiriyumāṟu tattam īṟṟuṭ kūṟupa.	 (6)
149.		laṉaveṉa varūum puḷḷi muṉṉart
					tanaveṉa variṟ ṟaṉavā kummē.
itu puḷḷimayaṅkiyali nōkkiya tōr varumoḻik karuvi kūṟukiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: la ṉa eṉa varūum puḷḷi muṉṉar - lakāra ṉakāra meṉṟu colla varukiṉṟa puḷḷikaḷiṉ muṉṉar, ta na eṉavariṉ - takāramum nakāramum mutaleṉṟu collum paṭiyākac cilacoṟkaḷvariṉ, ṟaṉavākum - niraṉiṟiyāṉē avi ṟakāra ṉakāraṅ kaḷākat tiriyum eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam:	kaḵṟītu kaṉṉaṉṟu, poṉṟītu poṉṉaṉṟu eṉa varum.  nilimoḻittiripu tattam īṟṟuṭ kūṟupa. 	(7)
150.	ṇaḷaveṉ puḷḷimuṉ ṭaṇaveṉat tōṉṟum.
ituvum atu.
itaṉ poruḷ: ṇaḷaveṉ puḷḷimuṉ - ṇakāra ḷakāra meṉṟu collap paṭum puḷḷikaḷiṉ muṉṉar atikārattāl takāra nakāraṅkaḷ varumeṉiṉ, ṭaṇa veṉat tōṉṟum - avi niraṉiṟiyāṉē ṭakāra ṇakāraṅkaḷāyt tirintu tōṉṟum eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam:	 maṇṭītu, maṇṇaṉṟu, muḵṭītu muṇṇaṉṟu                      eṉa varum. nilimoḻit tiripu tattam īṟṟuṭ   kūṟupa. 	(8)
151.		uyirī ṟākiya muṉṉilik kiḷaviyum
					puḷḷi yiṟuti muṉṉilik kiḷaviyu
					miyalpā kunavu muṟaḻā kunavumeṉ
					ṟāyī riyala valleḻuttu variṉē.
itu, muṉṉili viṉiccol vaṉkaṇattukkaṇ muṭiyu māṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: uyirīṟākiya muṉṉilik kiḷaviyum - uyirīṟāy vanta muṉṉili viṉiccoṟkaḷum, puḷḷiyiṟuti muṉṉilik kiḷaviyum - puḷḷiyīṟāy vanta muṉṉili viṉiccoṟkaḷum, valleḻuttu variṉ - valleḻuttu mutalākiya moḻivariṉ, iyalpākunavum uṟaḻākunavu meṉṟu āyīriyala - iyalpāy muṭivaṉavum uṟaḻntu muṭivaṉavumeṉa avviraṇṭu iyalpiṉiyuṭiya eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam:	eṟikoṟṟā koṇākoṟṟā uṇkoṟṟā tiṉkoṟṟā cāttā tēvā pūtā eṉa ivi iyalpu. naṭakoṟṟā naṭakkoṟṟā, īrkoṟṟā īrkkoṟṟā cāttā tēvā pūtā eṉa ivi uṟaḻcci. īṟuṟeṉṟu ōtiṉamiyiṉ viṉiccollē koḷka. ivi 6muṉṉiṉṟāṉ toḻi luṇarttuvaṉavum, avaṉit toḻiṟpaṭuttuvaṉavumeṉa iruvakiya. i ai āy mutaliyaṉa toḻiluṇarttuvaṉa. naṭa, vā, uṇ, tiṉ mutaliyaṉa uyirīṟum puḷḷiyīṟun toḻiṟpaṭuttuvaṉa. nilkoṟṟā niṟkoṟṟā eṉat tirintu uṟaḻntaṉavum, uṟaḻā kunavum eṉṉum potuvakiyāṉ muṭikka. iyalpu muṟaḻvumeṉ ṟiraṇṭiyalpiṉa eṉṉātu ākunavum eṉṟataṉāṉ, tukkoṟṟā nokkoṟṟā ñeḷḷā nākā māṭā vaṭukā eṉa ōreḻuttorumoḻi muṉṉili viṉic col mikkē muṭital koḷka.	(9)
152.		auveṉa varūum muyiriṟu collum
					ñanamava eṉṉum puḷḷi yiṟutiyuṅ
					kuṟṟiya lukarat tiṟutiyu muḷappaṭa
					muṟṟat tōṉṟā muṉṉili moḻikkē.
iḵtu - eytiyatu vilakkiṟṟu; muṟkūṟiyavaṟṟuṭ cila ākātaṉa vaṟṟi varintu uṇarttaliṉ.
itaṉ poruḷ: auveṉa varūum uyiriṟu collum - auveṉa varukiṉṟa uyirīṟṟuc collum, ñanamava eṉṉum puḷḷiyiṟutiyum - ñanamava eṉṟu collappaṭum puḷḷiyīṟṟuc collum, kuṟṟiyalukarattu iṟutiyum - kuṟṟiyalukaratti iṟutiyilēyuṭiya collum, muṉṉili moḻikku uḷappaṭa muṟṟat tōṉṟā - muṉṉar muṉṉili moḻikkup poruntak kūṟiya iyalpum uṟaḻcciyumākiya muṭipiṟku muṟṟat tōṉṟā eṉṟavāṟu.
muṟṟaveṉṟataṉāṉ nilimoḻi ukaram peṟṟu uṟaḻntu muṭital koḷka.
utāraṇam:	kuvukoṟṟā kuvukkoṟṟā, vuvu koṟṟā vuvukkoṟṟā, uriñukoṟṟā, uriñuk koṟṟā, porunu koṟṟā porunukkoṟṟā, tirumu koṟṟā tirumukkoṟṟā, tevvukoṟṟā tevvuk koṟṟā eṉavum 7kūṭṭukoṟṟā kūṭṭukkoṟṟā eṉavum varum.	(10)

153.		uyirī ṟākiya vuyartiṇip peyarum
					puḷḷi yiṟuti yuyartiṇip peyaru
					mellā vaḻiyu miyalpeṉa moḻipa.
iḵtu - uyartiṇippeyar vaṉkaṇam, meṉkaṇam, iṭik kaṇam, uyirkkaṇameṉṉum nāṉku kaṇattilum iruvaḻiyum muṭiyumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: uyirīṟākiya uyartiṇip peyarum - uyirīṟāy vanta uyartiṇip peyarkaḷum, puḷḷi yiṟuti uyartiṇip peyarum - puḷḷiyīṟṟiṉiyuṭiya uyartiṇip peyarkaḷum, ellā vaḻiyum - nāṉku kaṇattu alvaḻiyum vēṟṟumiyumākiya ellā iṭattum, iyalpeṉa moḻipa - iyalpāy muṭiyumeṉṟu kūṟuvar pulavar eṉṟavāṟu.
vaṉkaṇam oḻinta kaṇaṅkaḷi ña na ma ya va (eḻu. 144) eṉpataṉāṉ muṭippārum uḷar. atu poruntātu; ivvāciriyar uyartiṇip peyarum viravup peyarum eṭuttōtiyē muṭippā rātaliṉ.
utāraṇam:	nampi avaṉ eṉavum, naṅki avaḷ eṉavum niṟutti, alvaḻikkaṭ kuṟiyaṉ ciṟiyaṉ tīyaṉ periyaṉ eṉavum; kuṟiyaḷ ciṟiyaḷ tīyaḷ periyaḷ eṉavum; ñāṉṟāṉ nīṇṭāṉ māṇṭāṉ eṉavum; ñāṉ ṟāḷ nīṇṭāḷ māṇṭāḷ eṉavum; yāvaṉ valiyaṉ eṉavum; yāvaḷ valiyaḷ eṉavum; aṭintāṉ āyi ṉāṉ auviyattāṉ eṉavum; aḻakiyaḷ āṭiṉāḷ auviyat tāḷ eṉavum oṭṭuka. iṉi vēṟṟumikkaṭ ki cevi tali puṟam eṉavum; ñāṟci nīṭci māṭci eṉavum; yāppu vaṉmi eṉavum; aḻaku --- auviyam eṉavum ellāvaṟṟōṭum oṭṭuka. oruvēṉ eṉat taṉmip peyarkkaṇṇuṅ kuṟiyēṉ ciṟiyēṉ tīyēṉ periyēṉ eṉavum, ki cevi tali puṟam eṉavum oṭṭuka. nī muṉṉili viravuppeyarātaliṉ īṇṭik kākā.
iṉi uyirīṟu puḷḷiyiṟuti eṉṟa mikiyāṉē, uyar tiṇip peyar 8tirintu muṭivaṉavuṅ koḷka. kapilaparaṇar, iṟivaneṭuvēṭṭuvar, maruttuva māṇikkar eṉa ṉakara īṟu keṭṭu iyalpāy muṭintaṉa. ācīvakappaḷḷi, nikkantakkōṭṭam eṉa ivi avvīṟu keṭṭu oṟṟu mikku muṭintaṉa. īḻavakkatti, vāṇikatteru, aracakkaṉṉi, kōlikakkaruvi eṉa ivi orumi yīṟum paṉmi yīṟuṅ keṭṭu mikku muṭintaṉa. kumarakōṭṭam kumarak kōṭṭam, pirama kōṭṭam piramakkōṭṭam eṉa ivi īṟukeṭṭu valleḻuttu uṟaḻntaṉa. vaṇṇārappeṇṭir: iḵtu mikku muṭintatu. palcaṅkattār, pal cāṉṟōr, pallaracar eṉṟāṟ pōlvaṉa rakaravīṟum ataṉ muṉṉiṉṟa akaramuṅ keṭṭup piṟa ceyki kaḷum peṟṟu muṭintaṉa.
iṉi, ellā vaḻiyu meṉṟataṉāṉ, uyartiṇi viṉic col iyalpāyun tirintum muṭivaṉa ellāṅ koḷka. uṇku uṇṭu varutu cēṟu uṇpal uṇṭēṉ uṇpēṉ eṉṉun taṉmi viṉikaḷik koṟṟā cāttā tēvā pūtā eṉpaṉavaṟṟōṭu oṭṭuka. uṇṭīr cāṉṟīr pārppīr eṉa muṉṉilik kaṇṇum, uṇpa uṇṭār - cāṉṟōr pārppār eṉap paṭarkkikkaṇṇum oṭṭuka. ivi iyalpu. uṇṭaṉeñ cāṉṟēm uṇṭēnām eṉṟāṟ pōlvaṉa tirintu muṭintaṉa. piṟavum aṉṉa.	(11)
154.		avaṟṟuḷ
					ikara viṟupeyar tiripiṭa ṉuṭittē.
iḵtu - uyartiṇip peyaruṭ cilavaṟṟiṟku eytāta teytuvittatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: avaṟṟuḷ ikara iṟupeyar - muṟkūṟiya uyartiṇip peyarkaḷuḷ ikara īṟṟuppeyar, tiripiṭaṉu ṭittu - iruvaḻiyun tirintu muṭiyum iṭaṉuṭittu eṉṟavāṟu.
eṭṭippū, kāvitippū, nampippēṟu eṉa ivvuyar tiṇip peyarkaḷ vēṟṟumikkaṇ mikku muṭintaṉa. 9eṭṭi, kāviti eṉpaṉa tēyavaḻakkākiya ciṟappup peyar. eṭṭi maram aṉṟu. aḵtu eṭṭi kumara ṉiruntōṉ ṟaṉṉi (maṇi. 4 : 58) eṉṟataṉāṉ uṇarka. ivi eṭṭiyatupū eṭṭikkuppū eṉa viriyum. iṉi, nampikkollaṉ nampiccāṉṟāṉ nampittuṇi nampip piḷḷi eṉavum, ceṭṭikkūttaṉ cāttaṉ tēvaṉ pūtaṉ eṉavum alvaḻikkaṇ uyartiṇippeyar mikku muṭintaṉa. iṭaṉuṭit teṉṟataṉāṉ ikara īṟallātaṉavum īṟu tiriyātu niṉṟu valleḻuttup peṟutal koḷka. naṅkippeṇ naṅkiccāṇi eṉa alvaḻikkaṭ ciṟupāṉmi aikāra īṟu mikkaṉa. ivvīṟ ṟaḵṟiṇip peyar mikku muṭital uyir mayaṅkiyaluṭ kūṟupa.	(12)
155.	aḵṟiṇi viravuppeya riyalpumā ruḷavē.
itu viravuppeyaruḷ iyalpāy muṭivaṉavum uḷaveṉ kiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: aḵṟiṇi viravuppeyar - uyartiṇip peyarōṭu aḵṟiṇi viraviya viravuppeyar, iyalpumāruḷa - iyalpāy muṭivaṉavum uḷa, ummiyāṉ iyalpiṉṟi muṭivaṉa vum uḷa eṉṟavāṟu.
uyartiṇip peyarōṭu aḵṟiṇi ceṉṟu viraviṟ ṟeṉṟa teṉṉi? collatikārattu irutiṇic coṟkumō raṉṉa vurimiyiṉ (col. 174) eṉṟu cūttirañ ceyvārā leṉiṉ, atuvum poruntumāṟu kūṟutum. cāttaṉ cātti, muṭavaṉ muṭatti eṉa varum viravup peyarkkaṇ uyartiṇikku uriyavāka ōtiya āṇpālum peṇpālum uṇartti niṉṟa īṟṟe ḻuttukkaḷē aḵṟiṇi yāṇpālum peṇpālum uṇarttiṟ ṟeṉṟal vēṇṭum; eṉṉi? aḵṟiṇikku orumippālum paṉmippālum uṇarttum īṟaṉṟi āṇpālum peṇpālum uṇarttum īṟukaḷ uḷavāka āciriyar ōtāmiyiṉ. aṅṅaṉam uyartiṇi irupālum uṇarttum īṟukaḷ niṉṟē aḵṟiṇi yāṇ pāliyum peṇpāliyum uṇarttutaliṉ, aḵṟiṇi uyartiṇi yōṭu ceṉṟu viraviṟṟeṉṟu avaṟṟiṉ uṇmit taṉmit tōṟṟaṅ kūṟuvāṉ īṇṭuk kūṟiṉār. ivvāṟē viḷimarapiṉkaṭ 10kiḷanta viṟuti yaḵṟiṇi viravuppeyar (col. 152) eṉpuḻiyum āciriyar uyartiṇiyōṭu aḵṟiṇi viraviya viravuppeyareṉa āṇṭum uṇmit taṉmit tōṟṟaṅ kūṟuvār. māṇākkaṉ iṉitu uṇartaṟku ivvāṟu viravup peyariṉatu uṇmit taṉmit tōṟṟam iraṇṭu atikārattuṅ kūṟi, avviravuppeyar vaḻakkiṉkaṇ irutiṇip poruḷum uṇartti irutiṇic collāy niṟṟaṟkum otta urimiyavā meṉap pulappaṭa niṟkumāṟu kāṭṭiṉāreṉṟu  uṇarka.
iṉi, avi alvaḻikkaṇ iyalpāy niṟkumāṟu:- cāttaṉ koṟṟaṉ, cātti koṟṟi eṉa niṟuttik, kuṟiyaṉ ciṟiyaṉ tīyaṉ periyaṉ, kuṟiyaḷ ciṟiyaḷ tīyaḷ periyaḷ eṉavum; ñāṉṟāṉ nīṇṭāṉ māṇṭāṉ yāvaṉ valiyaṉ, ñāṉṟāḷ nīṇṭāḷ māṇṭāḷ yāvaḷ valiyaḷ eṉavum; aṭintāṉ auvittāṉ, aṭintāḷ auvittāḷ eṉavum, nāṉku kaṇattōṭum oṭṭi uṇarka.  iṉi, vēṟṟumikkaṇ ki cevi tali puṟam eṉavum, ñāṟci nīṭci māṭci yāppu vaṉmi aḻaku auviyam eṉavum oṭṭuka. ivaṟṟuḷ ṉakāram niṟpat takāra nakāram vantuḻit tiriyum utāraṇam īṇṭuk koḷḷaṟka.  iṉic cāttaṉkuṟitu, cāttikuṟitu eṉa aḵṟiṇi muṭipēṟ paṉavuṅ koḷka. ivaṟṟoṭu viṉiccol talippeyya ivi irutiṇikkum uriyavām. āṇṭu nāṟpatteṭṭuc cūttiraṅ kaḷāṉ muṭivataṉi īṇṭut tokuttār. iḵtu uyar tiṇikkum okkum. uṇmiyāṉ, iyalpiṉṟi muṭivaṉa ṉakāra īṟṟuṭ kāṭṭutum.	 (13)
156.		11puḷḷi yiṟutiyu muyiriṟu kiḷaviyum
						valleḻuttu mikuti colliya muṟiyāṟ
					12ṟammi ṉākiya toḻiṟcoṉ muṉvariṉ
						meymmi yākalu muṟaḻat tōṉṟalu
						mammuṟi yiraṇṭu muriyavi yuḷavē
						vēṟṟumi maruṅkiṟ pōṟṟal vēṇṭum.
itu, mēl uyirīṟṟiṟkum puḷḷiyīṟṟiṟkum vēṟṟumikkaṭ kūṟum muṭipu peṟātu niṟkum mūṉṟām vēṟṟumi muṭipu kūṟu kiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: tammiṉākiya toḻiṟ col - mūṉṟāvataṟku uriya viṉi mutaṟ poruḷāṉuḷavākiya toḻiṟcol, puḷḷiyiṟuti muṉṉum uyiriṟu kiḷavi muṉṉum variṉ - puḷḷiyīṟṟuc coṉ muṉṉarum uyirīṟṟuc coṉ muṉṉarum varumāyiṉ, meymmi yākalum uṟaḻat tōṉṟalum ammuṟiyiraṇṭum uriyavi uḷa - avaṟṟuḷ iyalpākalum uṟaḻattōṉṟalumākiya am muṟi yiraṇṭum peṟutaṟku uriya uḷavātalāl, vēṟṟumi maruṅkiṟ colliya muṟiyāṉ vēṇṭum valleḻuttu mikuti - uyir mayaṅkiyaluḷḷum puḷḷimayaṅkiyaluḷḷum vēṟṟumip puṇarccikkuc colliya muṟiyāṉ virumpum valleḻuttu mikutiyi, pōṟṟal - īṇṭuk koḷḷaṟka eṉṟavāṟu.
meymmi 13paṭṭāṅkātaliṉ iyalpām.
utāraṇam:	nāy puli eṉa niṟuttik kōṭpaṭṭāṉ cārap paṭṭāṉ tīṇṭappaṭṭāṉ pāyappaṭṭāṉ eṉa varuvittu iyalpāyavāṟu kāṇka. cūrkōṭpaṭṭāṉ cūrkkōṭ paṭṭāṉ, vaḷikōṭpaṭṭāṉ vaḷik kōṭpaṭṭāṉ, cārappaṭṭāṉ, tīṇṭappaṭṭāṉ, pāyappaṭṭāṉ eṉa ivi uṟaḻntaṉa. ivi nāṟpatteṭṭuc cūttiraṅkaḷāṉ muṭivaṉavaṟṟit tokuttār. 
puḷḷiyiṟuti uyiriṟukiḷavi eṉṟataṉāṟ pēeykōṭ paṭṭāṉ pēeykkōṭpaṭṭāṉ eṉa ekarappēṟum uṟaḻccikkuk koṭukka. `ammuṟi yiraṇṭu muriyavi yuḷavē eṉṟataṉāṟ pāmpu kōṭpaṭṭāṉ pāppukkōṭpaṭṭāṉ eṉṉum uṟaḻcciyuḷ nili moḻi yoṟṟut tiritaluṅ koḷka. ivvīṟukaḷ nāykkāl tērkkāl kiḷikkāl eṉa āṇṭu vēṟṟumikkaṇ valleḻuttu mikumāṟu kāṇka. 	(14)
157.		melleḻuttu mikuvaḻi valippoṭu tōṉṟalum
					valleḻuttu mikuvaḻi melippoṭu tōṉṟalu
					miyaṟki maruṅkiṉ mikaṟki tōṉṟalu
					muyirmika varuvaḻi yuyirkeṭa varutaluñ
					cāriyi yuḷvaḻic cāriyi keṭutaluñ
					cāriyi yuḷvaḻit taṉṉurupu niliyaluñ
					cāriyi yiyaṟki yuṟaḻat tōṉṟalu
					muyartiṇi maruṅki ṉoḻiyātu varutalu
					maḵṟiṇi viravuppeyark kavviya ṉiliyalu
					meypiṟi tākiṭat tiyaṟki yātalu
					maṉṉa piṟavun taṉṉiyaṉ maruṅkiṉ
					meypeṟak kiḷantu poruḷvarin ticikku
					mikāra vēṟṟumit tiripeṉa moḻipa.
iḵtu - iraṇṭām vēṟṟumit 14tiripu tokuttu uṇarttu kiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: melleḻuttu mikuvaḻi valippoṭu tōṉṟalum - marappeyark kiḷavi melleḻuttu mikumē (eḻu. 217) eṉṟata ṉāṉ viḷaṅkuṟittāṉeṉa melleḻuttu mikumiṭattu viḷakkuṟittāṉeṉa valleḻuttut tōṉṟuta lum,  valleḻuttu mikuvaḻi melippoṭu tōṉṟalum - makara viṟuti (eḻu. 310) eṉpataṉāṉ marakkuṟittāṉ eṉa valle ḻuttu mikumiṭattu maraṅkuṟittāṉ eṉa melleḻuttut tōṉṟutalum, iyaṟki maruṅkiṉ mikaṟki tōṉṟalum - tāyeṉ kiḷavi yiyaṟki yākum (eḻu. 358) eṉṟavaḻit tāykoli eṉa iyalpāy varumiṭattut tāykkoli eṉa mikuti tōṉṟutalum, uyirmika varuvaḻi uyirkeṭa varutalum - kuṟiyataṉ muṉṉarum (eḻu. 226) eṉavuṅ, kuṟṟeḻut timparum (eḻu. 267) eṉavum, ēye ṉiṟutikku (eḻu. 277) eṉavuṅ kūṟiyavaṟṟāṉ uyirmikku varumiṭattup palākkuṟittāṉ kaḻukkoṇarn tāṉ ēkkaṭṭiṉāṉ eṉa uyir keṭa varutalum, cāriyi uḷvaḻic cāriyikeṭutalum - vaṇṭum peṇṭum (eḻu. 420) eṉpataṉāṟ cāriyippēṟu uḷḷa iṭattu  vaṇṭu koṇarntāṉ eṉac cāriyi keṭṭu niṟṟalum, cāriyi uḷvaḻit taṉṉurupu niliyalum - vaṇṭum peṇṭum eṉpataṉāṟ cāriyippēṟu uḷḷa iṭattu vaṇṭi ṉik koṇarntāṉ eṉat taṉṉurupu niṟṟalum (itaṟku valle ḻuttuppēṟu īṟṟu vakiyāṟ koḷka), cāriyi iyaṟki uṟaḻat tōṉṟalum - puḷimarak kiḷavikku (eḻu. 244) eṉavum, paṉiyumariyum (eḻu. 283) eṉavum, pūlvē leṉṟā (eḻu. 375) eṉavum, peṟṟa cāriyi peṟātu iyalpāy niṉṟu puḷikuṟittāṉ puḷikkuṟittāṉ, paṉitaṭintāṉ,   paṉit taṭintāṉ, pūlkuṟittāṉ pūṟkuṟittāṉ eṉa mikkun tirintum uṟaḻcciyākat tōṉṟutalum, uyartiṇi maruṅkiṉ oḻiyātu varutalum - uyirīṟākiya uyar tiṇip peyarum (eḻu. 153) eṉpataṉāṉ, vēṟṟumikkaṇ iyalpāy varumeṉṟavi nampiyik koṇarntāṉ naṅkiyik koṇarntāṉ eṉṟavaḻi iraṇṭaṉurupu tokātē valloṟṟu mikku niṟṟalum, oḻiyātu eṉṟataṉāṉ, makaṟpeṟṟāṉ makaṭpeṟṟāṉ eṉavum, āṭūuvaṟicol (col. 2) maḻavarōṭṭiya avaṟkaṇ ṭemmuḷ eṉavum oḻintum varumeṉṟu koḷka. aḵṟiṇi viravuppeyarkku avviyal niliyalum - uyartiṇi yōṭu aḵṟiṇi viravum peyarkkuk koṟṟaṉik koṇarntāṉeṉa urupu tokātē niṟṟalum,  avviyal nili yalum eṉṟataṉāṉē, makappeṟṟē ṉeṉa viravuppeyark kaṇṇun tokutal koḷka. urupiyaluṭ, tēruṅ kāli (eḻu. 202) eṉṟa ilēcāṉ, itaṟkum muṉṉiyataṟkum valleḻuttup pēṟuṅ koḷka. mey piṟitākiṭattu iyaṟki yātalum - puḷḷi mayaṅki yaluḷ ṇakāra ṉakāra iṟuti valleḻuttiyiyiṉ meypiṟitā meṉṟa iṭattu meypiṟitā kātu, maṇkoṇarntāṉ poṉ koṇarntāṉ eṉa iyaṟkiyāy varutalum, aṉṉa piṟavum - avipōlvaṉa piṟavum, avi eṟkaṇṭu peyaruṅkāli yāḻaniṉ kaṟkeḻu ciṟukuṭi eṉavum, nappuṇar villā nayaṉillōr naṭpu eṉavum varuvaḻi, eṟkaṇṭu nappuṇarvu eṉṉun toṭakkaṅ kuṟukum uyar tiṇip peyarkaḷ melleḻuttup peṟutaṟku uriyaṉa valleḻuttup peṟutal koḷka. iṉṉun tiṉipiḷantāṉ mayirkuṟittāṉ taṟkoṇṭāṉ ceṟuttāṉ pukaḻntāṉ eṉa varum. taṉ iyal maruṅkiṉ - taṉṉiyē nōkkit tiripu naṭakku miṭattu, meypeṟak kiḷantu poruḷ varintu icikkum - poruḷpeṟa eṭuttōtappaṭṭu ēṉi vēṟṟumip poruṭ puṇarcciyatu potu muṭipiṉit tāṉ nīkki vēṟu muṭipiṟṟāy niṉṟu olikkum, aikāra vēṟṟumit tiripeṉa moḻipa - iraṇṭām vēṟṟumiyatu vēṟupaṭṭa puṇarcci eṉṟu kūṟuvar āciriyar eṉṟavāṟu.
meypeṟa eṉṟataṉāṉē cāriyiyuḷvaḻit taṉṉurupu niliyātu ceyyuṭkaṇ varuvaṉavum, piṟavaṟṟiṉkaṇ 15uṟaḻntu muṭivaṉavuṅ koḷka. maṟaṅkaṭinta varuṅkaṟpiṉ eṉavum, cil colliṟ palkūntal (puṟam. 166) eṉavum, āyiru tiṇiyiṉi cikkumaṉ (col. 1) eṉavum, piṟāṇṭum perumpāṉmiyum varumeṉṟu koḷka. mikoṇarntāṉ mik koṇarntāṉ, vilkōl viṟkōḷ eṉa uṟaḻntum varum. iṉi, ivvāṟu tiriyātu akattōttiṟ kūṟiya potu muṭipē tamakku muṭipāka varuvaṉavum koḷka. avi kaṭukkuṟit tāṉ, ceppuk koṇarntāṉ eṉṟāṟ pōlvaṉa. tammiṉākiya toḻiṟ coṉ muṉvariṉ (eḻu. 156) eṉṟa atikārattāṉ, viṉi vantuḻiyē iṅṅaṉam perumpāṉmi tirivateṉṟu uṇarka. iṉit taṉṉiṉa muṭitta leṉpataṉāṉ ēḻāvataṟkum viṉi yōṭu muṭivuḻit tirital koḷka. atu varipāy varuṭi, pulampuk kaṉaṉē pullaṇaṟ kāḷi (puṟam. 258) eṉṟāṟpōl varum.	(15)
158.		vēṟṟumi yalvaḻi iai eṉṉu
					mīṟṟuppeyark kiḷavi mūvaki niliya
					vavitā
					miyalpā kunavum valleḻuttu mikunavu
					muṟaḻā kunavu meṉmaṉār pulavar.
iḵtu - ikara īṟṟup peyarkkum aikāra īṟṟup peyarkkum alvaḻi muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: vēṟṟumi yalvaḻi - vēṟṟumi yallā iṭattu, i ai eṉṉum īṟṟuppeyarkkiḷavi mūvaki niliya - i ai eṉṉum īṟṟi yuṭiya peyarccoṟkaḷ mūvakiyākiya muṭipu niliyiyuṭiya; avitām - ammuṭipukaṭām, iyalpākunavum - iyalpāy muṭivaṉavum, valleḻuttu mikunavum - valleḻuttu mikku muṭivaṉavum, uṟaḻākunavum - uṟaḻcciyāy muṭivaṉavum, eṉmaṉār pulavar - eṉa iviyeṉṟu kūṟuvar pulavar eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam:	parutti kuṟitu, kāri kuṟitu ciṟitu tītu peritu eṉa ivi iyalpu. mācittiṅkaḷ, cittirittiṅkaḷ, alikkoṟṟaṉ, pulikkoṟṟaṉ, kāvikkaṇ, kuvaḷikkaṇ eṉa ivi mikuti. kiḷikuṟitu kiḷik kuṟitu, tiṉikuṟitu tiṉikkuṟitu eṉa ivi uṟaḻcci.
peyark kiḷavi mūvaki niliya veṉavē peyarallāta 16iraṇṭīṟṟu viṉiccollum iṭiccollum uric collum iyalpum mikutiyumākiya iruvaki niliyavām. ollikkoṇṭāṉ eṉpatu aikāra īṟṟu viṉiccoṉ mikuti. iṉi yaṇi (eḻut. 236) yeṉpataṉkaṇ ikarīṟṟu viṉiyeccam eṭuttōtupa. ivaṟṟiṟku iyalpu vantuḻik kāṇka. ceṉmati pāka iḵtu ikara īṟṟu iṭiccolliyalpu. mikuti vantuḻik kāṇka. tillic collē (col. 253) iḵtu aikāra īṟṟu iṭic coṉmikuti. iyalpu vantuḻik kāṇka. kaṭikā iḵtu ikara īṟṟu uriccolliyalpu. mikuti vantuḻik kāṇka. paṇittōḷ iḵtu aikāra īṟṟu uriccoṉ mikuti. iyalpu vantuḻik kāṇka.	(16)
159.		cuṭṭumuta lākiya vikara viṟutiyu
					mekaramutal viṉāvi ṉikara viṟutiyuñ
					cuṭṭucciṉi nīṭiya viye ṉiṟutiyum
					yāveṉ viṉāvi ṉiye ṉiṟutiyum
					valleḻuttu mikunavu muṟaḻā kunavuñ
					colliyaṉ maruṅki  ṉuḷaveṉa moḻipa.
iḵtu - ēḻām vēṟṟumi iṭapporuḷuṇartti niṉṟa ikara aikāra īṟṟu iṭiccoṉ muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: colliyal maruṅkiṉ - ikara aikāraṅkaṭku muṉṉark kūṟiya mūvaki ilakkaṇaṅkaḷuḷ iyalpi nīkkic cuṭṭu mutalākiya ikara iṟutiyum - cuṭṭeḻut tiṉi mutalākavuṭiya avvikara īṟṟu iṭiccollum, ekara viṉāviṉ mutal ikara iṟutiyum - ekaramākiya viṉāviṉi mutalākavuṭiya avvikara īṟṟu iṭic collum, cuṭṭucciṉi nīṭiya aiyeṉ iṟutiyum - cuṭṭākiya uṟup peḻuttu nīṇṭa avvikāra īṟṟu iṭiccollum, yāveṉ viṉāviṉ aiyeṉ iṟutiyum - yāveṉ viṉāviṉi mutaṟkaṇuṭiya avvikāra īṟṟu iṭiccollum, valleḻuttu mikunavum - valleḻuttu mikku muṭivaṉavum, uṟaḻākunavum - uṟaḻcciyāy muṭi vaṉavum, uḷaveṉa moḻipa - uḷaveṉṟu kūṟuvar pulavar eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam:atōḷikkoṇṭāṉ, itōḷik koṇṭāṉ, utōḷikkoṇṭāṉ, etōḷik koṇṭāṉ, ceṉṟāṉ tantāṉ pōyiṉāṉ eṉavum; āṇṭik koṇṭāṉ, īṇṭik koṇṭāṉ ūṇṭik koṇṭāṉ, yāṇṭikkoṇṭāṉ eṉavum ivi mikkaṉa. atōḷi avviṭameṉṉum poruṭṭu. avvaḻi koṇṭāṉ avvaḻik koṇṭāṉ, ivvaḻi koṇṭāṉ ivvaḻik koṇṭāṉ, uvvaḻi koṇṭāṉ uvvaḻik koṇṭāṉ, evvaḻi koṇṭāṉ  evvaḻik koṇṭāṉ eṉa uṟaḻntaṉa. cuṭṭucciṉi nīṇṭataṟkum yā viṉāviṟkum varum aikāra īṟṟukku utāraṇam, akkālattu, āyiṭikoṇṭāṉ āyiṭik koṇṭāṉ eṉṟāṟpōla ēṉiyavaṟṟiṟkum vaḻaṅ kiṟṟuppōlum. iṉi 17āṅkavikoṇṭāṉ āṅkavik koṇṭāṉ eṉpaṉa kāṭṭuvārum uḷar. avi tiripuṭiyaṉavām.  colliyal eṉṟataṉālē piṟa aikāra īṟu mikku muṭivaṉa koḷka. aṉṟik kūttar, paṇṭic cāṉṟōreṉavum, orutiṅ kaḷikkuḻavi, orunāḷikkuḻavi eṉavum varum.	(17)
160.		neṭiyataṉ muṉṉa roṟṟumey keṭutaluṅ
					kuṟiyataṉ muṉṉart taṉṉuru viraṭṭalu
					maṟiyat tōṉṟiya neṟiyiya leṉpa.
itu, puḷḷimayaṅkiyali nōkkiyatoru nilimoḻik karuvi kūṟukiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: neṭiyataṉ muṉṉar oṟṟu mey keṭutalum - neṭ ṭeḻuttiṉ muṉ niṉṟa oṟṟut taṉ vaṭivu keṭutalum, kuṟiyataṉ muṉṉart taṉ uru iraṭṭalum - kuṟṟeḻuttiṉ muṉ niṉṟa oṟṟuttaṉ vaṭivu iraṭṭittalum, aṟiyat tōṉṟiya neṟiyiyal eṉpa - aṟiyumpaṭi vanta aṭippāṭṭiya leṉṟu kūṟuvar āciriyar eṉṟavāṟu.
iṅṅaṉam, neṭiyataṉ muṉṉar oṟṟukkeṭuvaṉa: ṇakāramum, ṉakāramum, makāramum, lakāramum, ḷakāramum eṉa aivakiyavām.
utāraṇam: kōṇimirntatu, tāṉallaṉ, tānallar, vēṉaṉṟu, tōṇaṉṟu eṉa nakaram varumoḻiyātaṟkaṇ neṭi yataṉ muṉṉar oṟṟukkeṭṭatu. kōṟītu, vēṟītu eṉat takaram varumoḻiyātaṟkaṇ lakāravoṟṟuk keṭṭatu. ēṉiya vantuḻik kāṇka. ivaṟṟi laṉaveṉa varūum (eḻut. 149) ṇaḷaveṉ puḷḷi muṉ (eḻut. 150) eṉpaṉavaṟṟāṉ muṭittuk keṭumāṟu kāṇka. oṟṟiraṭṭuvaṉa, ñakāra nakāra rakāra ḻakāram oḻintaṉa. kaṇṇa ḻakitu, poṉṉakal, tammāṭi, collaḻakitu, eḷḷaḻakitu, neyya kal, tevvalaṉ eṉak kuṟiyataṉ muṉṉart taṉṉuru iraṭṭiṉa.
mēlic cūttirattu nāṉkaṉurupu piṟkūṟiyataṉāṉ, oṟṟiraṭṭutal uyir mutaṉmoḻikkaṇṇateṉṟu uṇarka. kuṟiyatu piṟkūṟiya muṟiyaṉṟik kūṟṟiṉāl tammi emmi niṉṉi eṉa neṭiyaṉa kuṟuki niṉṟavaḻiyuṅ kuṟiyataṉ muṉṉar oṟṟāy iraṭṭutalum, viraṉaṉṟu kuṟaṇimirntatu eṉak kuṟi liṇiyiṉ muṉṉar vanta oṟṟukkeṭutalum, varāṟītu naṉṟu eṉak kuṟiṉeṭiṟkaṇ niṉṟa oṟṟukkeṭutalum, atukoṟōḻi  eṉavuṅ kuriciṟīyaṉ eṉavun toṭarmoḻiyīṟṟu niṉṟa oṟṟuk keṭutalum, 18iṭiccollōṭu oṭṭuppaṭṭu niṟṟaluṅ, kāṟṟītu eṉavum viraṟṟītu eṉavum oṟṟu niṟṟaluṅ koḷka. iṉi aṟiya eṉṟataṉāṟ tēṉṟītu nāṇṭītu,  eṉṟāṟpōlvaṉa keṭāmi niṟṟaluṅ keṭutalum takara nakaraṅkaḷ vantuḻi yeṉpatūuṅ koḷka. neṟiyiya leṉṟataṉāṟ, cuṭṭiṉmuṉ uyir mutaṉmoḻi vantuḻi avvaṭi avvāṭi eṉa, iṭi vakaravoṟṟu il vaḻiyum iraṭṭutal koḷka.	(18)
161.		āṟa ṉurupiṉu nāṉka ṉurupiṉuṅ
					kūṟiya kuṟṟoṟ ṟiraṭṭa lilli
					yīṟāku puḷḷi yakaramoṭu niliyu
					neṭumutal kuṟuku moḻimuṉ ṉāṉa.
iḵtu urupiyali nōkkiyatōr nilimoḻikkaruvi kūṟukiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: neṭumutal kuṟukum moḻimuṉ āṉa - neṭitākiya mutaleḻuttuk kuṟuki muṭiyum aṟuvakippaṭṭa moḻikaḷiṉ muṉṉar vanta, āṟaṉurupiṉum nāṉkaṉurupiṉum - āṟām vēṟṟumikkaṇṇum nāṉkām vēṟṟumikkaṇṇum, kūṟiya kuṟṟoṟṟu iraṭṭalilli - muṉṉar nilimoḻikku iraṭṭu meṉṟa kuṟṟoṟṟu iraṭṭi varutalilli; īṟākupuḷḷi akara moṭu niliyum - nilimoḻi yīṟṟukkaṇ niṉṟa oṟṟukkaḷ akarampeṟṟu niṟkum eṉṟavāṟu.
urupiyalil, nīyeṉorupeyar (eḻut. 179) eṉavun, tāmnā meṉṉum (eḻut. 188) eṉavum, tāṉyāṉeṉṉum (eḻu. 192) eṉavuṅ kūṟiyavaṟṟāṟ kuṟuki oṟṟiraṭṭit tammi nammi emmi taṉṉi niṉṉi eṉṉi eṉa varuvaṉa, itaṉāṉē tamatu namatu ematu taṉatu niṉatu eṉatu eṉavum; tamakku namakku emakku taṉakku niṉakku eṉakku eṉavum oṟṟiraṭṭātu akaram peṟṟu vantaṉa. nāṉkāvataṟku oṟṟumikutal valleḻuttu mutaliya (eḻu. 114) eṉpataṉāṟ koḷka. āṟaṉurupākiya akaram ēṟi muṭiyāmikkuk kāraṇam āṟaṉurupi ṉakarakkiḷavi (eḻu. 115) eṉpuḻik kūṟiṉām. oṟṟiraṭṭāmiyum akarappēṟum, 19iraṇṭaṟkum otta viti yeṉṟu uṇarka. kūṟiya eṉṟataṉāṉ neṭumutal kuṟukāta tam nam num eṉa varuñ cāriyikaṭkum ivviru vitiyuṅ koḷka. ellār tamakkum, ellā namakkum, ellīr numakkum, ellār tamatum, ellā namatum, ellīr numatum eṉa varum.	(19)
162.	numme ṉiṟutiyu mannili tiriyātu.
itu, neṭumutal kuṟukāta nummeṉkiṉṟatum avviti peṟumeṉ kiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: nummeṉ iṟutiyum - neṭumutal kuṟukā nummeṉ ṉum makaravīṟum, annili tiriyātu - muṟkūṟiya kuṟṟoṟṟu iraṭṭāmiyum īṟākupuḷḷi akaramoṭu niliyalum eytum eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam: numatu numakku eṉa varum.	(20)
163.		ukaramoṭu puṇarum puḷḷi yiṟuti
					yakaramu muyirum varuvaḻi yiyaṟki.
itu, puḷḷi mayaṅkiyali nōkkiyatōr nilimoḻic ceyki kūṟukiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: ukaramoṭu puṇarum puḷḷi yiṟuti - ukarap pēṟṟōṭu puṇarum puḷḷiyīṟukaḷ, yakaramum uyirum varuvaḻi iyaṟki - yakaramum uyirum varumoḻiyāy varumiṭattu avvukaram peṟātu iyalpāy muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.
avvīṟukaḷāvaṉa, puḷḷi mayaṅkiyaluḷ ukaram peṟu meṉṟu vitikkum pala īṟukaḷumeṉṟu koḷka. uriñ yāṉā aṉantā ātā ilakā īntā uḻuntā ūrā eyiṉā ēṟā aiyā oḻukkā ōtā auviyā eṉavum, uriñyātu aḻaku eṉavum oṭṭuka. ēṉip puḷḷikaḷōṭum ēṟpaṉa aṟintu oṭṭuka.
ñakāriyoṟṟiya (eḻu. 296) eṉpataṉāṉum, ñanamava viyiyiṉum (eḻu. 297) eṉpataṉāṉum yakaramum uyirum vantāl ukaram peṟātu iyalpāmeṉpatu peṟutaliṉ īṇṭu vilakkal vēṇṭā veṉiṉ, eṭuttōttiṉvaḻiyatē uyttuṇarcci yeṉṟu koḷka.
itu mutalāka alvaḻi kūṟukiṉṟār.	(21)
164.		uyirum puḷḷiyu miṟuti yāki
					yaḷavu niṟiyu meṇṇuñ cuṭṭi
					yuḷaveṉap paṭṭa vellāc collun
					tattaṅ kiḷavi tammakap paṭṭa
					mutti varūuṅ kālan tōṉṟi
					ṉotta teṉpa vēyeṉ cāriyi.
iḵtu - aḷavum niṟiyum eṇṇumākiya peyarkaḷ tammiṟ puṇarumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: uyirum puḷḷiyum iṟutiyāki - uyirum 20puḷḷiyun tamakku īṟāy, aḷavum niṟiyum eṇṇuñ cuṭṭi vuḷaveṉappaṭṭa ellāc collum - aḷaviyum niṟiyiyum eṇṇiyuṅ karuti varuvaṉa uḷaveṉṟu āciriyar kūṟappaṭṭa ellāc coṟkaḷum, tattaṅ kiḷavi tammakappaṭṭa - tattamakku iṉamākiya coṟkaḷāyt tammiṟ kuṟinta coṟkaḷ, mutti varūuṅ kālan tōṉṟiṉ - tam muṉṉē varuṅ kālan tōṉṟu māyiṉ, ēyeṉ cāriyi ottatu eṉpa - tām ēyeṉ cāriyi peṟṟu muṭital poruntiṟṟeṉpar āciriyar eṉṟavāṟu.
munti mutti yeṉa vikāram.
 nāḻiyēyāḻākku, uḻakkēyāḻākku, kalaṉēpatakku eṉa aḷavuppeyar ēkāram peṟṟut tammuṉṉart tammiṟ kuṟintaṉa vantaṉa. toṭiyēkaḵcu, kaḻañcē kuṉṟi, koḷḷēyiyavi eṉa niṟippeyar ēkāram peṟṟut tammuṉṉart tammiṟ kuṟintaṉa vantaṉa. oṉṟēkāl, kālēkāṇi, kāṇiyēmuntiriki eṉa eṇṇuppeyar ēkāram peṟṟut tammuṉṉart tammiṟ kuṟintaṉa vantaṉa. atikārampaṭṭa puḷḷiyīṟu muṟkūṟāta taṉāṉē kuṟuṇi nāṉāḻi, ainnāḻiyuḻakku eṉa ēkāramiṉṟi varuvaṉavuṅ koḷka. 	(22)
165.		ariyeṉa varūum pālvari kiḷavikkup
					puriva taṉṟāṟ cāriyi yiyaṟki.
iḵtu - eytiyatu vilakkiṟṟu.
itaṉ poruḷ: ariyeṉa varūum pālvari
kiḷavikku - am mūvakic coṉ muṉṉar varum ari yeṉṟu colla varukiṉṟa poruṭkūṟṟi uṇaraniṉṟa colliṟku, cāriyiyiyaṟki purivataṉṟu - ēyeṉ cāriyi peṟuntaṉmi poruntuvataṉṟu eṉṟavāṟu.
āl - aci.
utāraṇam: uḻakkari, ceviṭṭari, mūvuḻakkari eṉavum; kaḵcari, kaḻañcari, toṭiyari, koḷḷari eṉavum; oṉṟari, pattari eṉavum ivi ēyeṉ cāriyi peṟāvāy vantaṉa. purivataṉ ṟeṉṟataṉāṟ kalavari yeṉpataṉi oṟṟuk keṭuttuc ceyki ceytu muṭikka. itaṉāṉē 21ceviṭṭari yeṉpuḻi ṭakaravoṟṟu mikutaluṅ koḷka. oṭṭutaṟ koḻukiya vaḻakku (eḻu. 132) aṉmiyiṟ cāriyi peṟāvāyiṉa veṉṟālō veṉiṉ, avi peṟṟum peṟātum varuvaṉavaṟṟiṟkuk kūṟiyatākalāṉum, itu tammakap paṭṭa (eḻu. 164) eṉa varintōtiṉamiyāṉum vilakkal vēṇṭiṟṟu.	(23)
166.		kuṟiyeṉ kiḷavi muṉvaru kāli
					niṟiyat tōṉṟum vēṟṟumi yiyaṟki.
iḵtu - eytiyatu vilakkip piṟituviti vakukkiṉṟatu; ēyeṉ cāriyi vilakki vēṟṟumi muṭipiṉōṭu māṭṭeṟitaliṉ.
itaṉ poruḷ: kuṟiyeṉ kiḷavi muṉvaru kāli - kuṟiyeṉṉuñ col aḷavuppeyar mutaliyavaṟṟiṉmuṉ varuṅkālattiṟku, vēṟṟumi yiyaṟki - vēṟṟumip puṇarcci muṭipiṟku urittākak kūṟuntaṉmi, niṟiyat tōṉṟum - nirampat tōṉṟum eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam:	urikkuṟi kalakkuṟi eṉavum, toṭikkuṟi koṭ kuṟi eṉavum, kāṇikkuṟi kāṟkuṟi eṉavum varum. uriya nelluṅ  kuṟinellu meṉka.
vēṟṟumi yiyaṟki yeṉavē ivi vēṟṟumi yaṉṟā yiṉa. eṉavē, urikkuṟi yeṉpataṟku uriyum uḻakkumeṉap poruḷāyiṟṟu. ēṉiyavum aṉṉa. 
muṉvaru kāli yeṉṟata ṉāṉē kalappayaṟu, kalappāku eṉṟāṟpōlap poruṭpeyarōṭu puṇarumvaḻiyum ivvēṟṟumi muṭipu eytuvikka. 22pākeṉṟatu pākkiṉi. niṟiya veṉṟataṉāṉē urikkūṟu, toṭikkūṟu, kāṇikkūṟu eṉak kūṟeṉṟataṟkum immuṭipu eytuvikka.	(24)
167.	kuṟṟiya lukarak kiṉṉē cāriyi.
itu vēṟṟumi muṭipu vilakki iṉ vakuttaliṉ eytiyatu vilakkip piṟituviti kūṟukiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: kuṟṟiyalukarakkuc cāriyi - kuṟṟiyalukara īṟṟu aḷavup peyar mutaliyavaṟṟiṟkuk kuṟiyeṉpataṉōṭu puṇarum vaḻi varuñ cāriyi, iṉṉē - iṉ cāriyiyēyām eṉṟavāṟu.
kuṟṟiyalukarakku itaṟku attu 23vitittu muṭikka. kuṟṟiya lukarak kiṉṉē yeṉpatum pāṭam.
utāraṇam: uḻakkiṉkuṟi āḻākkiṉkuṟi eṉavum, kaḻañciṉ kuṟi kaḵciṉkuṟi eṉavum, oṉṟiṉkuṟi pattiṉkuṟi eṉavum varum. itaṟku uḻakkuṅkuṟiyu meṉpatu poruḷ. itu vēṟṟumik kaṇṇāyiṉ uḻakkiṟkuṟiyeṉa niṟkum.	(25)
168.	attiṭi varūuṅ kalame ṉaḷavē.
ituvum eytiyatu vilakkip piṟituviti vakuttatu, vēṟṟumi vitivilakki attu vakuttaliṉ.
itaṉ poruḷ: kalameṉ aḷavē - kalameṉṉum aḷavuppeyar kuṟiyōṭu puṇarumiṭattu, attu iṭi varūum - attuc cāriyi iṭi vantu puṇarum eṉṟavāṟu.
kalattuk kuṟi. itaṉi attē vaṟṟē (eḻut. 33) eṉpataṉāṉ muṭikka. itaṟku kalamuṅ kuṟiyum eṉpatu poruḷ. cāriyi muṟkūṟiyavataṉāṉē, muṉ iṉcāriyi kalam peṟṟa vaḻi valleḻuttu vīḻkka.	(26)
169.		paṉiye ṉaḷavuṅ kāve ṉiṟiyu
					niṉiyuṅ kāli yiṉṉoṭu civaṇum.
ituvum atu; vēṟṟumiviti vilakki iṉ vakuttaliṉ.
itaṉ poruḷ: paṉiyeṉ aḷavuṅ kāveṉ niṟiyum - paṉi yeṉṉum aḷavuppeyarum kāveṉṉum niṟippeyaruṅ kuṟi yeṉpataṉoṭu puṇarumiṭattu, niṉiyuṅkāli iṉṉoṭu civaṇum - ārāyuṅkālattu iṉcāriyi peṟṟup puṇarum eṉṟavāṟu.
24paṉiyiṉkuṟi, kāviṉkuṟi eṉa varum. iviyum ummittoki. niṉiyuṅkāli eṉpataṉāṉ vēṟṟumikku uriya viti yeyti valleḻuttup peṟutaluñ ciṟupāṉmi koḷka. paṉikkuṟi, kākkuṟi eṉa varum.
ittuṇiyum alvaḻi muṭipu. ivaṟṟi vēṟṟumi yalvaḻi iai (eḻu. 158) eṉṉuñ cūttirattiṟ kūṟātu vēṟōti ṉār, ivi aḷavum, niṟiyum eṇṇumātaliṉ.	(27)
170.		aḷaviṟku niṟiyiṟku moḻimuta lāki
					yuḷaveṉap paṭṭa voṉpatiṟ ṟeḻuttē
					yavitāṅ
					kacatapa veṉṟā namava veṉṟā
					vakara vukaramō ṭaviyeṉa moḻipa.
itu, muṟkūṟiya mūṉṟaṉuḷ, aḷaviṟkum niṟikkum moḻikku mutalāmeḻuttu iṉit teṉkiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: aḷaviṟkum niṟiyiṟkum moḻi mutalāki uḷa veṉap paṭṭa oṉpatiṟ ṟeḻuttē - aḷavup peyarkkum niṟip peyarkkum moḻikku mutalāyuḷḷaṉa veṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭaṉa oṉpateḻuttukkaḷ; avitām kacatapa eṉṟā namava eṉṟā akaravukaramōṭu aviyeṉa moḻipa-avveḻuttukkaḷtām ka  - ca - ta - pa - kkaḷum na - ma- va - kkaḷum akara ukaramumākiya aviyeṉṟu kūṟuvar pulavar eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam:	kalam, cāṭi, tūti, pāṉi, nāḻi, maṇṭi, vaṭṭi, akal, uḻakku: ivi aḷavu. kaḻañcu, cīrakam, toṭi, palam, niṟi, mā, vari, anti: ivi niṟi. niṟikku ukara mutaṟpeyar vantuḻik kāṇka. iṉi, uḷaveṉappaṭṭa eṉṟataṉāṉē uḷaveṉappaṭāta ṉavum uḷa. avi, immi ōraṭi iṭā eṉa variyaṟi kūṟāta ṉavuṅ koḷka. iṉṉum itaṉāṉē, tēyavaḻakkāy oruñār, orutuvali eṉpaṉavuṅ koḷka. 25iṅṅaṉam variyaṟi kūṟiṉār, akattōttiṉuḷ muṭipu kūṟiyavaḻi atikārattāṉ vaṉkaṇattiṉmēṟ cellātu oḻinta kaṇattiṉuñ cellumeṉṟaṟku. eṇṇuppeyar variyaṟi yiṉmiyiṟ kūṟārāyiṉār.	(28)
171.	īṟiyaṉ maruṅki ṉiviyivaṟ ṟiyalpeṉak
					kūṟiya kiḷavip pallā ṟellā
					meyttalip paṭṭa vaḻakkoṭu civaṇi
					yottavi yuriya puṇarmoḻi niliyē.
iḵtu - ivvōttiṟkup puṟaṉaṭi; eṭuttōttāṉum ilēcāṉum muṭiyātaṉavaṟṟiṟku ituvē ōttākaliṉ.
itaṉ poruḷ: īṟu iyal maruṅkiṉ- uyirum puḷḷiyum iṟuti yākiya coṟkaḷ varumoḻiyoṭu kūṭi naṭakkumiṭattu, ivaṟṟu iyalpu  iviyeṉak kūṟiya kiḷavip pallāṟellām - im moḻikaḷiṉ muṭipu iviyeṉak kūṟi muṭikkappaṭṭa coṟkaḷi ṉuṭiya avvāṟṟāṉ muṭiyātuniṉṟa palavaki muṭipukaḷellām, meyt talippaṭṭa vaḻakkoṭu civaṇi-uṇmiyit talippaṭṭa vaḻakkoṭu kūṭi, puṇarmoḻinili ottavi uriya - puṇarum moḻikaḷiṉ nilimikkaṭ poruntiṉavi uriyavām eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam:	naṭañeḷḷā eṉa uyirīṟākiya muṉṉilik kiḷavi meṉkaṇattōṭu iyalpāy muṭintatu. maṇṇu koṟṟā maṇṇukkoṟṟā, maṉṉukoṟṟā maṉṉuk koṟṟā, uḷḷukoṟṟā uḷḷukkoṟṟā, kollu koṟṟā kollukkoṟṟā eṉpaṉa puḷḷiyiṟuti muṉṉilikkiḷavi ukaram peṟṟum uṟaḻntum muṭintaṉa. uriñuñeḷḷā : iḵtu auveṉa varūum (eḻu. 152) eṉpataṉ oḻipu. patakkanāṉāḻi, patakkamunnāḻi eṉa ivi ēyeṉ cāriyi peṟātu akkup peṟṟu ataṉ iṟuti meymmici yoṭuṅ keṭṭup puṇarntaṉa. vāṭṭāṉi tōṟṟaṇṭi eṉpaṉa takaram vantuḻit tirintu neṭiyataṉ muṉṉar oṟṟuk keṭātu niṉṟaṉa. cīrakari eṉpataṉic cīrakam ariyeṉa niṟuttik kakara voṟṟiṉ mēlēṟiṉa akarattiyum makaravoṟṟi yuṅ keṭuttu ari yeṉpataṉ akaratti yēṟṟi muṭikka. itu niṟip peyar. oru māvari yeṉpataṉi oru māvari eṉa niṟutti, varumoḻi akaraṅ keṭuttu orumāriyeṉa muṭikka. kalavari yeṉpataṉik kalari eṉa muṭikka. 26akara makaraṅ keṭuttu nākaṇi yeṉap piṟavum varuvaṉavellām ic cūttirattāṉ muṭikka.			(29)
172.	palaraṟi coṉmuṉ yāva reṉṉum
					peyariṭi vakaraṅ keṭutalu mēṉi
					yoṉṟaṟi coṉmuṉ yāteṉ viṉāviṭi
					yoṉṟiya vakaram varutalu miraṇṭu
					maruviṉ pāttiyiṟ ṟiriyumaṉ payiṉṟē.
itu, marūuccoṉ muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: palaraṟi coṉmuṉ yāvareṉṉum peyariṭi vakaraṅ keṭutalum - palari yaṟiyum avar mutaliya colliṉ muṉṉar varukiṉṟa yāvareṉṉum peyar iṭiyil vakaraṅ keṭutalum, ēṉi oṉṟaṟi coṉmuṉ yāteṉ viṉā iṭi oṉṟiya vakaram varutalum - oḻinta oṉṟaṉi aṟiyum atu mutaliya colliṉ muṉṉar varukiṉṟa yāteṉṉum viṉāccol iṭiyilē uyiroṭu poruntiya vakaram varutalum, iraṇṭum - ākiya avi yiraṇṭum, maruviṉ pāttiyiṉ maṉ payiṉṟu tiriyum - maru muṭipiṉ pakutiyiṭattu mikavum payiṉṟu tiriyum eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam: avar yār eṉavum, atu yāvatu eṉavum varum. avar yāvareṉpatu vakaraṅ keṭṭu avar yāreṉa niṉṟa vaḻi, yāa reṉṉum viṉāviṉ kiḷavi (co. 210) eṉṟa viṉi yiyaluṭ kūṟum viṉikkuṟippuc collām piṟaveṉiṉ, ākātu; avvakaraṅ keṭṭā lum īṇṭu yāvareṉṉum peyart taṉmi yāyē niṟṟaliṉ. atu peṟṟavā ṟeṉṉi yeṉiṉ, īṇṭup palaraṟi coṉmuṉ vanta yāvareṉpataṉ vakaram keṭumeṉavē, ēṉi avaṉ avaḷ eṉṉum irupāl muṉṉum yāvareṉpatu vārāteṉṟum atu tirintu maruvāy niṟkumeṉṟuṅ kūṟutalāṉum, yāvareṉṉum peyariṭi eṉpataṉāṉum peṟṟām. itaṉāṉē avaṉyāvar, avaḷ yāvar eṉṟāṟ pālvaḻuvā meṉpatu peṟṟām. itaṉi yāvaṉ yāvaḷ yāvareṉṉu, māvayiṉ mūṉṟōṭu (co. 162) eṉap peyarāka ōtiyavāṟṟāṉ uṇarka. aṉṟiyum yāreṉṉum viṉāviṉ kiḷavi muppāṟkum urit teṉṟu yāreṉṉum viṉā viṉikkuṟippiṉi avaṉyār, avaḷyār, avaryār eṉa muppāṟkum oppa urimi kūṟuta lāṉum atu vēṟeṉpatu peṟṟām. atu viṉiyiyaluḷ ōtiṉamiyāṉum viṉāviṟ kēṟṟal (co. 66) eṉap payaṉiliyāka ōtiṉamiyāṉum vēṟāyiṟṟu.
iṉi yāryārk kaṇṭē yuvappar eṉap palaraṟi coṉ muṉṉaraṉṟi iyalpāka vanta yāreṉpatu yāṇṭu aṭaṅku meṉiṉ, atuvum yāri yārikkaṇṭeṉa urupu viritaliṉ yāvari eṉṉum vakaraṅkeṭṭa peyarēyām. aṅṅaṉam nili moḻi varumoḻiyāy niṟṟal payiṉṟu eṉṟataṉāṟ koḷka. itaṉāṉē, yāvatu naṉṟeṉa vuṇarār māṭṭum (kuṟun. 78) eṉa ēṉi oṉṟaṟicollum nilimoḻiyāy niṟṟal koḷka. iṉṉum itaṉāṉē, yāravar, yāvatatu eṉa ivviru collum nilimoḻiyāy varutal koḷka.	(30)
tokimarapu muṟṟiṟṟu.
kaṇēcayar aṭikkuṟippukaḷ:
1.	ka ca ta pa mutaliya moḻimēṟṟōṉṟum melleḻutteṉṟatu.              viḷa + kōṭu eṉpaṉa puṇaruṅkāl varumoḻik kakaramnōkki viḷaṅkōṭu eṉa melleḻuttu mikutaṟkēyaṉṟi, maram kuṟitu eṉpaṉa puṇaruṅkāl kakaram mutaliya nōkki melleḻuttut tiritaṟkum viti eṉka. ic cūttirattiṉ karuttu uyirīṟṟiṉmuṉ ka ca ta pa varuṅkāl avvavaṟṟiṉ iṉam mikum eṉpatum nilimoḻi yīṟṟiluḷḷa melleḻuttukkaḷ annāṉkum varuṅkāl avvavviṉamākat tiriyum eṉpatumē. 
2.	viḷakkuṟumi ēḻāvatu; viḷakkuṟittāṉ iraṇṭāvatu.
3.	eṭuttōttu eṉṟatu - cūttiratti. vitikaḷi eṭuttōtuvatu eṉpatu karuttu. ilēcu eṉṟatu - miki mutaliyavaṟṟi. iṉi ellām eṉṟa taṉāl uyirkkaṇamāyi ṉoṟṟiraṭṭiyum uṭampaṭumey peṟṟum uyirēṟiyum muṭiyuṅ karuvit tiripukaḷ tiripeṉappaṭā ivviyalpiṉkaṇ eṉṟu uṇarka eṉa ōtiṉamiyāṉē karuvit tiripiṉatum, ceykiyiṉatum vēṟupāṭu iṉitu aṟiyappaṭum.
4.	kāy mey eṉṉumoḻikaḷi iṅkē īreḻuttoru moḻiyeṉṟu koṇṭa nacciṉārkkiṉiyar `ōreḻuttoru moḻi īreḻuttoru moḻi' eṉṉuñ cūttirattu ivi mutaliyavaṟṟi oṟṟut taḷḷik koḷḷa vēṇṭumeṉṟatu avarukkē yuṭaṉpāṭaṉmiyik kāṭṭum. eḻuttiyalpu nōkki īṇṭuk kūṟiṉāreṉiṉ? āṇṭum avvāṟē kūṟiviṭutal poruttameṉpatu avarkku muṭaṉpāṭātal kāṇka. 
5.	ceyki - puṇarcci; karuvic cūttiramaṉṟu eṉṟapaṭi.
6.	muṉṉiṉṟāṉ toḻiluṇarttuvaṉa muṉṉili viṉimuṟṟu. muṉṉiṉṟāṉit toḻiṟpaṭuttuvaṉa ēval viṉimuṟṟu. ivi av viraṇṭaṟkum vēṟupāṭu. 
7.	kuṟṟiyalukarattiṟuti. 
8.	tiripu - vikāram.
9.	eṭṭi kāviti eṉpaṉa aracaraḷikkum ciṟappup peyar (paṭṭap peyar).
10.	cēṉāvariyarkkum ituvē karuttātal ac cūttirivuri yāṉuṇarka.
11.	puḷḷiyiṟutiyuḷ kuṟṟiyalukaravīṟum aṭaṅkum. pāmpu mutaliya kuṟṟiya lukara vīṟu.
12.	nāykōṭpaṭṭāṉ eṉpuḻi. tantoḻil. koḷḷutal. tammiṉākiya toḻil - koḷḷappaṭutal.
13.	paṭṭāṅku - uṇmi. uṇmiyāvatu - vikāramiṉṟi iyalpāy niṟṟal ātaliṉ meymmi iyalpu eṉṟapaṭi.
14.	tiripu - vikāram.
15.	uṟaḻntu muṭintatu.
16.	iraṇṭīṟu - i, ai.
17.	āṅkavi - aṅkavi eṉpataṉ tiripupōlum.
18.	iṭiccollōṭu oṭṭuppaṭṭuniṟṟalum eṉṟa vākkiyam īṇṭup poruttamākak kāṇavilli. atukol eṉpatit toṭarmoḻi eṉṟamiyāl ataṟkēṟpa atukoṟōḻi eṉpatu iṭiccol lōṭu oṭṭuppaṭṭu niṉṟatu eṉṟu kūṟiyirukkalām pōlum. āyka.
19.	iraṇṭaṟkum - āṟaṉurupiṟkum nāṉkaṉurupiṟkum.
20.	puḷḷi eṉṟataṉāl puḷḷi peṟum meyyīṟum, kuṟṟiyalukaravīṟuṅ koḷḷappaṭum. itu ematu karuttu. 
21.	ceviṭṭari eṉpatu - `tammakappaṭṭa mutti varuṅkālan tōṉṟiṉ ottateṉpa... ēyeṉcāriyi' eṉpataṉāl ceviṭṭēyari eṉa ē eṉcāriyi peṟṟu varavēṇṭum. aṅṅaṉam peṟavēṇṭiya iṭattup peṟātu ceviṭṭari eṉa niṟṟaliṉ, itaṟku iccūttira viti vēṇṭiya tāyiṟṟu. peṟāmal varuvaṉavaṟṟiṟkē oṭṭutaṟkoḻukiya vaḻakkaṉṟu eṉṉum viti koḷḷappaṭum. ātaliṉ itu, ataṉāṟ koḷḷappaṭā teṉka.
22.	pāku eṉṟatu - pākkiṉi eṉṟār. `kuṟṟapāku koḻippavar' eṉpatu pōṉṟa ilakkiya vaḻakku nōkki.
23.	vitittu eṉpatu virittu eṉṟiruntirukka vēṇṭum.
24.	iṉṉum oru paṉip poḻutu niṟkiṟatu eṉpataṉāṉ, paṉi  aḷavup peyarātal peṟappaṭum.
25.	iṅṅaṉam variyaṟi kūṟiṉār, akattōttiṉuḷ muṭivu kūṟiyavaḻi atikārattāṉ vaṉkaṇattiṉmēṟ cellātu oḻinta kaṇattiṉuñ cellumeṉṟaṟku, eṉpataṟku. "aḷavum niṟiyum" eṉa eṭuttuk kūṟuñ cūttiraṅkaḷuḷ, vaṉkaṇattiṉ māttiram cellāmal ēṉiya kaṇattiṉum ac cūttiraviti cellumeṉṟaṟku eṉpatu karuttu.
26.	nākam eṉpataṉ akaramakaraṅ keṭuttu eṉka. 

urupiyal
vēṟṟumiyurupukaḷ peyarkaḷōṭu poruntum muṟimi yiṉi yuṇarttuvatu urupiyalākum. itaṉkaṇ muppatu cūttiraṅkaḷ uḷḷaṉa. peyarum ataṉāl ēṟkappaṭum vēṟṟumiyurupum ākiya avviraṇṭiṟkum iṭiyē varum cāriyikaḷ iviyeṉpatum, vēṟṟumiyurupiṉi yēṟkuṅkāl peyarkaḷ peṟum iyalpum tiripumāvaṉa ivi yeṉpatum ivviyalil vakuttu viḷakkappeṟṟuḷḷaṉa.
uyirkaḷuḷ a ā u ū ē au eṉṉum āṟiṉiyum īṟākavuṭiya peyarkaḷum, meykaḷuḷ ñakara nakaravīṟṟup peyarkaḷum, tev eṉṉum vakaravīṟṟup peyarum, makaravīṟṟup peyarkaḷ cilavum, kuṟṟiyalukara vīṟṟup peyarkaḷum urupēṟ kuṅkāl iṭiyē iṉcāriyi peṟuvaṉa.
paṉmip poruḷik karutiṉa akara vīṟṟup peyarkaḷum, yā eṉṉum ākāravīṟṟu viṉāppeyarum, avi, ivi, uvi, yāvi, av, iv, uv eṉpaṉavum vaṟṟuccāriyi peṟṟu urupēṟpaṉa. yāvi eṉṉum viṉāppeyar vaṟṟuc cāriyi peṟuṅkāl appeyariṉ īṟṟil niṉṟa aikāramum ataṉālūrappaṭṭa vakarameyyum keṭṭu muṭiyum. ellām eṉṉum potuppeyar aḵṟiṇikkaṇvariṉ vaṟṟuccāriyiyum, uyar tiṇikkaṇvariṉ nam cāriyiyum peṟuvatuṭaṉ vēṟṟumi yurupiṉ iṟutiyil um cāriyiyum peṟṟumuṭiyum.
atu, itu, utu eṉavarum ukaravīṟṟuc cuṭṭuppeyarum, ēḻ eṉṉum ḻakaravīṟṟu eṇṇuppeyarum, kuṟṟiyalukaravīṟṟuḷ varum ellā eṇṇuppeyarkaḷum, yātu eṉṉum viṉāp peyarum, aḵtu, iḵtu, uḵtu eṉavarum cuṭṭup peyarkaḷum aṉcāriyi peṟṟu urupēṟpaṉavām. aṉcāriyi peṟuṅkāl atu, itu, utu eṉpaṉa iṟutiyukaraṅ keṭṭu muṭivaṉa; aḵtu, iḵtu, uḵtu eṉpaṉa āytaṅkeṭṭu muṭivaṉa. paḵteṉpataṉi yiṟutiyākavuṭiya orupatumutal eṇpatu variyuḷḷa eṇṇuppeyarkaḷ eṭṭum, aṉcāriyiyēyaṉṟi āṉcāriyi peṟutalum uṇṭu. āṉcāriyi peṟuṅkāl nilimoḻiyiluḷḷa paḵteṉpataṉ kaṇ pakarameymaṭṭum niṟka aḵteṉpatu keṭum.
ōkāra vīṟṟuppeyar urupēṟkuṅkāl oṉcāriyi peṟum.
akara ākāra vīṟṟu marappeyarkaḷ ēḻāmurupu peṟumvaḻi attuccāriyipeṟum. makaravīṟṟup peyarkaḷ urupēṟkuṅkāl attuccāriyi peṟuvaṉa. aḻaṉ, puḻaṉ eṉpaṉa attum iṉṉum peṟṟu uṟaḻvaṉa.
nī eṉṉum peyar niṉ eṉattirintu urupēṟkum. makara vīṟṟuḷ num eṉpatum tām, nām, yām eṉpaṉavum attum iṉṉum peṟātu iyalpāy muṭivaṉa. yām eṉṉum peyaril yakarattiṉmēlēṟiya ākāram ekaramākat tiriya aṅku niṉṟa yakaram keṭum. tām, nām eṉpaṉa mutalkuṟuki muṟiyē tam nam eṉa niṟkum. ellārum eṉṉum paṭarkkic colliṭattum ellīrum eṉṉum muṉṉilic colliṭattum iṟutiyil niṉṟa um eṉpatu keṭa, avvirupeyarum muṟiyē tam cāriyiyum nam cāriyiyum peṟṟu urupēṟkum. avipeṟum urupiṉ piṉṉar um cāriyi vantu poruntum
 ṉakara vīṟṟuḷ tāṉ eṉpatu mutalkuṟukit taṉ eṉṟum yāṉ eṉpatu mutaṟkaṇ niṉṟa yakaraṅkeṭṭu ataṉiyūrntu niṉṟa ākāram ekaramāki eṉ eṉṟum tirintu urupēṟkum.
neṭiṟṟoṭark kuṟṟiyalukaraṅkaḷuḷ (ṭaṟa) oṟṟi raṭṭikkuñ coṟkaḷ urupēṟkuṅkāl iṉcāriyipeṟātu iyalpāvaṉa.
kuṟṟukaravīṟṟut ticippeyarkaḷ kaṇṇurupiṉi ēṟkuṅ kāl iṉcāriyi peṟātu iyalpātalumuṇṭu. ivvāṟu iyalpāyavaḻi iṟutiyiluḷḷa kuṟṟiyalukaram tāṉ ūrntu niṉṟa meyyōṭu cērakkeṭum.
iṅku eṭutturikkappaṭṭa peyarkaḷallāta ēṉiya peyarkaḷ cāriyi peṟṟē urupēṟṟal vēṇṭumeṉṉum variyaṟiyillātaṉavām. avi cāriyi peṟṟum peṟātum muṭivaṉavām.
- ka. veḷḷivāraṇaṉār 
nūlvarici 10, pak. 176-178

āṟāvatu
urupiyal
173.		aā uū ēau veṉṉu
					mappā lāṟa ṉilimoḻi muṉṉar
					vēṟṟumi yurupiṟ kiṉṉē cāriyi
eṉpatu cūttiram.
urupukaḷōṭu peyar puṇarum iyalpu uṇarttiṉamiyiṉ, ivvōttu urupiya leṉṉum peyarttāyiṟṟu. mēl tokut tup puṇarttataṉi īṇṭu virittup puṇarkkiṉṟārā kaliṉ, ivvōttut tokimarapōṭu iyipuṭittāyiṟṟu.
iccūttiram - akara mutaliya īṟṟāṉ varum āṟu īṟṟuc coṟkaḷ niṉṟu iṉ peṟṟu urupiṉōṭu puṇarumāṟu kūṟu kiṉṟatu. urupiṉ poruḷpaṭa varum puṇarcci mēṟkūṟupa.
itaṉ poruḷ: a ā u ū ē au eṉṉum appāl āṟaṉ nili moḻi muṉṉar - a ā u ū ē au eṉṟu collappaṭukiṉṟa akkūṟṟu āṟaṉiyum īṟākavuṭiya nilimoḻikaḷiṉ muṉṉar varukiṉṟa, vēṟṟumi yurupiṟku iṉṉē cāriyi - vēṟṟumi yurupukaṭku iṭiyē varuñ cāriyi iṉ cāriyiyē eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam: viḷaviṉi viḷaviṉoṭu viḷaviṟku viḷaviṉatu viḷaviṉkaṇ eṉavum, palāviṉi palāviṉoṭu palāviṟku palāviṉatu palāviṉkaṇ eṉavum, kaṭuviṉi kaṭuviṉoṭu kaṭuviṟku kaṭuviṉatu kaṭuviṉkaṇ eṉavum, taḻūuviṉi taḻūuviṉoṭu taḻūuviṟku taḻūuviṉatu taḻūuviṉkaṇ eṉavum, cēviṉi cēviṉoṭu cēviṟku cēviṉatu cēviṉkaṇ eṉavum, vuviṉi vuviṉoṭu vuviṟku vuviṉatu vuviṉ kaṇ eṉavum varum. ivvāṟē ceyki yaṟintu oṭṭuka.
iṉṉeṉa varūum vēṟṟumi yurupiṟ
kiṉṉeṉ cāriyi yiṉmi vēṇṭum	(eḻut. 131)
eṉavē, ēṉiya iṉ peṟumeṉṟaliṉ, 1ñanamayava (eḻut. 144) eṉpataṉāṉ iyalpeṉṟatu vilakkiṟṟām.	(1)
174.		pallavi nutaliya vakara viṟupeyar
					vaṟṟoṭu civaṇa lecca miṉṟē.
iḵtu iṉcāriyi vilakki vaṟṟu vakuttaliṉ, eytiyatu vilakkip piṟituviti vakukkiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: pallavi nutaliya peyariṟu akaram-paṉmip poruḷik karutiṉa peyarkaḷiṉ iṟuti niṉṟa akaram, vaṟṟoṭu civaṇal eccamiṉṟu-vaṟṟuc cāriyiyōṭu poruntutali oḻitalilli eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam:	pallavaṟṟi pallavaṟṟoṭu, palavaṟṟi palavaṟ ṟoṭu, cillavaṟṟi cillavaṟṟoṭu, cilavaṟṟi cilavaṟṟoṭu, uḷḷavaṟṟi uḷḷavaṟṟoṭu, illavaṟṟi illavaṟ ṟoṭu eṉa oṭṭuka.
eccamiṉṟi eṉṟataṉāṉē, 2mēl iṉ peṟṟaṉa piṟa cāri yiyum peṟutal koḷka. 	nilātti, tulātti, makatti eṉa varum. iṉṉum itaṉāṉē, pallavi nutaliyavaṟṟiṉkaṇ mūṉṟā murupu vaṟṟup peṟṟē muṭital koḷka.	(2)
175. yāveṉ viṉāvu māyiya ṟiriyātu.
iḵtu - ākāra īṟṟuḷ oṉṟaṟku eytiyatu vilakkip piṟitu viti vakuttatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: yāveṉ viṉāvum - yāveṉṟu collappaṭum ākāra īṟṟu viṉāppeyarum, āyiyal 
tiriyātu - muṟkūṟiya vaṟṟup pēṟṟiṟ ṟiriyātu eṉṟavāṟu.
yāvaṟṟi yāvaṟṟoṭu eṉa varum.	(3)
176.		cuṭṭumuta lukara maṉṉoṭu civaṇi
					yoṭṭiya meyyoḻit tukaraṅ keṭumē.
iḵtu ukara īṟṟiṟku eytiyatu vilakkip piṟituviti vakuttatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: cuṭṭu mutal ukaram aṉṉoṭu civaṇi - cuṭṭeḻuttiṉi mutalākavuṭiya ukara īṟṟuccol aṉ cāriyi yoṭu porunti, oṭṭiya mey oḻittu ukaraṅ keṭum - tāṉ poruntiya meyyi niṟutti ukaraṅ keṭum eṉṟavāṟu.
ataṉi ataṉoṭu, itaṉi itaṉoṭu, utaṉi utaṉoṭu eṉa varum. 3atiṉi atiṉoṭu eṉṟāṟ pōlvaṉa marūu muṭippuḻi muṭintaṉa. oṭṭiya eṉṟataṉāṟ, cuṭṭumutal ukaramaṉṟip piṟa ukaramum uyir varuvaḻik keṭuvaṉa koḷka. avi katavu, kaḷavu, kaṉavu eṉa niṟutti, aḻakitu illi eṉa varuvittu ukaraṅ keṭuttu muṭikka. ivaṟṟi vakara īṟākki ukaram peṟṟaṉaveṉṟu kōṭu meṉiṉ, vaḻakkiṉkaṇṇuñ ceyyuṭkaṇṇum payiṉṟu varum vakara īṟukaḷi oḻittu āciriyar vakarak kiḷavi nāṉmoḻi yīṟṟatu (eḻu. 81) eṉṟāṟ pōla varintōtal kuṉṟak kūṟalām. ātaliṉ, avi ukara īṟeṉṟē koḷka. avi, celavu varavu taravu uṇavu kaṉavu eṉa vaḻakkiṉkaṇṇum, puṉka ṇuṭittāṟ puṇarvu (kuṟaḷ. 1152), pāṭaṟiyā tāṉi yiravu, kaṇṇārak kāṇak katavu (muttoḷ. 42) eṉac ceyyuṭkaṇṇum payiṉṟu varumāṟu uṇarka.		(4)
177.		cuṭṭumuta lākiya viye ṉiṟuti
					vaṟṟoṭu civaṇi niṟṟalu murittē.
iḵtu - aikāra īṟṟiṟku muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: cuṭṭu mutalākiya aiye ṉiṟuti - cuṭṭeḻuttiṉi mutalāka vuṭiya aikāra īṟṟuccol, vaṟṟoṭu civaṇi niṟṟalum urittu - vaṟṟuc cāriyiyoṭu porunti niṟṟalum urittu eṉṟavāṟu. ummiyāṉ vaṟṟoṭu cila urupiṉkaṇ iṉ cāriyi peṟṟu niṟṟalum urittu eṉṟavāṟu.
aviyaṟṟi aviyaṟṟoṭu, iviyaṟṟi iviyaṟ ṟoṭu, uviyaṟṟi uviyaṟṟoṭu eṉa oṭṭuka. iṅṅaṉam aikāram niṟka, vaṟṟu vantuḻi vaḵkāṉ meykeṭa (eḻut. 122) eṉpataṉāṉ muṭikka.
iṉi, ummiyāṉ aviyaṟṟiṟku, aviyaṟṟiṉkaṇ eṉa nāṉkāvatum ēḻāvatum, vaṟṟum iṉṉum peṟṟu vantavāṟu kāṇka. iṉi, oṉṟeṉa muṭittal eṉpataṉāṟ pallavi nutaliya akara īṟṟiṟkum ivviraṇṭu urupiṉkaṇ vaṟṟum iṉṉum koṭuttup palavaṟṟiṟku, palavaṟṟiṉkaṇ eṉa muṭikka. itumēl varuvaṉa vaṟṟiṟkum okkum.	(5)

178.		yāveṉ viṉāvi ṉiye ṉiṟutiyu
					māyiya ṟiriyā teṉmaṉār pulava
					rāvayiṉ vakara miyoṭuṅ keṭumē.
ituvum atu.
itaṉ poruḷ: 4yāveṉ viṉāviṉ aiyeṉ iṟutiyum - yāveṉṉum viṉāviṉiyuṭiya aikāra īṟṟuc collum, āyiyal tiriyātu eṉmaṉār pulavar-muṟkūṟiya cuṭṭu mutal aikāram pōl vaṟṟuppeṟum avviyalpiṉ tiriyāteṉṟu colluvar āciriyar; āvayiṉ vakaram aiyoṭuṅ keṭum-avvīṟṟiṭattu vakaram aikārattoṭu kūṭak keṭum eṉṟavāṟu.
 yāvaṟṟi, yāvaṟṟoṭu eṉa oṭṭuka. vakaram vaṟṟumici yoṟṟeṉṟu keṭuvataṉik kēṭu ōtiya mikiyāṉē, piṟa aikāramum vaṟṟuppeṟutal koḷka. kariyavaṟṟi kariyavaṟṟoṭu, neṭiyavaṟṟi neṭiyavaṟṟoṭu, kuṟiyavaṟṟi kuṟiyavaṟṟoṭu eṉa ellāvaṟṟōṭum oṭṭuka. ivi karumi neṭumi kuṟumi eṉṉum paṇpuppeyaraṉṟik kariyavi neṭiyavi kuṟiyavi eṉap paṇpukoḷ peyarāy niṟṟaliṉ, vakara aikāram keṭuttu vaṟṟuccāriyi koṭuttu muṭikkappaṭṭaṉa. ivi aimpālaṟiyum paṇpu tokumoḻi aṉmi uṇarka.	(6)
179.		nīye  ṉorupeyar neṭumutal kuṟuku
					māvayi ṉakara moṟṟā kummē.
iḵtu īkāra īṟu iṉṉavāṟu puṇarumeṉkiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: nī eṉ oru peyar neṭu mutal kuṟukum - nīyeṉṉum oru peyar taṉmēl niṉṟa neṭiyatākiya īkāraṅ kuṟuki ikaramām; āvayiṉ ṉakaram oṟṟākum - avviṭattu varum ṉakaram oṟṟāy niṟkum eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam: niṉṉi niṉṉoṭu niṉakku eṉac ceykiyaṟintu oṭṭuka. niṉakku eṉpataṟku, āṟa ṉurupiṉum nāṉka ṉurupiṉum (eḻu. 161) valleḻuttu mutaliya (eḻu. 114) eṉpaṉa koṇarntu muṭikka. niṉatu eṉpataṟku, āṟa ṉurupi ṉakarak kiḷavi (eḻu. 115) eṉpataṉāṉ muṭikka. niṉ eṉpatu nī eṉpataṉ vēṟoru peyarō eṉak karututali vilakkutaṟku oru peyar eṉṟār. peyar kuṟukum eṉṉātu mutal kuṟukum eṉṟatu, appeyariṉ 5eḻuttiṉkaṇṇatu kuṟukka meṉṟaṟku. neṭumutal eṉavē nakaraṅ kuṟukutali vilakkiṟṟu. 6uyirmey yoṟṟumi paṟṟi neṭiyatu mutalāyiṟṟu. uṭimiyu miṉmiyu moṭuvayi ṉokkum (eḻu. 123) eṉṟataṉi nōkki oṭuviṭattuc cāriyi peṟṟē vanta atikāratti māṟṟutaṟkuc cāriyip pēṟṟiṭi 7eḻuttup pēṟu kūṟiṉār.	(7)
180.	ōkāra viṟutik koṉṉē cāriyi.
iḵtu - ōkāra īṟu iṉṉavāṟu puṇarumeṉkiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ:  ōkāra iṟutikku oṉṉē cāriyi-ōkāra īṟṟiṟku iṭivaruñ cāriyi oṉcāriyi eṉṟavāṟu.
kōoṉi, kōoṉoṭu eṉa oṭṭuka. oṉṉē eṉṟa ēkārattāṟ perumpāṉmiyāka varuñ cāriyi oṉṉē; ciṟupāṉmi iṉ cāriyi varumeṉṟu koḷka. oṉṟātu niṉṟa kōviṉi yaṭarkka vanta (cīvaka. 316) eṉavum, kōviṉi kōviṉoṭu, cōviṉi cōviṉoṭu, ōviṉi ōviṉoṭu eṉavum varum.
ōveṉpatu matakunīr tāṅkum palaki.	(8)
181.		aā eṉṉum marappeyark kiḷavik
					kattoṭuñ civaṇu mēḻa ṉurupē.
iḵtu - akara ākāra īṟṟiṟku eytiyataṉmēṟ ciṟappuviti kūṟukiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: a ā eṉṉum marappeyark kiḷavikku - a ā veṉṟu collappaṭum maratti uṇarttukiṉṟa peyarākiya colliṟku, ēḻaṉurupu attoṭuñ civaṇum - ēḻāmurupu iṉṉōṭaṉṟi attōṭum poruntum eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam: viḷavattukkaṇ, palāvattukkaṇ eṉavarum.
valleḻuttu mutaliya (eḻu. 114) eṉpataṉāṉ valle ḻuttuk koṭuttut teṟṟeṉ ṟaṟṟē (eḻu. 133) eṉpataṉāṉ attiṉakara makaramuṉik (eḻu. 125) keṭāmic ceyki ceytu muṭikka.	(9)
182.	ñanaveṉ puḷḷik kiṉṉē cāriyi.
itu, puḷḷiyīṟṟuḷ ñakara īṟum nakara īṟum muṭiyumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: ñana eṉ puḷḷikku iṉṉē cāriyi - ña na veṉṟu collappaṭukiṉṟa puḷḷiyīṟukaṭku varuñcāriyi iṉ cāriyi eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam: uriñiṉi uriñiṉoṭu, poruniṉi poruni ṉoṭu eṉa oṭṭuka.	(10)
183.		cuṭṭumutal vakara miyu meyyuṅ
					keṭṭa viṟuti yiyaṟṟiri piṉṟē.
itu, nāṉku moḻikku īṟām vakara īṟṟuṭ cuṭṭumutal vakara īṟṟiṟku muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: cuṭṭu mutal vakaram-av iv uv eṉṉuñ cuṭṭeḻuttiṉi mutalākavuṭiya vakara īṟṟuc col, aiyum meyyuṅ keṭṭa iṟutiyiyal tiripiṉṟu-aikāramum aikārattāṉ ūrappaṭṭa meyyuṅ keṭṭu vaṟṟup peṟṟu muṭinta yāvi (eḻu. 178) eṉṉum aikāra īṟṟuc colliyalpil tiripiṉṟi vaṟṟuppeṟṟu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam: avaṟṟi avaṟṟoṭu, ivaṟṟi ivaṟṟoṭu, uvaṟṟi uvaṟṟoṭu eṉa oṭṭuka.	(11)
184.	ēṉi vakara miṉṉoṭu civaṇum.
iḵtu - eytāta teytuvittatu; peyarkkē yaṉṟi uric col vakarattiṟku muṭipu kūṟutaliṉ.
itaṉ poruḷ: ēṉi vakaram iṉṉoṭu civaṇum - oḻinta uric col vakaram iṉcāriyiyōṭu porunti muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.
8tevviṉi, tevviṉoṭu eṉa oṭṭuka. iḵtu uric collāyiṉum 9paṭuttalōciyāṟ peyarā yiṟṟu. 	(12)
185.	maḵkāṉ puḷḷimu ṉattē cāriyi.
itu, makara īṟu puṇarumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: maḵkāṉ puḷḷimuṉ attē cāriyi - makaramākiya puḷḷiyīṟṟuc coṉmuṉ varuñ cāriyi attuc cāriyi eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam:	maratti marattoṭu, nukatti nukattoṭu eṉa oṭṭuka. attē vaṟṟē (eḻu. 133) atti ṉakaram (eḻut. 125) eṉpaṉavaṟṟāṉ muṭikka.	(13)

186.	iṉṉiṭi varūu moḻiyumā ruḷavē.
itu, makara īṟṟiṟ cilavaṟṟiṟku eytiyatu vilakkip piṟitu viti vakukkiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: iṉṉiṭi varūum moḻiyumā ruḷa - makara īṟṟuc coṟkaḷuḷ attē yaṉṟi iṉcāriyi iṭiyē vantu muṭiyuñ coṟkaḷum uḷa eṉṟavāṟu.
mār aci.
utāraṇam: urumiṉi urumiṉoṭu, tirumiṉi tirumiṉoṭu eṉa voṭṭuka.	(14)
187.	numme ṉiṟuti yiyaṟki yākum.
itu, makara īṟṟuḷ oṉṟaṟku eytiyatu vilakkip piṟituviti vakukkiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: nummeṉ iṟuti iyaṟki yākum - nummeṉṉum makara īṟu mēṟkūṟiya attum iṉṉum peṟātu iyalpāka muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam: 	 nummi, nummoṭu, numakku, nummiṉ, numatu, nuṅkaṇ eṉavarum.
numakku numatu eṉpaṉavaṟṟiṟku āṟa ṉurupiṉum nāṉka ṉurupiṉum (eḻu. 161) nummeṉ iṟutiyu mannili (eḻu. 162) valleḻuttu mutaliya (eḻu. 114) āṟa ṉurupi ṉakarak kiḷavi (eḻut. 115) eṉpaṉa koṇarntu muṭikka.
nuṅkaṇeṉpataṟku 10mēlic cūttirattu mey eṉṟata ṉāṉ makara voṟṟuk keṭuttu, valleḻuttu mutaliya (eḻu. 114) eṉpataṉāṉ melloṟṟuk koṭukka. iyaṟki yeṉṟār cāriyi peṟāmi karuti.	(15)
188.		tānā meṉṉu makara viṟutiyum
					yāme ṉiṟutiyu mataṉō raṉṉa
					āē ākum yāme ṉiṟuti
					yāvayiṉ yakaramey keṭutal vēṇṭu
					mēṉi yiraṇṭu neṭumutal kuṟukum.
itu, makara īṟṟil muṟkūṟiya muṭipu ovvātaṉavaṟṟiṟku muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: tām nām eṉṉum makara iṟutiyum yāmeṉ iṟutiyum ataṉōraṉṉa - tām nām eṉṟu kūṟappaṭum makara īṟum yām eṉṉum makara īṟum num eṉṉum makara īṟu pōla attum iṉṉum peṟātu muṭitaliyuṭiya; yāmeṉ iṟuti ā e ākum - yā meṉṉum makara īṟṟuc colliṉ ākāram ekāramām; ā vayiṉ yakaramey keṭutal vēṇṭum - avviṭattu niṉṟa yakara mākiya mey keṭutali virumpum āciriyaṉ; ēṉi iraṇṭum neṭumutal kuṟukum - oḻinta tām nām eṉṉum iraṇṭum neṭiya vākiya mutal kuṟukit tam nam eṉa niṟkum eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam: 	 tammi tammoṭu, nammi nammoṭu, emmi em moṭu eṉa āṟu urupōṭum oṭṭuka. āṟaṉurupiṟkum nāṉka ṉurupiṟkuṅ karuvi yaṟintu muṭikka.
mey yeṉṟataṉāṟ piṟavayiṉ meyyum keṭukka. taṅkaṇ naṅkaṇ eṅkaṇ eṉa ēḻaṉurupiṉkaṇ makaraṅ keṭuttu valleḻuttu mutaliya (eḻu. 114) eṉpataṉāṉ melleḻuttuk koṭukka.	(16)
189.		ellā meṉṉu miṟuti muṉṉar
					vaṟṟeṉ cāriyi muṟṟat tōṉṟu
					mummi niliyu miṟuti yāṉa.
itu, makara īṟṟuḷ oṉṟaṟku eytiyatu vilakkip piṟituviti vakukkiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: ellā meṉṉum iṟuti muṉṉar vaṟṟeṉ cāriyi muṟṟat tōṉṟum - ellā meṉṉum makara īṟṟuc coṉ muṉṉar attum iṉṉum aṉṟi vaṟṟeṉṉuñ cāriyi muṭiyat tōṉṟi muṭiyum; umminiliyum iṟutiyāṉa - āṇṭu ummeṉṉuñ cāriyi iṟutikkaṇ nilipeṟum eṉṟavāṟu.
makaram vaṟṟiṉmici yoṟṟeṉak keṭukka.
ellāvaṟṟiyum, ellāvaṟṟiṉum, ellāvaṟṟuk kaṇṇum eṉavarum. muṟṟa veṉṟataṉāṉ 11ēṉi muṟṟukarat tiṟkum ummiṉ ukaraṅ keṭuttuk koḷka. ellāvaṟṟoṭum, ellāvaṟṟukkum ellāvaṟṟatum eṉa varum. muṟṟukaramātaliṉ ēṟi muṭiyā.	(17)
190.	uyartiṇi yāyi ṉammiṭi varumē.
iḵtu  eytiyatu vilakkip piṟituviti vakukkiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: uyartiṇiyāyiṉ - ellāmeṉa niṉṟa makara īṟṟu viravup peyar uyartiṇip peyarāmeṉiṉ, nam iṭivarum - nam meṉṉuñ cāriyi iṭi niṉṟu puṇarum eṉṟavāṟu.
makara īṟṟiṉi mēl vaṟṟiṉmici yoṟṟeṉak keṭutta atikārat tāṟ keṭukka. 12ellā nammiyum, ellā nammiṉum, ellā naṅ kaṇum eṉa ukaram peṟṟum; ellānammoṭum, ellā namakkum, ellā namatum eṉa ukaraṅ keṭṭum makaram niṟkum. ivaṟṟiṟku nam ellāriyum nam ellāroṭum eṉpatu poruḷāka oṭṭuka. itaṟku nammu vakuttatē vēṟupāṭu. īṟāku puḷḷi akaramoṭu niṟṟal (eḻu. 161) nāṉkāvataṟkum āṟāvataṟ kuṅ koḷka.	(18)
191.		ellāru meṉṉum paṭarkki yiṟutiyu
					mellīru meṉṉu muṉṉili yiṟutiyu
					moṟṟu mukaramuṅ keṭumeṉa moḻipa
					niṟṟal vēṇṭum rakarap puḷḷi
					yummi niliyu miṟuti yāṉa
					tammiṭi varūum paṭarkki mēṉa
					nummiṭi varūu muṉṉili moḻikkē.
itu makarīṟṟu uyartiṇip peyarkku muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: ellāru meṉṉum paṭarkki yiṟutiyum - ellāru meṉṉum makara īṟṟu uyartiṇip paṭarkkip peyarum, ellīru meṉṉum muṉṉili yiṟutiyum - ellīru meṉṉum makara īṟṟu uyartiṇi muṉṉilippeyarum, oṟṟum ukaramuṅkeṭumeṉa moḻipa - makara voṟṟum ataṉmuṉṉiṉṟa ukaramuṅkeṭṭu muṭiyumeṉṟu colluvar pulavar; rakarap puḷḷi niṟṟal vēṇṭum - avvukaram ēṟi niṉṟa rakara oṟṟukkeṭātu niṟṟali virumpum āciriyaṉ; iṟutiyāṉa ummi niliyum-avviru moḻiyiṉi ṟutikkaṇṇum ummeṉṉuñ cāriyi nilipeṟum; paṭarkki mēṉa tam iṭi varūum - paṭarkkic col liṭattut tammuc cāriyi iṭivarum; muṉṉili moḻikku num iṭi varūum - muṉṉilic coṟku nummuc cāriyi iṭi varum eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam:	ellārtammiyum, ellār tammiṉum, ellār taṅkaṇum eṉa ukaram peṟṟum, ellārtammoṭum, ellārtamakkum, ellār tamatum eṉa ukaraṅ keṭṭum, makaram niṟkum. ellīrnummiyum, ellīr nummiṉum, ellīrnuṅkaṇum eṉa ukaram peṟṟum, ellīrnummoṭum, ellīrnumakkum, ellīrnumatum eṉa ukaraṅ keṭṭum makaram niṟkum.
13muṉṉar mey (eḻu. 188) eṉṟa ilēcāṟ koṇṭa makarakkēṭu ivaṟṟiṟkum mēlvaruvaṉavaṟṟiṟkuṅ koḷka.
paṭarkkip peyar muṟkūṟiya vataṉāṉē, rakara īṟṟup paṭark kip peyarum muṉṉilippeyarum, makarīṟṟut taṉmip peyarum, tam num nam eṉṉuñ cāriyi iṭiyē peṟṟu iṟuti ummuccāriyiyum peṟṟu muṭivaṉa koḷka. kariyārtammiyum cāṉṟārtammiyum eṉavum, kariyīr nummiyum cāṉṟīrnummiyum eṉavum, kariyēmnammiyum iruvēm nammiyum eṉavum ellā vurupoṭuñ ceyki yaṟintu oṭṭuka. ukaramum oṟṟum eṉṉātataṉāṉ, ikkāṭṭiyavaṟṟiṟ kellām mūṉṟu urupiṉkaṇṇum ummiṉ ukaraṅkeṭutal koḷka. niṟṟal vēṇṭum rakarap puḷḷi eṉṟataṉāṉē, tammup peṟāmi varumaviyum koḷka. ellārkku mellā nikaḻpavi (kuṟaḷ. 582) eṉavarum.	(19)
192.		tāṉyā ṉeṉṉu māyī riṟutiyu
					mēṉmup peyaroṭum vēṟupā ṭilavē.
itu - ṉakara īṟṟuṭ cilavaṟṟiṟku muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: tāṉ yāṉ eṉṉum āyī riṟutiyum - tāṉ yāṉ eṉṟu collappaṭṭa avviraṇṭu ṉakara īṟum, mēl muppeyarōṭum vēṟupāṭu ilavē-mēl makara īṟṟuṭ kūṟiya mūṉṟu peyarōṭum vēṟupāṭiṉṟit tāṉeṉpatu kuṟukiyum yāṉeṉpataṉ kaṇ ākāram ekaramāy yakara voṟṟuk keṭṭum muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.
taṉṉi eṉṉi eṉa ellāvurupōṭum oṭṭuka; ceyki yaṟintu. oṟṟiraṭṭutal neṭiyataṉ muṉṉar eṉpataṉuḷ ilēcāṟ koḷka.	(20)
193.		aḻaṉē puḻaṉē yāyiru moḻikku
					mattu miṉṉu muḻaṟat tōṉṟa
					lotta teṉpa vuṇaru mōrē.
ituvum atu.
itaṉ poruḷ: aḻaṉē puḻaṉē āyiru moḻikkum - aḻaṉ puḻaṉ ākiya avviru moḻikkum, attum iṉṉum uṟaḻattōṉṟal otta teṉpa - attuc cāriyiyum iṉcāriyiyum māṟi varat tōṉṟutalip poruntiṟ ṟeṉpar, uṇarumōr - aṟivōr eṉṟavāṟu.
aḻatti aḻaṉiṉi, puḻatti puḻaṉiṉi eṉac cey ki yaṟintu ellāvurupiṉōṭum oṭṭuka. ṉakaratti attiṉ mici oṟṟeṉṟu keṭuttu atti ṉakaram (eḻu. 125) eṉpataṉāṉ muṭikka. 14tōṉṟa leṉṟataṉāṉ, evaṉ eṉa niṟutti vaṟṟuk koṭuttu vēṇṭuñceyki ceytu evaṟṟi, evaṟṟoṭu eṉa muṭikka ellāvurupiṉōṭum oṭṭuka. 15eṟṟi eṉpuḻi nili moḻi vakaram itaṉāṟ keṭukka. iṉi otta teṉṟataṉāl ekiṉ eṉa niṟutti, attum iṉṉuṅ koṭuttuc ceyki ceytu ekiṉatti, ekiṉiṉi eṉa oṭṭuka. attu iṉitu icittaliṉ muṟkūṟiṉār.	(21)
194.		aṉṉeṉ cāriyi yēḻa ṉiṟuti
					muṉṉart tōṉṟu miyaṟkit teṉpa.
iḵtu - ēḻeṉṉum eṇṇup peyar aṉcāriyi peṟṟup puṇarka eṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: aṉṉeṉ cāriyi ēḻuṉiṟuti muṉṉart tōṉṟum iyaṟkit teṉpa - aṉṉeṉṉuñ cāriyi ēḻeṉ ṉum eṇṇup peyariṉ muṉṉē tōṉṟum iyalpiṉi yuṭit teṉṟu kūṟuvar āciriyar eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam: ēḻaṉi, ēḻaṟku, ēḻaṉiṉ eṉka. ēṉi yurupukaḷōṭuñ ceyki yaṟintu oṭṭuka. cāriyi muṟkūṟiyavataṉāṟ, piṟavum aṉpeṟuvaṉa koḷka. pūḻaṉi, yāḻaṉi eṉa ēṉiyavaṟṟōṭum oṭṭuka. mēl varukiṉṟa iṉcāriyiyic cēravittamiyāṉ, avi yellām iṉcāriyi peṟṟu varutaluṅ koḷka. ēḻiṉi, pūḻiṉi, yāḻiṉi eṉa varum.	(22)
195.		kuṟṟiya lukarat tiṟuti muṉṉar
					muṟṟat tōṉṟu miṉṉeṉ cāriyi.
itu, kuṟṟukara īṟṟiṟku muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: kuṟṟiyalukarattu iṟuti muṉṉar - kuṟṟiyalukara mākiya īṟṟiṉ muṉṉar, muṟṟattōṉṟum iṉṉeṉ cāriyi - muṭiyat tōṉṟum iṉṉeṉṉuñ cāriyi eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam:	nākiṉi nākiṉoṭu, varakiṉi varakiṉoṭu eṉa varum. ēṉiyavaṟṟoṭuñ ceyki yaṟintu oṭṭuka.
muṟṟa eṉṟataṉāṉē piṟa cāriyi peṟuvaṉavuṅ koḷka.  16vaḻakkattāṟ pāṭṭārāyntāṉ eṉavum, kariyataṉi eṉavum varum.			(23)
196.		neṭṭeḻut timpa roṟṟumikat tōṉṟu
					mappāṉ moḻika ḷalvaḻi yāṉa.
iḵtu akkuṟṟiya lukaraṅkaḷuṭ cilavaṟṟiṟku iṉavoṟṟu mikumeṉkiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: neṭṭeḻuttimpar oṟṟu mikat tōṉṟum - neṭ ṭeḻuttiṉ piṉṉar varukiṉṟa kuṟṟukaraṅkaṭku iṉavoṟṟu mikat tōṉṟā niṟkum; appāl moḻikaḷ alvaḻi āṉa - oṟṟu mikat tōṉṟāta kacatapakkaḷ īṟākiya moḻikaḷ allāta   iṭattu eṉṟavāṟu.
eṉavē, ṭakāra ṟakāraṅkaḷ īṟāṉa colliṭit tōṉṟumāyiṟṟu.
utāraṇam:	yāṭṭi yāṭṭoṭu yāṭṭukku yāṭṭiṉ yāṭṭatu yāṭṭukkaṇ eṉavum, yāṟṟi cōṟṟi eṉavum iṉa voṟṟu mikkaṉa. ivi appāl moḻikaḷ allaṉa. nāku, kācu, pōtu, kāpu eṉṟāṟ pōlvaṉa appāl moḻikaḷ; avi iṉavoṟṟu mikāvāyiṉa. 	(24)
197.		avitām
					iyaṟkiya vākuñ ceyaṟkiya veṉpa.
iḵtu eytiyatu vilakkiṟṟu.
itaṉ poruḷ: avitām iyaṟkiya ākuñ ceyaṟkiya eṉpa - aṅṅaṉam iṉavoṟṟu mikuvaṉatām iṉcāriyi peṟātu iyalpāka muṭiyuñ ceytiyiyuṭiya veṉṟu kūṟuvar āciriyar eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam: muṉṉark kāṭṭiyaṉavē koḷka.
ceyaṟkiya eṉṟa mikiyāṉē, uyirttoṭar moḻi kaḷil ēṟpaṉavaṟṟiṟkum oṟṟu mikattōṉṟutal koḷka. muyiṟṟi muyiṟṟoṭu muyiṟṟukku muyiṟṟiṉ muyiṟṟatu muyiṟṟukkaṇ eṉa varum. iṉṉum itaṉāṉē yāṭṭiṉi muyiṟṟiṉi eṉa vilakkiya iṉpeṟutaluṅ koḷka. (25)
198.	eṇṇi ṉiṟuti yaṉṉoṭu civaṇum.
itu, kuṟṟukara īṟṟu eṇṇuppeyar muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ:  eṇṇiṉ iṟuti aṉṉoṭu civaṇum - eṇṇup peyarkaḷiṉatu kuṟṟukara īṟu aṉcāriyiyōṭu poruntum eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam: oṉṟaṉi iraṇṭaṉi eṉa ellā eṇṇi ṉiyum, ellā urupiṉōṭuñ ceyki yaṟintu oṭṭuka. muṉṉarc ceyaṟkiya eṉṟa ilēcāṉē, oṉṟiṉi iraṇṭiṉi eṉa iṉ cāriyiyuṅ koṭukka.	(26)
199.		oṉṟumuta lākap pattūrntu varūu
					mellā veṇṇuñ colluṅ kāli
					yāṉiṭi variṉu māṉa milli
					yaḵteṉ kiḷavi yāvayiṟ keṭumē
					yuytal vēṇṭum paḵkāṉ meyyē.
iḵtu oṉṟu mutalāka eṭṭu iṟutiyāka niṉṟa kuṟṟukara īṟṟu eṇṇuppeyar ēḻiṉōṭum, patteṉṉum eṇṇup peyar vantu puṇarntu oṉṟāy niṉṟa coṟkaḷ cāriyi peṟṟut tiriyumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: oṉṟu mutalākap pattu ūrntu varūum ellā eṇṇum - oṉṟumutalāka eṭṭīṟāka niṉṟa eṇkaḷiṉ mēlē patteṉṉum eṇṇuppeyar ēṟi varukiṉṟa orupatu mutalāṉa ellā eṇkaḷiyum, colluṅkāli - muṭipu kūṟuṅ kālattu, āṉ iṭivariṉum māṉa milli - muṟkūṟiya aṉ cāriyiyē yaṉṟi āṉ cāriyi iṭiyē variṉuṅ kuṟṟamilli; āvayiṉ aḵteṉ kiḷavi keṭum - av āṉ peṟṟuḻip paḵteṉṉum eṇṇiṭattu aḵteṉṉuñ coṟ keṭṭup pōm; paḵkāṉ mey uytal vēṇṭum - avvakarattāṉ ūrappaṭṭa pakaramākiya oṟṟuk keṭātu niṟṟali āciriyaṉ virumpum eṉṟavāṟu.
niṉṟa patta ṉoṟṟukkeṭa vāytam, 
vantiṭi niliyum 	(eḻu. 437) 
eṉpataṉāṉ āytam peṟṟatu.
utāraṇam: orupaḵtu irupaḵtu muppaḵtu nāṟpaḵtu aimpaḵtu aṟupaḵtu eṇpaḵtu eṉak kuṟṟiyalukarap puṇariyaluḷ vitikkumāṟē niṟutti, aḵteṉpa taṉik keṭuttup pakaravoṟṟi niṟutti, āṉ cāriyi koṭuttu, orupāṉi irupāṉi eṉa ellā eṇṇoṭum ellā urupiṉiyuñ ceyki yaṟintu oṭṭuka. ummi etirmaṟiyātaliṉ oru paḵtaṉi irupaḵtaṉi eṉa ellāvaṟṟōṭum oṭṭuka.
colluṅ kāli eṉṟataṉāṉ, pattūr kiḷaviyē yaṉṟi 17oṉpāṉ mutaṉili (eḻu. 463) oṉpāṟ koṟṟiṭi mikumē (eḻu. 475) eṉṟāṟpōla varuvaṉavaṟṟiṉkaṇṇum pakarattuḷ akaram pirittu aḵteṉpatu keṭuttu āṉ koṭukka.	(27)
200.		yāte ṉiṟutiyuñ cuṭṭumuta lākiya
					vāyta viṟutiyu maṉṉoṭu civaṇu
					māytaṅ keṭuta lāvayi ṉāṉa.
iḵtu eṇṇuppeyarallāta kuṟṟukara īṟṟuṭ cilavaṟṟiṟku muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: yātu eṉ iṟutiyum - yāteṉa varuṅ kuṟṟukara īṟum, cuṭṭu mutalākiya āyta iṟutiyum - cuṭṭeḻuttiṉi mutalāka vuṭiya āytat toṭarmoḻik kuṟṟukara īṟum, aṉṉoṭu civaṇum - aṉcāriyi yoṭu poruntum; āvayiṉ āṉa āytaṅ keṭutal - avviṭattu vanta āytaṅ keṭum eṉṟavāṟu.
yātaṉi yātaṉoṭu eṉavum, ataṉi ataṉoṭu, itaṉi itaṉoṭu, utaṉi utaṉoṭu eṉavum varum.	(28)
201.		ēḻa ṉurupiṟkut ticippeyar muṉṉarc
					cāriyik kiḷavi yiyaṟkiyu māku
					māvayi ṉiṟuti meyyoṭuṅ keṭumē.
ituvuṅ kuṟṟukara īṟṟuṭ cilavaṟṟiṟku ēḻaṉurupoṭu muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: ticip peyar muṉṉar ēḻaṉurupiṟku - ticip peyarkaḷiṉ muṉṉar vanta kaṇṇeṉṉum urupiṟku muṭipu kūṟuṅkāl, cāriyik kiḷavi iyaṟkiyumākum - muṉkūṟiya iṉ cāriyi yākiya col niṉṟu muṭitalē yaṉṟi nillātu iyalpāyum muṭiyum; āvayiṉ iṟuti meyyoṭuṅ keṭum - aṅṅaṉam iyal pāyavaḻit ticippeyariṟutik kuṟṟukaran taṉṉāṉ ūrappaṭṭa meyyoṭuṅ keṭum eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam:	vaṭakkiṉkaṇ kiḻakkiṉkaṇ teṟkiṉkaṇ mēṟkiṉ kaṇ eṉavum, vaṭakkaṇ kiḻakkaṇ teṟkaṇ mēṟkaṇ eṉavum varum. iṉpeṟuvaḻi ukaraṅ keṭāteṉṟu uṇarka.
āvayi ṉeṉṟataṉāṟ kīḻcār kīḻpuṭi, mēlcār mēl puṭi, teṉcār teṉpuṭi, vaṭacār vaṭapuṭi eṉac cāriyi yiṉṟip pala vikārappaṭṭu niṟpaṉavuṅ koḷka. iṉṉum itaṉāṉē, kīḻikkuḷam mēlikkuḷam kīḻiccēri mēlic cēri eṉa aikāram peṟutaluṅ koḷka.	(29)
202.		puḷḷi yiṟutiyu muyiriṟu kiḷaviyuñ
					colliya valla vēṉiya vellān
					tēruṅ kāli yurupoṭu civaṇic
					cāriyi niliyuṅ kaṭappā ṭilavē.
iḵtu ivvōttiṉuḷ eṭuttōttāṉum ilēcāṉum muṭiyātu niṉṟavaṟṟiṟkellām, ituvē ōttāyatōr puṟaṉaṭi kūṟukiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: colliya alla puḷḷiyiṟutiyum uyiriṟu kiḷaviyum - muṟkūṟiya puḷḷiyīṟum uyirīṟum allāta puḷḷiyīṟṟuc collum uyirīṟṟuc collum, ēṉiyavu mellām - muṟkūṟiya īṟukaḷ tammu ḷoḻintu niṉṟaṉavu mellām, tēruṅ kāli urupoṭu civaṇic cāriyi niliyuṅ kaṭappāṭu ila - ārāyuṅ kālattu urupukaḷoṭu poruntic cāriyi niṉṟu muṭiyum muṟi miyi uṭiyavalla, niṉṟum nillātum muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.
ēṉiyavumeṉa ummi virikka. kūṟāta puḷḷi yīṟukaḷ aintu. avi ṇakara, yakara, rakara, lakara, ḷakaraṅkaḷām. maṇṇiṉi maṇṇi, vēyiṉi vēyi, nāriṉi nāri, kalliṉi kalli, muḷḷiṉi muḷḷi eṉa varum. uyirīṟṟuḷ oḻintatu 18ikaram oṉṟumēyātaliṉ, ataṉip piṟkūṟiṉār. kiḷiyiṉi kiḷiyi eṉa varum.
iṉit tāṉ yāṉ aḻaṉ puḻaṉ eṉṉum ṉakara īṟṟiṉum, ēḻeṉṉum ḻakara īṟṟiṉum oḻintaṉa, poṉṉiṉi poṉṉi, tāḻiṉi tāḻi eṉṟāṟpōla varuvaṉa piṟavumām. iṉi, īkāra īṟṟuḷ oḻintaṉa tīyiṉi tīyi, īyiṉi īyi, vīyiṉi vīyi eṉṟāṟpōlvaṉa piṟavumām. aikāra īṟṟuḷ oḻintaṉa, tiṉi yiṉi tiṉiyi, kaḻiyiṉi kaḻiyi eṉṟāṟ pōlvaṉa piṟavu mām. ēṉi īṟukaḷiṉum varuvaṉa uṇarntu koḷka.
19mēlē peyarīṟṟuc ceykiyellān tattam īṟṟiṉkaṇ muṭippārātaliṉ avi īṇṭuk kūṟal vēṇṭā.
iṉit tēruṅkāli eṉṟataṉāṉē, urupukaḷ nili moḻiyāka niṉṟu tamporuḷōṭu puṇarumvaḻi vēṟupaṭum urupīṟṟuc ceyki yellām īṇṭu muṭittuk koḷka.
utāraṇam: nampiyik koṇarntāṉ, maṇṇiṉik koṇarntāṉ, koṟṟaṉik koṇarntāṉ eṉa 20mūvakip poruḷōṭuṅ kūṭi niṉṟa urupiṟku oṟṟukkoṭukka. maliyoṭu porutatu, mattikiyāṟ puṭittāṉ, cāttaṟkuk koṭuttāṉ, ūrkkuc ceṉṟāṉ, kākkiyiṟ karitu, kākkiyatu pali, maṭiyuṭ paḻukkāy, taṭāviṉuṭ koṇṭāṉ eṉṉun toṭakkattaṉa urupu kāraṇamākap poruḷōṭu puṇarum vaḻi iyalpāyum īṟutirintum oṟṟumikkum vantaṉa koḷka.
iṉik, kaṇ kāl puṟam mutaliyaṉa peyarāyum urupāyum niṟkumātaliṉ, avi urupākak koḷḷumvaḻi vēṟupaṭuñ ceykikaḷellām ivvilēcāṉ muṭikka. iḵtu urupiyalātaliṉ urupoṭu civaṇi eṉa vēṇṭā, ammiki yāṉē urupupuṇarccikkaṭ ceṉṟa cāriyikaḷellām 21īṟṟup potu muṭipu uḷvaḻip poruṭpuṇarccikkuṅ koḷka. viḷaviṉkōṭu, kiḷiyiṉkāl eṉa ellā īṟṟiṉuṅ koḷka. nampiyi, koṟṟaṉi eṉa uyirīṟum puḷḷiyīṟuñ cāriyi peṟātu iyalpāy muṭivaṉavum īṇṭē koḷka.	(30)
urupiyal muṟṟiṟṟu.
kaṇēcayyar aṭikkuṟippukaḷ:
1.	ña na ma ya va eṉṉun tokimarapu 2-m cūttirattāl iyalpeṉṟati vilakkiṟṟeṉṟatu. urupukaḷ nilimoḻimuṉ iyalpākātu iṉpeṟu meṉṟati.
2. 	mēl eṉṟatu urupiyal 1-ñ cūttiratti.
3.	atiṉi atiṉoṭu eṉpaṉa marumuṭippuḻi muṭintaṉa eṉṟatu. `vaḻaṅkiyaṉ maruṅkiṉ maruvoṭu tirinavum' eṉṉum atikārap puṟaṉa ṭiyāṉ muṭintamiyi. itaṉāl ataṉ atiṉ eṉap piṟkālattu maruviyateṉpatu karuttuppōlum.
4.	yāveṉ viṉāviṉ aiyeṉiṟuti yāvi eṉpatu.
5.	eḻuttu eṉṟatu īkāra uyiri.
6.	uyirmey - nī.
7.	eḻuttuppēṟu eṉṟatu - ic cūttirattāṟ kūṟiya ṉakaratti.
8.	tev - paki; uriccol. tevvup pakiyākum eṉpatu uriyiyal 
18-ñ cūttiram.
9.	paṭuttalōciyāṟ peyarāyiṟṟu eṉṟatu pakivaṉi yuṇarttaliṉ.
10.	mēliccūttirameṉṟatu varuñcūttiratti.
11.	ēṉi muṟṟukaram eṉṟatu - oṭu, ku, itu eṉpavaṟṟi.
12.	nammiyum eṉṟa iṭattil ummil ukaraṅ keṭātu peṟṟu niṉṟatu. nammoṭum eṉṟa iṭattil ummil ukaraṅ keṭṭu makaram niṉṟatu eṉpatu karuttu.
13.	muṉṉar `mey' eṉṟatu  16-m cūttirattuḷ varum mey eṉpati. taṅkaṇ eṉpuḻi tam eṉṟa nilimoḻiyiṉmakarak kēṭṭukkuviti `mey' eṉṟatu.
14.	iccūttirattāl evaṉ eṉpataṟku vaṟṟuk koṭuttu, vaṟṟumici yoṟṟeṉṟu ṉakaraṅ keṭuttu `vaḵkāṉ meykeṭa' eṉṉuñ cūttirattu `ākiyapaṇpu' eṉṟamiyāl aikārattiṉ muṉṉaṉṟi, akarattiṉ muṉṉum vaṟṟiṉ vakaraṅ keṭumeṉa vakara akarattik keṭuttu evaṟṟi eṉa muṭikka. 
15.	eṟṟi eṉpatil nilimoḻi vakaraṅ keṭṭataṟku vitiyum `tōṉṟal' eṉpatu.
16.	vaḻakku, kaṟiyatu eṉpaṉa kuṟṟukaraṅkaḷ; attum aṉṉum peṟṟaṉa.
17.	`oṉpāṉ mutaṉili' eṉpatu. oṉpaḵtu āṉ peṟṟataṟku utāraṇam. ēṉiyavumaṉṉa.
18.	ikara vīṟṟiṟku tāraṇam kiḷiyiṉi. kiḷiyi eṉpaṉa.
19.	peyarīṟu eṉṟatu urupēṟkum peyarīṟṟi, nilimoḻiyāy niṉṟu urupēṟpaṉa peyarātaliṉ peyarīṟeṉṟār. peyarīṟākiya nili moḻi yīṟṟiṉ ceyki īṟukaṭōṟuṅ kūṟappaṭumeṉṟapaṭi.
20.	mūvakip poruḷ eṉṟatu mūvakip peyari. avi uyartiṇip peyar. aḵṟiṇip peyar. potuppeyar.
21.	poruḷ eṉṟatu - urupallāta varumoḻiyi. īṟṟup poruḷ - varumoḻip poruḷ. 

uyir mayaṅkiyal
uyirīṟu niṉṟu valleḻuttōṭum ciṟupāṉmi ēṉi yeḻuttukkaḷōṭum puṇarumāṟu kūṟuvatu uyir mayaṅkiya lākum. mayaṅkutal - kalattal. uyirum puḷḷiyum iṟutiyākiya uyartiṇip peyarkaḷ alvaḻi vēṟṟumiyākiya iru vaḻiyilum iyalpāmeṉavum, oru cila viṭaṅkaḷil aḵṟiṇi viravup peyar iyalpātalumuṇṭeṉavum toki marapil vitantu kūṟiya āciriyar, aviyoḻinta uyirīṟṟu aḵṟiṇip peyarkaḷiyum ēṉi viṉimuṟṟu viṉiyeccam peyarec caṅkaḷiyum akaravīṟumutal aukāravīṟumuṭiya neṭuṅkaṇakku muṟiyil vittu uṇarttukiṉṟār. ivviyalil 93-cūttiraṅkaḷ uḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟuṭ pala māṭṭēṟṟu muṟiyil amintaṉavākum. ivviyaliṟ kūṟappaṭṭa vitikaḷ yāvum valleḻuttu mikuvaṉa, iyalpāvaṉa, melleḻuttu mikuvaṉa, uyirmika varuvaṉa, nīṭavaruvaṉa kuṟukivaruvaṉa, cāriyipeṟuvaṉa, piṟavāṟu tirivaṉa eṉṉum ivvakiyuḷ aṭaṅkuvaṉavākum.
a, i, u. eṉṉum mūṉṟu cuṭṭiṉ muṉṉum uyirum yakaramumvariṉ vakaravoṟṟum kacatapañanamava eṉpaṉavariṉ vanta oṟṟeḻuttukkaḷum mikumeṉpatum ceyyuḷuḷ cuṭṭu nīṇṭu muṭiyumeṉpatum 3-6, 34, 35, 53-ām cūttiraṅkaḷiṟ collap paṭṭaṉa.
ā, ā, ī, u, ū, ē, ō, ōḷa eṉpavaṟṟi īṟākavuṭiya peyarkaḷ alvaḻi vēṟṟumiyākiya īriṭattum valleḻuttu mikappeṟumeṉṟum, ikara aikāra vīṟṟup peyarkaḷ vēṟṟumip poruṭpuṇarccikkaṇ valleḻuttu mikappeṟu meṉṟum 1, 14, 19, 22, 33, 47, 57, 62, 64, 72, 74, 78, 87, 90, 93-ām, cūttiraṅkaḷil āciriyar virittukkūṟiyuḷḷār.
alvaḻikkaṇ akaravīṟṟuḷ aṉṉaveṉṉum uvamavurupum, aṇmicuṭṭiya uyartiṇi viḷippeyarum, ceymmaṉa eṉṉum viṉiccollum, ēvalik karutiya viyaṅkōḷ viṉiyum, ceyta eṉṉum vāypāṭṭup peyareccamum, ceyyiya eṉṉum vāypāṭṭu viṉiyeccamum, amma eṉṉum uriyaciyum, akara vīṟṟup palavaṟicollum, ākāravīṟṟuḷ ā, mā eṉṉum peyarc collum, uyartiṇi viḷip peyarum, yā viṉāvum muṟṟuviṉiyum, miyā eṉṉum acic collum, taṉ toḻilik kuṟittuvarum ākāra viṉāvi yuṭiya viṉiccollum, īkāra vīṟṟuḷ nī, mī eṉpaṉavum pakaravīkāramum, ukaravīṟṟuc cuṭṭup peyarkaḷum, tēṟṟap poruḷil varum ekaravīṟum, ciṟappup poruḷil varum okaravīṟum, māṟukōḷeccap poruḷilum viṉāp poruṇmiyilum eṇṇupporuṇmiyilum aiyap poruṇmiyilum varum ēkāra ōkāra vīṟṟiṭic coṟkaḷum valleḻuttu mikātu iyalpāvaṉa.
akaravīṟṟu marappeyarum ā, i, u, ē, ai ākiya īṟukaḷil eṭuttōtiya piṭā, taḷā, mā (maram) ā, mā, (vilaṅku) uti, oṭu, cē, vici, ñemi, nami eṉṉum peyarkaḷum valleḻuttu mutaṉmoḻivariṉ melleḻuttu mikku muṭivaṉavām. yā, piṭā, taḷā, puḷi (cuvi) eṉpaṉa valleḻuttup peṟutalumuṇṭu.
ummittokikkaṇvarum ākāravīṟṟuppeyarum vēṟṟumikkaṇ kuṟiṟkīḻ niṉṟa ākāravīṟṟuppeyarum ōreḻuttoru moḻiyākiya ākāra vīṟṟuppeyarum akaram mikku muṭiyum. irā eṉṉuñ collukku akaramikutal illi. kuṟiṟkīḻ niṉṟa ūkāravīṟum ōreḻuttuttoru moḻiyākiya ūkāra vīṟum vēṟṟumikkaṇ ukaram mikkumuṭiyum. 
ē yeṉṉum peyar vēṟṟumikkaṇ ekaram mikku muṭiyum. ōkāravīṟu vēṟṟumik kaṇ okaram mikum. aukāravīṟṟup peyar iruvaḻiyilum ukarammikum.
ceyyuṭkaṇvarum akaraccuṭṭum, amma eṉṉum iṭic colliṟutiyum, pala cila eṉṉuñ coṟkaḷiṉ iṟuti akaramum, ḻakarameyyiyūrnta iṟuti ukaramum nīṇṭu muṭivaṉavām.
ā muṉvarum pakara vīkāram kuṟukum. kuṟiṟkīḻ niṉṟa iṟuti ākāram kuṟuki ukaram peṟum.
maka, āṭūu, makaṭūu cē eṉpaṉa iṉcāriyipeṟum. nilā attuccāriyipeṟum. paṉi (kālam), vaḷi (pūtam), maḻi eṉpaṉa attuccāriyiyum iṉcāriyiyum peṟuvaṉa. puḷi, eru, ceru, paṉi, ari, āviri eṉpaṉa amcāriyipeṟuvaṉa. ikara aikāra vīṟṟu nāṭ peyarmuṉ toḻiṟcolvariṉ iṭiyē āṉcāriyi varum. avviraṇṭīṟṟut tiṅkaṭ peyar muṉ toḻiṟcolvariṉ iṭiyē ikkuccāriyi varum. ūveṉṉum ōreḻuttorumoḻi ṉakaravoṟṟum akkuc cāriyiyum peṟum.
a, ī, u, ai, ō eṉṉum īṟukaḷukku urupiyaliṟ kūṟappaṭṭa cāriyikaḷi ivviyaliluḷḷa 18, 51, 61, 79, 92-ām cūttiraṅkaḷāl avvīṟṟup poruṭ puṇarccikkum māṭṭeṟintu viti kūṟuvar āciriyar.
cāva eṉpataṉ vakaravuyirmeyyum vāḻiya eṉpataṉ yakaravuyirmeyyum keṭṭu muṭiyum. nāḻi eṉpataṉmuṉ uri yeṉṉuñcol vantupuṇariṉ nilimoḻiyīṟṟiṉ ikaram tāṉ ēṟiya ḻakara meyyuṭaṉkeṭa avviṭattu ṭakaramey tōṉṟi muṭiyum. ceru eṉpataṉ muṉvarum amcāriyiyiṉ makaraṅkeṭa varumoḻi valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum. paṉi, āviri eṉpaṉa amcāriyi peṟuṅkāl iṟuti aikāraṅ keṭṭu muṭivaṉa. paṉi eṉṉuñcolmuṉ aṭṭu eṉpatu varumoḻiyāyvariṉ nili moḻiyiṟuti aikāraṅkeṭa avviṭattu ākāram tōṉṟum. pala cila eṉpavi tam muṉṉart tāmvariṉ iṟuti niṉṟa lakara vuyirmey lakaravoṟṟākat tiriyum. iṉṟiyeṉpataṉ iṟutiyikaram ceyyuḷuḷ ukaramākattiriyum. cuṭṭup peyarīṟṟu ukaram aṉṟu eṉpataṉōṭu puṇaruṅkāl ākāramākat tiritalum aiyeṉpataṉōṭu puṇaruṅkāl keṭutalum ceyyuḷiṭat tuṇ ṭākun tiripukaḷām. vēṭki yeṉṉuñcolmuṉ avā eṉpatu vantu puṇariṉ nilimoḻiyīṟṟiluḷḷa aikāram tāṉ ūrntu niṉṟa kakarameyyoṭuṅkeṭa aṅkuḷḷa ṭakaram ṇakara māyt tirital ceyyuṭkaṇvarun tiripākum.
-ka.veḷḷivāraṇaṉār 
			            nūlvarici 10, pak. 178-181

ēḻāvatu
uyirmayaṅkiyal
203.		akara viṟutip peyarnili muṉṉar
					vēṟṟumi yalvaḻik kacatapat tōṉṟiṟ
					ṟatta motta voṟṟiṭi mikumē
eṉpatu cūttiram. uyirīṟu niṉṟu vaṉkaṇattōṭuñ ciṟupāṉmi ēṉik kaṇaṅkaḷoṭum mayaṅkip puṇarum iyalpu uṇarttiṉamiyiṉ, ivvōttu uyirmayaṅkiya leṉṉum peyarttāyiṟṟu. mēṟ peyarōṭu urupu puṇarumāṟu kūṟip peyarvaruvaḻi urupu tokku niṉṟa poruṭ puṇarcci kūṟukiṉṟamiyiṉ urupiyalōṭu iyipuṭit tāyiṟṟu.
iccūttiram, akara īṟṟuppeyar alvaḻikkaṇ vaṉkaṇat tōṭu puṇarumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: akara iṟutip peyarnili muṉṉar - akaramākiya iṟutiyi yuṭiya peyarccoṉ muṉṉar, vēṟṟumi yalvaḻik kacatapat tōṉṟiṉ - vēṟṟumiyallāta viṭattuk kacatapa mutaṉ moḻikaḷ varumoḻiyāyt tōṉṟu māyiṉ, tattam otta oṟṟu iṭi mikum - tattamakkup poruntiya ak kacatapakkaḷākiya oṟṟu iṭikkaṇ mikum eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam: viḷakkuṟitu, nuṇakkuṟitu, atakkuṟitu, ciṟitu, tītu, peritu eṉa oṭṭuka. ivi aḵṟiṇi iyaṟpeya rākiya eḻuvāy viṉik kuṟippup paṇpākiya payaṉiliyōṭu muṭintaṉa.
otta veṉṟamiyātu 1`tatta motta eṉṟataṉāṉ, akara īṟṟu uriccol valleḻuttu mikkum, melleḻuttu mikkum muṭiyum muṭipum, akaran 2taṉṉi uṇara niṉṟavaḻi vaṉkaṇat tōṭu mikku muṭiyum muṭipum koḷka. taṭakki tavak koṇṭāṉ vayakkaḷiṟu (puṟam. 100) vayappuli (akam. 22) kuḻakkaṉṟu eṉavum, taṭañcevi kamañcūl (muruku. 7)  eṉavum, akkuṟitu ciṟitu tītu peritu eṉavum varum. iṉi, iṭiccol valloṟṟup peṟṟu varuvaṉa uḷavēl avaṟṟiyum ivvilēciṉāṉ muṭittuk koḷka.	(1)
204.		viṉiyeñcu kiḷaviyu muvamak kiḷaviyu
					meṉave ṉeccamuñ cuṭṭi ṉiṟutiyu
					māṅka veṉṉu muriyacik kiḷaviyu
					ñāṅkark kiḷanta valleḻuttu mikumē.
iḵtu akara īṟṟu viṉiccollum iṭiccollum puṇarumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: viṉiyeñcu kiḷaviyum - viṉiyi oḻipāka vuṭiya akara īṟṟu viṉiccollum, uvamak kiḷaviyum - uvama vurupāy niṉṟa akara īṟṟu iṭiccollum, eṉaveṉ eccamum - eṉaveṉṉum vāypāṭṭāṉ niṉṟa akara īṟṟu iṭic collum, cuṭṭiṉ iṟutiyum - cuṭṭākiya akara īṟṟu iṭic collum, āṅka eṉṉum uriyacik kiḷaviyum - āṅka veṉṉum akara īṟṟu uriyaci yiṭiccollum, ñāṅkark kiḷanta valleḻuttu mikum - muṉṉark kūṟiya valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam:	uṇa tāva cāva eṉa niṟuttik, koṇṭāṉ ceṉṟāṉ tantāṉ pōyiṉāṉ eṉa valleḻuttuk koṭuttu muṭikka. ivviṉiyeccam oḻintaṉa ellām iṭiccol leṉṟu uṇarka. pulipōlak koṉṟāṉ ceṉṟāṉ tāviṉāṉ pōyiṉāṉ eṉavum, koḷḷeṉak koṇṭāṉ ceṉṟāṉ tantāṉ pōyiṉāṉ eṉavum, akkoṟṟaṉ cāttaṉ tēvaṉ pūtaṉ eṉavum, āṅkak koṇṭāṉ ceṉṟāṉ tantāṉ pōyiṉāṉ, āṅkak kuyilu mayiluṅ kāṭṭik, kēcavaṉi viṭuttup pōki yōḷē eṉavum varum.  3uvamam viṉiyecca viṉikkuṟippēṉum, oṉṟaṉoṭu poruvappaṭutal nōkki uvamaviyaliṉkaṇ āciriyar vēṟupaṭuttik kūṟiṉār. eṉaveṉṉum eccamum, irucolliyum iyivikkiṉṟa nilimiyāṉ iṭiccollōttiṉuḷ vēṟōtiṉār. āṅka eṉpatu, ēḻaṉurupiṉ poruḷpaṭa vantatallāmi āṅka veṉṉu muriyaci eṉṟataṉāṉum āṅka vuriyaci (co. 279) eṉṉum iṭiyiyaṟ cūttirattāṉum uṇarka. ivi iyalpukaṇattukkaṇ muṭiyum muṭipu ñanamayava (eḻut. 144) eṉpuḻik kūṟiyatēyām. avi, tāva pulipōla koḷḷeṉa āṅka eṉa niṟutti, ña na ma ya va mutaliya moḻi ēṟpaṉa koṇarntu puṇarttu iyalpāmāṟu oṭṭik koḷka. cuṭṭu mēṟkūṟupa.	(2)
205.		cuṭṭiṉmuṉṉar ñanamat tōṉṟi
					ṉoṭṭiya voṟṟiṭi mikutal vēṇṭum.
itu, ñanamayava (eḻu. 144) eṉṉuñ cūttirattāṉ meṉ kaṇam iyalpākum eṉa muṟkūṟiyataṉi vilakki mikku muṭika eṉṟaliṉ,  eytiyatu vilakkip piṟituviti vakukkiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: cuṭṭiṉ muṉṉar ñanamat tōṉṟiṉ - akaraccuṭṭiṉ muṉṉar ña na ma kkaḷ mutalākiya moḻivariṉ, oṭṭiya oṟṟu iṭi mikutal vēṇṭum - tattamakkup poruntiṉa oṟṟu iṭimikutali virumpum āciriyaṉ eṉṟavāṟu.
aññāṇ, annūl, ammaṇi eṉa varum.
oṭṭiya veṉṟataṉāṉ aññeḷintatu, annaṉṟu, am māṇṭatu eṉa akaran taṉṉi yuṇara niṉṟavaḻiyum mikutal koḷka.		(3)
206.	yavamuṉ variṉē vakara moṟṟum.
ituvum atu.
itaṉ poruḷ: yava muṉ variṉ - yakara vakara mutaṉmoḻi akarac cuṭṭiṉ muṉṉē variṉ, vakaram oṟṟum - iṭikkaṇ vakaram oṟṟām eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam: avyāḻ, avvaḷi eṉa varum.
varumoḻi muṟkūṟiyavataṉāl, akaran taṉṉi yuṇara niṉṟa vaḻiyum vakaram mikutal koḷka. avvaḷintatu eṉa varum.	(4)
207.	uyirmuṉ variṉu māyiya ṟiriyātu.
ituvum atu.
itaṉ poruḷ: uyir muṉ variṉum āyiyal tiriyātu - uyirkaḷ akarac cuṭṭiṉ muṉ variṉum muṟkūṟiya vakaram mikku varum iyalpiṉ tiriyātu eṉṟavāṟu.
a eṉa niṉṟa cuṭṭiṉ muṉṉar aṭi eṉa varuvittu vakaram oṟṟit taṉṉuru iraṭṭi uyirēṟṟi, avvaṭi avvāṭi avvili avvīyam av vural avvūrti av veḻu avvēṇi avviyam avvoḻukkam avvōṭi avvuviyam eṉa oṭṭuka.
neṭiyataṉ muṉṉar (eḻu. 160) eṉpataṉuḷ neṟiyiyal eṉṟataṉāṉ iraṭṭutal kūṟiṉamiyiṉ, atu nilimoḻit toḻileṉpatu peṟappaṭṭatu. varumoḻi muṟkūṟiyavataṉāṉ, akaran taṉṉi uṇara niṉṟa vaḻiyum vakaram mikutal koḷka. avvaḻakitu eṉa varum. tiriyā teṉṟataṉāṉ, mēṟ cuṭṭu nīṇṭavaḻi vakarakkēṭu koḷka.	(5)
208.	nīṭa varutal ceyyuḷu ḷurittē.
iḵtu eytiyatu vilakkic ceyyuṭku āvatōr viti kūṟukiṉṟatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: nīṭa varutal ceyyuḷuḷ urittu - akaraccuṭṭu nīṭa varutal ceyyuḷiṭattu urittu eṉṟavāṟu.
utāraṇam:	āyiru tiṇiyi ṉicikkumaṉa collē (col. 1), āyirupāṟcol (col. 3) eṉa varum.
